




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

dīghanikāyau


sīlakkhandhavaggapāḷi


1. brahmajālasuttaṃ

paribbājakakathā

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| suppiyaupi khau paribbājakau antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti saddhiṃ antaivāsinā brahmadattaina māṇavaina| tatra sudaṃ suppiyau paribbājakau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; suppiyassa pana paribbājakassa antaivāsī brahmadattau māṇavau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa  vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| itiha tai ubhau ācariyantaivāsī añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā anubaddhā (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) haunti bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca|

2. atha khau bhagavā ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ rājāgārakai aikarattivāsaṃ upagacchi upagañchi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saddhiṃ  bhikkhusaṅghaina| suppiyaupi khau paribbājakau ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ rājāgārakai aikarattivāsaṃ upagacchi upagañchi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) antaivāsinā brahmadattaina māṇavaina| tatrapi sudaṃ suppiyau paribbājakau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa  avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; suppiyassa pana  paribbājakassa antaivāsī brahmadattau māṇavau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| itiha tai ubhau ācariyantaivāsī añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā viharanti|

3. atha khau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhitānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayaṃ saṅkhiyadhammau udapādi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatā suppaṭividitā| ayañhi suppiyau paribbājakau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; suppiyassa pana paribbājakassa antaivāsī brahmadattau māṇavau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| itihamai ubhau ācariyantaivāsī añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā  haunti bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’’ti|

4. atha khau bhagavā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ imaṃ saṅkhiyadhammaṃ viditvā yaina maṇḍalamāḷau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāyanuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhitānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayaṃ saṅkhiyadhammau udapādi – ‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatā suppaṭividitā| ayañhi suppiyau paribbājakau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa  avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; suppiyassa pana paribbājakassa antaivāsī brahmadattau māṇavau anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| itihamai ubhau ācariyantaivāsī añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa ujuvipac‍canīkavādā bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau  anubandhā haunti bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’ti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

5. ‘‘mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā avaṇṇaṃ  bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhaihi na āghātau na appac‍cayau na caitasau anabhiraddhi karaṇīyā| mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra cai tumhai assatha kupitā vā anattamanā vā, tumhaṃ yaivassa taina antarāyau| mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra cai tumhai assatha kupitā vā anattamanā vā, api nu tumhai paraisaṃ subhāsitaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ ājānaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhaihi abhūtaṃ abhūtatau nibbaiṭhaitabbaṃ – ‘itipaitaṃ abhūtaṃ, itipaitaṃ atacchaṃ, natthi caitaṃ amhaisu, na ca panaitaṃ amhaisu saṃvij‍jatī’ti|

6. ‘‘mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhaihi na ānandau na saumanassaṃ na caitasau uppilāvitattaṃ karaṇīyaṃ| mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra cai tumhai assatha ānandinau sumanā uppilāvitā tumhaṃ yaivassa taina antarāyau| mamaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, parai vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, dhammassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, saṅghassa vā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhaihi bhūtaṃ bhūtatau paṭijānitabbaṃ – ‘itipaitaṃ bhūtaṃ, itipaitaṃ tacchaṃ, atthi caitaṃ amhaisu, saṃvij‍jati ca panaitaṃ amhaisū’ti|

cūḷasīlaṃ

7. ‘‘appamattakaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, auramattakaṃ sīlamattakaṃ, yaina puthuj‍janau  tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya| katamañ‍ca taṃ, bhikkhavai, appamattakaṃ auramattakaṃ sīlamattakaṃ, yaina puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya?

8. ‘‘‘pāṇātipātaṃ  pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau nihitadaṇḍau, nihitasatthau, laj‍jī, dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharatī’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

‘‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharatī’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

‘‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī samaṇau gautamau ārācārī anācārī (ka॰) viratau paṭiviratau (katthaci) maithunā gāmadhammā’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

9. ‘‘‘musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau ṭhaitau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassā’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

‘‘‘pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī  samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

‘‘‘pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau, yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

‘‘‘samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina sāpadaisaṃ  pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhita’nti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

10. ‘bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā  samārabbhā (sī॰ ka॰) paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘‘aikabhattikau samaṇau gautamau rattūparatau viratau paṭiviratau (katthaci) vikālabhaujanā…|

nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā nac‍cagītavāditavisukadassanā (ka॰) paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā  paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā sāviyaugā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau…|

chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

cūḷasīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

majjhimasīlaṃ

11. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ saiyyathīdaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) – mūlabījaṃ khandhabījaṃ phaḷubījaṃ aggabījaṃ bījabījamaiva pañ‍camaṃ pañ‍camaṃ iti vā (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰); iti aivarūpā bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

12. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ san‍nidhikāraparibhaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – an‍nasan‍nidhiṃ pānasan‍nidhiṃ vatthasan‍nidhiṃ yānasan‍nidhiṃ sayanasan‍nidhiṃ gandhasan‍nidhiṃ āmisasan‍nidhiṃ iti vā iti aivarūpā san‍nidhikāraparibhaugā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

13. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vāditaṃ paikkhaṃ akkhānaṃ pāṇissaraṃ vaitāḷaṃ kumbhathūṇaṃ kumbhathūnaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), kumbhathūṇaṃ (sī॰) saubhanakaṃ saubhanagharakaṃ (sī॰), saubhanagarakaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) caṇḍālaṃ vaṃsaṃ dhauvanaṃ hatthiyuddhaṃ assayuddhaṃ mahiṃsayuddhaṃ mahisayuddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) usabhayuddhaṃ ajayuddhaṃ maiṇḍayuddhaṃ kuk‍kuṭayuddhaṃ vaṭṭakayuddhaṃ daṇḍayuddhaṃ muṭṭhiyuddhaṃ nibbuddhaṃ uyyaudhikaṃ balaggaṃ sainābyūhaṃ anīkadassanaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpā visūkadassanā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

14. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ jūtappamādaṭṭhānānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhapadaṃ dasapadaṃ ākāsaṃ parihārapathaṃ santikaṃ khalikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ salākahatthaṃ akkhaṃ paṅgacīraṃ vaṅkakaṃ maukkhacikaṃ ciṅgulikaṃ  ciṅgulakaṃ (ka॰ sī॰) pattāḷhakaṃ rathakaṃ dhanukaṃ  akkharikaṃ manaisikaṃ yathāvaj‍jaṃ iti  vā iti aivarūpā jūtappamādaṭṭhānānuyaugā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

15. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai  aivarūpaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandiṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ gaunakaṃ cittakaṃ paṭikaṃ paṭalikaṃ tūlikaṃ vikatikaṃ uddalaumiṃ aikantalaumiṃ kaṭṭissaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kuttakaṃ hatthattharaṃ assattharaṃ rathattharaṃ hatthattharaṇaṃ assattharaṇaṃ rathattharaṇaṃ (sī॰ ka॰ pī॰) ajinappavaiṇiṃ kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ sauttaracchadaṃ ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpā uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

16. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ maṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – ucchādanaṃ parimaddanaṃ nhāpanaṃ sambāhanaṃ ādāsaṃ añ‍janaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ mālāvilaipanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mukhacuṇṇaṃ mukhalaipanaṃ hatthabandhaṃ sikhābandhaṃ daṇḍaṃ nāḷikaṃ asiṃ khaggaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), asiṃ khaggaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) chattaṃ citrupāhanaṃ uṇhīsaṃ maṇiṃ vālabījaniṃ audātāni vatthāni dīghadasāni iti vā iti aivarūpā maṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānānuyaugā  paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

17. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sūrakathaṃ  visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānakathāya paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

18. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ viggāhikakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi, kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi, micchā paṭipan‍nau tvamasi, ahamasmi sammā paṭipan‍nau, sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai, puraivacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchāvacanīyaṃ purai avaca, adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattaṃ, āraupitau tai vādau, niggahitau tvamasi, cara vādappamaukkhāya, nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausīti iti vā iti aivarūpāya viggāhikakathāya  paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

19. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – rañ‍ñaṃ, rājamahāmattānaṃ, khattiyānaṃ, brāhmaṇānaṃ, gahapatikānaṃ, kumārānaṃ ‘‘idha gaccha, amutrāgaccha, idaṃ hara, amutra idaṃ āharā’’ti iti vā iti aivarūpā dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

20. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai kuhakā ca haunti, lapakā ca naimittikā ca nippaisikā ca, lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīṃsitārau ca lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigiṃ bhitārau (sī॰ syā॰), lābhaina ca lābhaṃ nijigīsitārau (pī॰) iti iti vā, iti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) aivarūpā kuhanalapanā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

majjhimasīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

mahāsīlaṃ

21. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṅgaṃ nimittaṃ uppātaṃ supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ mūsikacchin‍naṃ aggihaumaṃ dabbihaumaṃ thusahaumaṃ kaṇahaumaṃ taṇḍulahaumaṃ sappihaumaṃ tailahaumaṃ mukhahaumaṃ lauhitahaumaṃ aṅgavij‍jā vatthuvij‍jā  khattavij‍jā khaittavij‍jā (bahūsu) sivavij‍jā bhūtavij‍jā bhūrivij‍jā ahivij‍jā visavij‍jā vicchikavij‍jā mūsikavij‍jā sakuṇavij‍jā vāyasavij‍jā pak‍kajjhānaṃ saraparittāṇaṃ migacak‍kaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

22. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – maṇilakkhaṇaṃ vatthalakkhaṇaṃ daṇḍalakkhaṇaṃ satthalakkhaṇaṃ asilakkhaṇaṃ usulakkhaṇaṃ dhanulakkhaṇaṃ āvudhalakkhaṇaṃ itthilakkhaṇaṃ purisalakkhaṇaṃ kumāralakkhaṇaṃ kumārilakkhaṇaṃ dāsalakkhaṇaṃ dāsilakkhaṇaṃ hatthilakkhaṇaṃ  assalakkhaṇaṃ mahiṃsalakkhaṇaṃ mahisalakkhaṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) usabhalakkhaṇaṃ gaulakkhaṇaṃ ajalakkhaṇaṃ maiṇḍalakkhaṇaṃ kuk‍kuṭalakkhaṇaṃ vaṭṭakalakkhaṇaṃ gaudhālakkhaṇaṃ kaṇṇikālakkhaṇaṃ kacchapalakkhaṇaṃ migalakkhaṇaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

23. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – rañ‍ñaṃ niyyānaṃ bhavissati, rañ‍ñaṃ  aniyyānaṃ bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ upayānaṃ bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ  apayānaṃ bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ upayānaṃ bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ apayānaṃ bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ jayau bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ parājayau bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ jayau bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ parājayau bhavissati, iti imassa jayau bhavissati, imassa parājayau bhavissati iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

24. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – candaggāhau bhavissati, sūriyaggāhau suriyaggāhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavissati, nakkhattaggāhau bhavissati, candimasūriyānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, candimasūriyānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, nakkhattānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, nakkhattānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, uk‍kāpātau bhavissati, disāḍāhau bhavissati, bhūmicālau bhavissati, daivadudrabhi daivadundubhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavissati, candimasūriyanakkhattānaṃ uggamanaṃ augamanaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau candaggāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau sūriyaggāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau nakkhattaggāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ  uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ nakkhattānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ nakkhattānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau uk‍kāpātau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau disāḍāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau bhūmicālau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau daivadudrabhi bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ candimasūriyanakkhattānaṃ uggamanaṃ augamanaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ bhavissati iti vā  iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

25. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – suvuṭṭhikā bhavissati, dubbuṭṭhikā bhavissati, subhikkhaṃ bhavissati, dubbhikkhaṃ bhavissati, khaimaṃ bhavissati, bhayaṃ bhavissati, raugau bhavissati, āraugyaṃ bhavissati, muddā, gaṇanā, saṅkhānaṃ, kāvaiyyaṃ, laukāyataṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

26. ‘‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – āvāhanaṃ vivāhanaṃ saṃvaraṇaṃ vivaraṇaṃ saṃkiraṇaṃ  vikiraṇaṃ subhagakaraṇaṃ dubbhagakaraṇaṃ viruddhagabbhakaraṇaṃ jivhānibandhanaṃ hanusaṃhananaṃ hatthābhijappanaṃ hanujappanaṃ kaṇṇajappanaṃ ādāsapañhaṃ kumārikapañhaṃ daivapañhaṃ ādic‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ mahatupaṭṭhānaṃ abbhuj‍jalanaṃ sirivhāyanaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

27. ‘‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – santikammaṃ paṇidhikammaṃ bhūtakammaṃ bhūrikammaṃ vassakammaṃ vaussakammaṃ vatthukammaṃ vatthuparikammaṃ ācamanaṃ nhāpanaṃ juhanaṃ vamanaṃ viraicanaṃ uddhaṃviraicanaṃ adhauviraicanaṃ sīsaviraicanaṃ kaṇṇatailaṃ naittatappanaṃ natthukammaṃ añ‍janaṃ pac‍cañ‍janaṃ sālākiyaṃ sal‍lakattiyaṃ dārakatikicchā mūlabhaisaj‍jānaṃ anuppadānaṃ ausadhīnaṃ paṭimaukkhau iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau samaṇau gautamau’ti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

‘‘idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, appamattakaṃ auramattakaṃ sīlamattakaṃ, yaina puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa vaṇṇaṃ vadamānau vadaiyya|

mahāsīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pubbantakappikā

28. ‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaiva dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ| katamai ca tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ?

29. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau, pubbantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni adhivuttipadāni (sī॰ pī॰) abhivadanti aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni  adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi?

sassatavādau

30. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā, sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi?

31. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai ( ) (parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpakkilaisai) (syā॰ ka॰) anaikavihitaṃ  pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikānipi jātisatāni anaikānipi jātisahassāni anaikānipi jātisatasahassāni – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ  anussarati|

‘‘sau  aivamāha – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau; tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisamaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya  anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusāmi, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi  jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikānipi jātisatāni anaikānipi jātisahassāni anaikānipi jātisatasahassāni – amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| imināmahaṃ aitaṃ jānāmi ‘‘yathā sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau; tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

32. ‘‘dutiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ dvaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni tīṇipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni cattāripi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni pañ‍capi  saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni dasapi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī  aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘sau  aivamāha – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau; tai ca  sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisamaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusāmi yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭaṃ dvaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni tīṇipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni cattāripi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni pañ‍capi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni dasapi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni| amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| imināmahaṃ aitaṃ jānāmi ‘‘yathā sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau, tai ca  sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

33. ‘‘tatiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya  tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ – dasapi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni vīsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni tiṃsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni cattālīsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘sau  aivamāha – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau  ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau; tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisamaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusāmi, yathāsamāhitai cittai anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| saiyyathidaṃ – dasapi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni vīsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni tiṃsampi  saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni cattālīsampi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭāni – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| imināmahaṃ aitaṃ jānāmi ‘‘yathā sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau, tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

34. ‘‘catutthai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā tak‍kī hauti vīmaṃsī, sau tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃ paṭibhānaṃ aivamāha – ‘sassatau attā ca laukau ca vañjhau kūṭaṭṭhau aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitau; tai ca sattā sandhāvanti saṃsaranti cavanti upapaj‍janti, atthitvaiva sassatisama’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

35. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina; natthi itau bahiddhā|

36. ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau pajānāti – ‘imai diṭṭhiṭṭhānā aivaṃgahitā aivaṃparāmaṭṭhā aivaṃgatikā bhavanti aivaṃabhisamparāyā’ti, tañ‍ca tathāgatau pajānāti, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāti; tañ‍ca pajānanaṃ pajānaṃ (?) dī॰ ni॰ 3.36 pāḷiaṭṭhakathā passitabbaṃ na  parāmasati, aparāmasatau cassa pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā| vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau|

37. ‘‘imai khau tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā  nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

aikac‍casassatavādau

38. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca  pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi?

39. ‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina ayaṃ laukau saṃvaṭṭati| saṃvaṭṭamānai laukai yaibhuyyaina sattā ābhassarasaṃvattanikā haunti| tai tattha haunti manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau, ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti|

40. ‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau, yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina ayaṃ laukau vivaṭṭati| vivaṭṭamānai laukai suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ pātubhavati| atha khau añ‍ñatarau sattau āyukkhayā  vā puñ‍ñakkhayā vā ābhassarakāyā cavitvā suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jati| sau  tattha hauti manaumayau pītibhakkhau sayaṃpabhau antalikkhacarau subhaṭṭhāyī, ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhati|

41. ‘‘tassa tattha aikakassa dīgharattaṃ nivusitattā anabhirati paritassanā upapaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata añ‍ñaipi sattā itthattaṃ āgacchaiyyu’nti| atha añ‍ñaipi sattā āyukkhayā vā puñ‍ñakkhayā vā ābhassarakāyā cavitvā brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍janti tassa sattassa sahabyataṃ| taipi tattha haunti manaumayā pītibhakkhā sayaṃpabhā antalikkhacarā subhaṭṭhāyinau, ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhanti|

42. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yau sau sattau paṭhamaṃ upapan‍nau tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahamasmi brahmā mahābrahmā  abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā saj‍jitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ| mayā imai sattā nimmitā| taṃ kissa haitu? mamañhi pubbai aitadahausi – ‘‘ahau vata añ‍ñaipi sattā itthattaṃ āgacchaiyyu’’nti| iti mama ca manaupaṇidhi, imai ca sattā itthattaṃ āgatā’ti|

‘‘yaipi tai sattā pacchā upapan‍nā, taisampi aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ| iminā mayaṃ bhautā brahmunā nimmitā| taṃ kissa haitu? imañhi mayaṃ addasāma idha paṭhamaṃ upapan‍naṃ, mayaṃ panamha pacchā upapan‍nā’ti|

43. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yau sau sattau paṭhamaṃ upapan‍nau, sau dīghāyukatarau ca hauti vaṇṇavantatarau ca mahaisakkhatarau ca| yai pana tai sattā pacchā upapan‍nā, tai appāyukatarā ca haunti dubbaṇṇatarā ca appaisakkhatarā ca|

44. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau  ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘‘sau aivamāha – ‘yau khau sau bhavaṃ brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ, yaina mayaṃ bhautā brahmunā nimmitā, sau nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| yai pana mayaṃ ahumhā taina bhautā brahmunā  nimmitā, tai mayaṃ anic‍cā addhuvā appāyukā cavanadhammā itthattaṃ āgatā’ti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

45. ‘‘dutiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā  aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? santi, bhikkhavai, khiḍḍāpadausikā nāma daivā, tai ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā hasakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā (ka॰) viharanti| taisaṃ ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ sati sammussati pamussati (sī॰ syā॰)| satiyā sammausā tai daivā tamhā kāyā cavanti|

46. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya  anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘‘sau aivamāha – ‘yai khau tai bhauntau daivā na khiḍḍāpadausikā, tai na ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā viharanti| taisaṃ na ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ sati na sammussati| satiyā  asammausā tai daivā tamhā kāyā na cavanti; nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassanti| yai pana mayaṃ ahumhā khiḍḍāpadausikā, tai mayaṃ ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nā viharimhā| taisaṃ nau ativailaṃ hassakhiḍḍāratidhammasamāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ sati sammussati| satiyā sammausā aivaṃ mayaṃ tamhā kāyā cutā anic‍cā addhuvā appāyukā cavanadhammā itthattaṃ āgatā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

47. ‘‘tatiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? santi, bhikkhavai, manaupadausikā nāma  daivā, tai ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyanti| tai ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyantā añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi cittāni padūsainti| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paduṭṭhacittā kilantakāyā kilantacittā| tai daivā tamhā kāyā cavanti|

48. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya  sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai taṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, tatau paraṃ nānussarati|

‘‘sau aivamāha – ‘yai khau tai bhauntau daivā na manaupadausikā, tai nātivailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyanti| tai nātivailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyantā añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi cittāni nappadūsainti| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ appaduṭṭhacittā akilantakāyā akilantacittā| tai daivā tamhā kāyā na cavanti, nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva  ṭhassanti| yai pana mayaṃ ahumhā manaupadausikā, tai mayaṃ ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyimhā| tai mayaṃ ativailaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ upanijjhāyantā añ‍ñamañ‍ñamhi cittāni padūsimhā, tai mayaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paduṭṭhacittā kilantakāyā kilantacittā| aivaṃ mayaṃ tamhā kāyā cutā anic‍cā addhuvā appāyukā cavanadhammā itthattaṃ āgatā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

49. ‘‘catutthai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā tak‍kī hauti vīmaṃsī| sau tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃpaṭibhānaṃ aivamāha – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ vuc‍cati cakkhuṃ itipi sautaṃ itipi ghānaṃ itipi jivhā itipi kāyau itipi, ayaṃ attā anic‍cau addhuvau asassatau vipariṇāmadhammau| yañ‍ca khau idaṃ vuc‍cati cittanti vā manauti vā viñ‍ñāṇanti vā ayaṃ attā nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

50. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi  vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina; natthi itau bahiddhā|

51. ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau pajānāti – ‘imai diṭṭhiṭṭhānā  aivaṃgahitā aivaṃparāmaṭṭhā aivaṃgatikā bhavanti aivaṃabhisamparāyā’ti| tañ‍ca tathāgatau pajānāti, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāti, tañ‍ca pajānanaṃ na parāmasati, aparāmasatau cassa pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā| vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau|

52. ‘‘imai khau tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

antānantavādau

53. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi?

54. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai  cittai antasañ‍ñī laukasmiṃ viharati|

‘‘sau aivamāha – ‘antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusāmi, yathāsamāhitai cittai antasañ‍ñī laukasmiṃ viharāmi| imināmahaṃ aitaṃ jānāmi – yathā antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti|

55. ‘‘dutiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ  phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai anantasañ‍ñī laukasmiṃ viharati|

‘‘sau  aivamāha – ‘anantau ayaṃ laukau apariyantau| yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau’’ti, taisaṃ musā| anantau ayaṃ laukau apariyantau| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusāmi, yathāsamāhitai cittai anantasañ‍ñī laukasmiṃ viharāmi| imināmahaṃ aitaṃ jānāmi – yathā anantau ayaṃ laukau apariyantau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ  ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti|

56. ‘‘tatiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai uddhamadhau antasañ‍ñī laukasmiṃ viharati, tiriyaṃ anantasañ‍ñī|

‘‘sau aivamāha – ‘antavā ca ayaṃ laukau anantau ca| yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau’’ti, taisaṃ musā| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘anantau ayaṃ laukau apariyantau’’ti, taisampi musā| antavā ca ayaṃ laukau anantau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusāmi, yathāsamāhitai cittai uddhamadhau antasañ‍ñī laukasmiṃ viharāmi, tiriyaṃ anantasañ‍ñī| imināmahaṃ aitaṃ jānāmi – yathā antavā ca ayaṃ laukau anantau cā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti|

57. ‘‘catutthai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā tak‍kī hauti vīmaṃsī| sau tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃpaṭibhānaṃ aivamāha – ‘naivāyaṃ laukau antavā, na panānantau| yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘antavā ayaṃ laukau parivaṭumau’’ti, taisaṃ musā| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā  aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘anantau ayaṃ laukau apariyantau’’ti, taisampi musā| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘antavā ca ayaṃ laukau anantau cā’’ti, taisampi musā| naivāyaṃ laukau antavā, na panānantau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti|

58. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina; natthi itau bahiddhā|

59. ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau pajānāti – ‘imai diṭṭhiṭṭhānā aivaṃgahitā aivaṃparāmaṭṭhā aivaṃgatikā bhavanti aivaṃabhisamparāyā’ti| tañ‍ca tathāgatau pajānāti, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāti, tañ‍ca pajānanaṃ na parāmasati, aparāmasatau cassa pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā| vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau|

60. ‘‘imai khau tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā gambhīrā duddasā  duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

amarāvikkhaipavādau

61. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā, tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ catūhi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ catūhi vatthūhi?

62. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ‘idaṃ kusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘idaṃ akusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’’nti  yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| ahañ‍cai khau pana ‘‘idaṃ  kusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau, ‘idaṃ kusala’nti vā byākaraiyyaṃ, ‘idaṃ akusala’nti vā byākaraiyyaṃ, taṃ mamassa musā| yaṃ mamassa musā, sau mamassa vighātau| yau mamassa vighātau sau mamassa antarāyau’ti| iti sau musāvādabhayā musāvādaparijaigucchā naividaṃ kusalanti byākarauti, na panidaṃ akusalanti byākarauti, tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍jati amarāvikkhaipaṃ – ‘aivantipi mai nau; tathātipi mai nau; añ‍ñathātipi mai nau; nautipi mai  nau; nau nautipi mai nau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ|

63. ‘‘dutiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ‘idaṃ kusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘idaṃ akusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| ahañ‍cai khau pana ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau, ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti vā byākaraiyyaṃ, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’nti vā byākaraiyyaṃ, tattha mai assa chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā paṭighau vā| yattha yau (?) mai assa chandau vā rāgau vā dausau vā paṭighau vā, taṃ mamassa upādānaṃ| yaṃ mamassa upādānaṃ, sau mamassa vighātau| yau mamassa vighātau, sau mamassa antarāyau’ti| iti  sau upādānabhayā upādānaparijaigucchā naividaṃ kusalanti byākarauti, na panidaṃ akusalanti byākarauti, tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍jati amarāvikkhaipaṃ – ‘aivantipi mai nau; tathātipi mai nau; añ‍ñathātipi  mai nau; nautipi mai nau; nau nautipi mai nau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā  tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ|

64. ‘‘tatiyai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā ‘idaṃ kusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘idaṃ akusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāmi| ahañ‍cai khau pana ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau ‘‘idaṃ akusala’’nti yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau ‘‘idaṃ kusala’’nti vā byākaraiyyaṃ, ‘‘idaṃ akusala’’nti vā byākaraiyyaṃ| santi hi khau samaṇabrāhmaṇā paṇḍitā nipuṇā  kataparappavādā vālavaidhirūpā, tai bhindantā vaubhindantā (sī॰ pī॰) mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni, tai maṃ tattha samanuyuñ‍jaiyyuṃ samanugāhaiyyuṃ samanubhāsaiyyuṃ| yai maṃ tattha samanuyuñ‍jaiyyuṃ samanugāhaiyyuṃ samanubhāsaiyyuṃ, taisāhaṃ na sampāyaiyyaṃ| yaisāhaṃ na sampāyaiyyaṃ, sau mamassa vighātau| yau mamassa vighātau, sau mamassa  antarāyau’ti| iti sau anuyaugabhayā anuyaugaparijaigucchā naividaṃ kusalanti byākarauti, na panidaṃ akusalanti byākarauti, tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍jati amarāvikkhaipaṃ – ‘aivantipi mai nau; tathātipi mai nau; añ‍ñathātipi mai nau; nautipi mai nau; nau nautipi mai nau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha  aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ|

65. ‘‘catutthai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā mandau hauti maumūhau| sau mandattā maumūhattā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍jati amarāvikkhaipaṃ – ‘atthi parau laukau’ti iti cai maṃ pucchasi, ‘atthi parau laukau’ti iti cai mai  assa, ‘atthi parau laukau’ti iti tai naṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, ‘aivantipi mai nau, tathātipi mai nau, añ‍ñathātipi mai nau, nautipi mai nau, nau nautipi mai nau’ti| ‘natthi parau laukau…pai॰… ‘atthi ca natthi ca parau laukau…pai॰… ‘naivatthi na natthi parau laukau…pai॰… ‘atthi sattā aupapātikā  …pai॰… ‘natthi sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… ‘atthi ca natthi ca sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… ‘naivatthi na natthi sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… ‘atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ sukaṭaduk‍kaṭānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰… ‘natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰… ‘atthi ca natthi ca sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰… ‘naivatthi na natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰… ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā…pai॰… ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā…pai॰… ‘hauti ca na ca hauti na hauti ca (sī॰ ka॰) tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti iti cai maṃ pucchasi, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti cai mai assa, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti iti tai naṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, ‘aivantipi mai nau, tathātipi mai nau, añ‍ñathātipi mai nau, nautipi mai nau, nau nautipi mai nau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ|

66. ‘‘imaihi  khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā  tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ catūhi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina, natthi itau bahiddhā…pai॰… yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

adhic‍casamuppan‍navādau

67. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti dvīhi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā  kimāgamma kimārabbha adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti dvīhi vatthūhi?

68. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, asañ‍ñasattā nāma daivā| sañ‍ñuppādā ca pana tai daivā tamhā kāyā cavanti| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatarau sattau tamhā kāyā cavitvā itthattaṃ āgacchati| itthattaṃ āgatau samānau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau ātappamanvāya padhānamanvāya anuyaugamanvāya appamādamanvāya sammāmanasikāramanvāya tathārūpaṃ caitausamādhiṃ phusati, yathāsamāhitai cittai sañ‍ñuppādaṃ anussarati, tatau paraṃ  nānussarati| sau aivamāha – ‘adhic‍casamuppan‍nau attā ca laukau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? ahañhi pubbai nāhausiṃ, saumhi aitarahi ahutvā santatāya pariṇatau’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca  laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

69. ‘‘dutiyai  ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā tak‍kī hauti vīmaṃsī| sau tak‍kapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃpaṭibhānaṃ aivamāha – ‘adhic‍casamuppan‍nau attā ca laukau cā’ti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yaṃ āgamma yaṃ ārabbha aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti|

70. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti dvīhi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva dvīhi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina, natthi itau bahiddhā…pai॰… yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

71. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti aṭṭhārasahi  vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantamārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina, natthi itau  bahiddhā|

72. ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau pajānāti – ‘imai diṭṭhiṭṭhānā aivaṃgahitā aivaṃparāmaṭṭhā aivaṃgatikā bhavanti aivaṃabhisamparāyā’ti| tañ‍ca tathāgatau pajānāti, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāti, tañ‍ca pajānanaṃ na parāmasati, aparāmasatau cassa pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti viditā| vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau|

73. ‘‘imai  khau tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

dutiyabhāṇavārau|

aparantakappikā

74. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau, aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti catucattārīsāya catucattālīsāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti catucattārīsāya vatthūhi?

sañ‍ñīvādau

75. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā  sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sauḷasahi vatthūhi| tai  ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha uddhamāghātanikā sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sauḷasahi  vatthūhi?

76. ‘‘‘rūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā sañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti| ‘arūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā sañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti| ‘rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti…pai॰… naivarūpī nārūpī attā hauti… antavā attā hauti… anantavā attā hauti… antavā ca anantavā ca attā hauti… naivantavā nānantavā attā hauti… aikattasañ‍ñī attā hauti… nānattasañ‍ñī attā hauti… parittasañ‍ñī attā hauti… appamāṇasañ‍ñī attā hauti… aikantasukhī attā hauti… aikantadukkhī attā hauti| sukhadukkhī attā hauti| adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā sañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

77. ‘‘imaihi  khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sauḷasahi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā uddhamāghātanikā sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva sauḷasahi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina, natthi itau bahiddhā…pai॰… yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

asañ‍ñīvādau

78. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha uddhamāghātanikā  asañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi?

79. ‘‘‘rūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā asañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti| ‘arūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā asañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti| ‘rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti…pai॰… naivarūpī nārūpī attā hauti… antavā attā hauti… anantavā attā hauti… antavā ca anantavā ca attā hauti… naivantavā nānantavā attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā asañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

80. ‘‘imaihi  khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina, natthi itau bahiddhā…pai॰…  yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādau

81. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā, uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi?

82. ‘‘‘rūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti ‘arūpī  attā hauti…pai॰… rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti… naivarūpī nārūpī attā hauti… antavā attā hauti… anantavā attā hauti… antavā ca anantavā ca attā hauti… naivantavā nānantavā attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī’ti naṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

83. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva aṭṭhahi vatthūhi…pai॰…  yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

ucchaidavādau

84. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sattahi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau  samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sattahi vatthūhi?

85. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhī (ka॰ pī॰) – ‘yatau khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

86. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi  khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā sammā samucchin‍nau hauti| atthi khau, bhau, añ‍ñau attā dibbau rūpī kāmāvacarau kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhau| taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi| tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| sau khau, bhau, attā yatau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

87. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā sammā samucchin‍nau hauti| atthi khau, bhau, añ‍ñau attā dibbau rūpī manaumayau sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgī ahīnindriyau| taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi| tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| sau khau, bhau, attā yatau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

88. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā  sammā samucchin‍nau hauti| atthi khau, bhau, añ‍ñau attā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ  amanasikārā ‘‘anantau ākāsau’’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagau| taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi  na passasi| tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| sau khau, bhau, attā yatau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

89. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā sammā samucchin‍nau hauti| atthi khau, bhau, añ‍ñau attā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagau| taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi| tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| sau khau, bhau, attā yatau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

90. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, sau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā sammā samucchin‍nau hauti| atthi khau, bhau, añ‍ñau attā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘‘natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagau| taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi| tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| sau khau, bhau, attā yatau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

91. ‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā sammā samucchin‍nau hauti| atthi khau, bhau, añ‍ñau attā  sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘‘santamaitaṃ paṇītamaita’’nti naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagau| taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi| tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| sau khau, bhau, attā yatau kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sammā samucchin‍nau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

92. ‘‘imaihi  khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sattahi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva sattahi vatthūhi…pai॰… yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādau

93. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍cahi vatthūhi| tai ca bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kimāgamma kimārabbha diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā  satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍cahi vatthūhi?

94. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘‘yatau khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

95. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha –‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? kāmā hi, bhau, anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, taisaṃ vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| yatau khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ  attā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

96. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau  aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? yadaiva tattha vitak‍kitaṃ vicāritaṃ, aitainaitaṃ auḷārikaṃ akkhāyati| yatau khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

97. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? yadaiva tattha pītigataṃ caitasau uppilāvitattaṃ, aitainaitaṃ auḷārikaṃ akkhāyati| yatau khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati, satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittāvatā khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

98. ‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘atthi khau, bhau, aisau attā, yaṃ tvaṃ vadaisi, naisau natthīti vadāmi; nau ca khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā aittāvatā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hauti| taṃ kissa haitu? yadaiva tattha sukhamiti caitasau ābhaugau, aitainaitaṃ auḷārikaṃ akkhāyati| yatau khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ  upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, aittāvatā  khau, bhau, ayaṃ attā paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pattau hautī’ti| itthaikai satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

99. ‘‘imaihi  khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍cahi vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, sabbai tai imaihaiva pañ‍cahi vatthūhi…pai॰…  yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

100. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti catucattārīsāya vatthūhi| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva catucattārīsāya vatthūhi…pai॰…  yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

101. ‘‘imaihi khau tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā ca aparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi|

102. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā pubbantakappikā vā aparantakappikā vā pubbantāparantakappikā vā pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni  abhivadanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina; natthi itau bahiddhā|

103. ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau pajānāti – ‘imai diṭṭhiṭṭhānā aivaṃgahitā aivaṃparāmaṭṭhā aivaṃgatikā bhavanti aivaṃabhisamparāyā’ti| tañ‍ca tathāgatau pajānāti, tatau ca uttaritaraṃ pajānāti, tañ‍ca pajānanaṃ na parāmasati, aparāmasatau cassa pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva nibbuti  viditā| vaidanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau|

104. ‘‘imai  khau tai, bhikkhavai, dhammā gambhīrā duddasā duranubaudhā santā paṇītā atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇā paṇḍitavaidanīyā, yai tathāgatau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti, yaihi tathāgatassa yathābhuc‍caṃ vaṇṇaṃ sammā vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ|

paritassitavipphanditavārau

105. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

106. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

107. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

108. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

109. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti dvīhi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

110. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantaṃ  ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti aṭṭhārasahi  vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

111. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sauḷasahi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

112. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

113. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

114. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sattahi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

115. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍cahi vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

116. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti catucattārīsāya vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

117. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā ca aparantakappikā ca  pubbantāparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi, tadapi taisaṃ bhavataṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ajānataṃ apassataṃ vaidayitaṃ taṇhāgatānaṃ paritassitavipphanditamaiva|

phassapac‍cayāvārau

118. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā  sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

119. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

120. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

121. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ catūhi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

122. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti dvīhi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

123. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti  aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

124. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sauḷasahi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

125. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

126. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

127. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sattahi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

128. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍cahi vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

129. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā  aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti catucattārīsāya vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

130. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā ca aparantakappikā  ca pubbantāparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jativārau

131. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

132. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍ca asassatikā aikac‍caṃ sassataṃ aikac‍caṃ asassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

133. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā antānantaṃ laukassa pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

134. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā tattha tattha pañhaṃ puṭṭhā samānā vācāvikkhaipaṃ āpaj‍janti amarāvikkhaipaṃ catūhi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

135. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti dvīhi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

136. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā pubbantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

137. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā  sañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ sañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sauḷasahi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

138. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā, uddhamāghātanaṃ asañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

139. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā uddhamāghātanaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñiṃ attānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti aṭṭhahi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

140. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ucchaidavādā satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñapainti sattahi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

141. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā satau sattassa paramadiṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍cahi vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

142. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā aparantānudiṭṭhinau aparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti catucattārīsāya vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

143. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā ca aparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

diṭṭhigatikādhiṭṭhānavaṭṭakathā

144. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sassatavādā sassataṃ attānañ‍ca laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapainti catūhi vatthūhi, yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aikac‍casassatikā aikac‍caasassatikā…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā antānantikā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā amarāvikkhaipikā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā  adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā sañ‍ñīvādā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā asañ‍ñīvādā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhamāghātanikā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā ucchaidavādā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparantakappikā… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pubbantakappikā ca aparantakappikā  ca pubbantāparantakappikā ca pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi, sabbai tai chahi phassāyatanaihi phussa phussa paṭisaṃvaidainti taisaṃ vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti|

vivaṭṭakathādi

145. ‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ imaihi sabbaihaiva uttaritaraṃ pajānāti|

146. ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā pubbantakappikā vā aparantakappikā vā pubbantāparantakappikā vā pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi antaujālīkatā, aittha sitāva ummuj‍jamānā ummuj‍janti, aittha pariyāpan‍nā antaujālīkatāva ummuj‍jamānā ummuj‍janti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau kaivaṭṭau vā kaivaṭṭantaivāsī vā sukhumacchikaina jālaina parittaṃ udakadahaṃ udakarahadaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) auttharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘yai khau kaici imasmiṃ udakadahai auḷārikā pāṇā, sabbai tai antaujālīkatā| aittha sitāva ummuj‍jamānā  ummuj‍janti; aittha pariyāpan‍nā antaujālīkatāva  ummuj‍jamānā ummuj‍jantī’ti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā pubbantakappikā vā aparantakappikā vā pubbantāparantakappikā vā pubbantāparantānudiṭṭhinau pubbantāparantaṃ ārabbha anaikavihitāni adhimuttipadāni abhivadanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva dvāsaṭṭhiyā vatthūhi antaujālīkatā aittha sitāva ummuj‍jamānā ummuj‍janti, aittha pariyāpan‍nā antaujālīkatāva ummuj‍jamānā ummuj‍janti|

147. ‘‘ucchin‍nabhavanaittikau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa kāyau tiṭṭhati| yāvassa kāyau ṭhassati, tāva naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā| kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā na naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ambapiṇḍiyā vaṇṭacchin‍nāya yāni kānici ambāni vaṇṭapaṭibandhāni vaṇṭūpanibandhanāni (sī॰ pī॰), vaṇḍapaṭibaddhāni (ka॰), sabbāni tāni tadanvayāni bhavanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ucchin‍nabhavanaittikau tathāgatassa kāyau tiṭṭhati, yāvassa kāyau ṭhassati, tāva naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā, kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā na naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā’’ti|

148. aivaṃ  vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai, kau nāmau ayaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, ānanda, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ atthajālantipi naṃ dhāraihi, dhammajālantipi naṃ dhāraihi, brahmajālantipi naṃ dhāraihi, diṭṭhijālantipi naṃ dhāraihi, anuttarau  saṅgāmavijayautipi naṃ dhāraihī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā|

149. attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai dasasahassī sahassī (katthaci) laukadhātu akampitthāti|

brahmajālasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|



2. sāmañ‍ñaphalasuttaṃ

rājāmac‍cakathā

150. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa ambavanai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi| taina khau pana samayaina rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tadahupausathai pan‍narasai kaumudiyā cātumāsiniyā puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā rājāmac‍caparivutau uparipāsādavaragatau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tadahupausathai udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘ramaṇīyā vata bhau dausinā ratti, abhirūpā vata bhau dausinā ratti, dassanīyā vata bhau dausinā ratti, pāsādikā vata bhau dausinā ratti, lakkhañ‍ñā vata bhau dausinā ratti| kaṃ nu khvaj‍ja samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā payirupāsaiyyāma, yaṃ nau payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti?

151. aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau rājāmac‍cau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ ajātasattuṃ vaidaihiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, pūraṇau kassapau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| taṃ daivau pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

152. añ‍ñataraupi khau rājāmac‍cau  rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ ajātasattuṃ vaidaihiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, makkhali  gausālau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| taṃ daivau makkhaliṃ gausālaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa makkhaliṃ gausālaṃ payirupāsatau  cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

153. añ‍ñataraupi  khau rājāmac‍cau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ ajātasattuṃ vaidaihiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, ajitau kaisakambalau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| taṃ daivau ajitaṃ kaisakambalaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa ajitaṃ kaisakambalaṃ payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhī ahausi|
