154. añ‍ñataraupi khau rājāmac‍cau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ ajātasattuṃ vaidaihiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, pakudhau pakuddhau (sī॰) kac‍cāyanau saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| taṃ daivau pakudhaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa pakudhaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

155. añ‍ñataraupi khau rājāmac‍cau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ ajātasattuṃ  vaidaihiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, sañ‍cayau sañ‍jayau (sī॰ syā॰) bailaṭṭhaputtau bail‍laṭṭhiputtau (sī॰), vailaṭṭhaputtau (syā॰) saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| taṃ daivau sañ‍cayaṃ bailaṭṭhaputtaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa sañ‍cayaṃ bailaṭṭhaputtaṃ payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

156. añ‍ñataraupi khau rājāmac‍cau rājānaṃ māgadhaṃ ajātasattuṃ vaidaihiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, nigaṇṭhau  nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰), nātaputtau (pī॰) saṅghī caiva gaṇī ca gaṇācariyau ca ñātau yasassī titthakarau sādhusammatau bahujanassa rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| taṃ daivau nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa nigaṇṭhaṃ  nāṭaputtaṃ payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

kaumārabhac‍cajīvakakathā

157. taina  khau pana samayaina jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa avidūrai tuṇhībhūtau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tvaṃ pana, samma jīvaka, kiṃ tuṇhī’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau amhākaṃ ambavanai viharati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi| taṃ khau pana bhagavantaṃ bhagavantaṃ gautamaṃ (sī॰ ka॰ pī॰) aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau  abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| taṃ daivau bhagavantaṃ payirupāsatu| appaiva nāma daivassa bhagavantaṃ payirupāsatau cittaṃ pasīdaiyyā’ti|

158. ‘‘taina hi, samma jīvaka, hatthiyānāni kappāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, daivā’’ti khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa paṭissuṇitvā pañ‍camattāni hatthinikāsatāni kappāpaitvā rañ‍ñau ca ārauhaṇīyaṃ nāgaṃ, rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘‘kappitāni khau tai, daiva, hatthiyānāni, yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

159. atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau pañ‍casu hatthinikāsataisu pac‍caikā itthiyau āraupaitvā ārauhaṇīyaṃ nāgaṃ abhiruhitvā uk‍kāsu dhāriyamānāsu rājagahamhā niyyāsi mahac‍carājānubhāvaina, yaina jīvakassa kaumārabhac‍cassa ambavanaṃ taina pāyāsi|

atha khau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa avidūrai ambavanassa ahudaiva bhayaṃ, ahu chambhitattaṃ, ahu laumahaṃsau| atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu  vaidaihiputtau bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau  jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci maṃ, samma jīvaka, na vañ‍caisi? kac‍ci maṃ, samma jīvaka, na palambhaisi? kac‍ci maṃ, samma jīvaka, na pac‍catthikānaṃ daisi? kathañhi nāma tāva mahatau bhikkhusaṅghassa aḍḍhataiḷasānaṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ naiva khipitasaddau bhavissati, na uk‍kāsitasaddau na nigghausau’’ti|

‘‘mā bhāyi, mahārāja, mā bhāyi, mahārāja| na taṃ daiva, vañ‍caimi; na taṃ, daiva, palambhāmi; na taṃ, daiva, pac‍catthikānaṃ daimi| abhik‍kama, mahārāja, abhik‍kama, mahārāja, aitai maṇḍalamāḷai dīpā padīpā (sī॰ syā॰) jhāyantī’’ti|

sāmañ‍ñaphalapucchā

160. atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau yāvatikā nāgassa bhūmi nāgaina gantvā, nāgā pac‍caurauhitvā, pattikauva padikauva (syā॰) yaina maṇḍalamāḷassa dvāraṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ pana, samma jīvaka, bhagavā’’ti? ‘‘aisau, mahārāja, bhagavā; aisau, mahārāja, bhagavā majjhimaṃ thambhaṃ nissāya puratthābhimukhau nisin‍nau purakkhatau bhikkhusaṅghassā’’ti|

161. atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā rahadamiva vippasan‍naṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘iminā mai upasamaina udayabhaddau udāyibhaddau (sī॰ pī॰) kumārau saman‍nāgatau hautu, yainaitarahi upasamaina bhikkhusaṅghau saman‍nāgatau’’ti| ‘‘agamā khau tvaṃ, mahārāja, yathāpaima’’nti| ‘‘piyau mai, bhantai, udayabhaddau kumārau| iminā mai, bhantai, upasamaina udayabhaddau kumārau saman‍nāgatau hautu yainaitarahi upasamaina bhikkhusaṅghau saman‍nāgatau’’ti|

162. atha  khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā, bhikkhusaṅghassa añ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā, aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pucchaiyyāmahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva daisaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva daisaṃ laisamattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰); sacai mai bhagavā aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘puccha, mahārāja, yadākaṅkhasī’’ti|

163. ‘‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhantai, puthusippāyatanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – hatthārauhā assārauhā rathikā dhanuggahā cailakā calakā piṇḍadāyakā uggā rājaputtā pakkhandinau mahānāgā sūrā cammayaudhinau dāsikaputtā āḷārikā kappakā nhāpakā nahāpikā (sī॰), nhāpikā (syā॰) sūdā mālākārā rajakā paisakārā naḷakārā kumbhakārā gaṇakā muddikā, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi aivaṃgatāni puthusippāyatanāni, tai diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sippaphalaṃ upajīvanti; tai taina attānaṃ sukhainti  pīṇainti pīnainti (katthaci), mātāpitarau sukhainti pīṇainti, puttadāraṃ sukhainti pīṇainti, mittāmac‍cai sukhainti pīṇainti, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu samaṇaisu brāhmaṇaisu (ka॰) uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhapainti sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| sak‍kā nu khau, bhantai, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’’nti?

164. ‘‘abhijānāsi nau tvaṃ, mahārāja, imaṃ pañhaṃ añ‍ñai samaṇabrāhmaṇai pucchitā’’ti? ‘‘abhijānāmahaṃ, bhantai, imaṃ pañhaṃ añ‍ñai samaṇabrāhmaṇai pucchitā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tai, mahārāja, byākariṃsu, sacai tai agaru bhāsassū’’ti| ‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, garu, yatthassa bhagavā nisin‍nau, bhagavantarūpau vā’’ti cāti (sī॰ ka॰)| ‘‘taina  hi, mahārāja, bhāsassū’’ti|

pūraṇakassapavādau

165. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ yaina pūraṇau kassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pūraṇaina kassapaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhau kassapa, puthusippāyatanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – hatthārauhā assārauhā rathikā dhanuggahā  cailakā calakā piṇḍadāyakā uggā rājaputtā pakkhandinau mahānāgā sūrā cammayaudhinau dāsikaputtā āḷārikā kappakā nhāpakā sūdā mālākārā rajakā paisakārā naḷakārā kumbhakārā gaṇakā muddikā, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi aivaṃgatāni puthusippāyatanāni- tai diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sippaphalaṃ upajīvanti; tai taina attānaṃ sukhainti pīṇainti, mātāpitarau sukhainti pīṇainti, puttadāraṃ sukhainti pīṇainti, mittāmac‍cai sukhainti pīṇainti, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhapainti sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| sak‍kā nu khau, bhau kassapa, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ  pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

166. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, pūraṇau kassapau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘karautau khau, mahārāja, kārayatau, chindatau chaidāpayatau, pacatau pācāpayatau saucayatau, saucāpayatau, kilamatau kilamāpayatau, phandatau phandāpayatau, pāṇamatipātāpayatau, adin‍naṃ ādiyatau, sandhiṃ chindatau, nil‍laupaṃ haratau, aikāgārikaṃ karautau, paripanthai tiṭṭhatau, paradāraṃ gacchatau, musā bhaṇatau, karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikaṃ maṃsakhalaṃ aikaṃ maṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇaṃ caipi  gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya hanantau ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttarañ‍caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya dadantau dāpaintau yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina  damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| itthaṃ khau mai, bhantai, pūraṇau kassapau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau akiriyaṃ byākāsi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ambaṃ vā puṭṭhau labujaṃ byākaraiyya, labujaṃ vā puṭṭhau ambaṃ byākaraiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, pūraṇau kassapau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau akiriyaṃ byākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pūraṇassa kassapassa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭikausitvā  anattamanau, anattamanavācaṃ anicchāraitvā, tamaiva vācaṃ anuggaṇhantau anik‍kuj‍jantau anik‍kuj‍jaintau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ pak‍kāmiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

makkhaligausālavādau

167. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ yaina makkhali gausālau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā makkhalinā gausālaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, makkhaliṃ gausālaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhau gausāla, puthusippāyatanāni…pai॰… sak‍kā nu khau, bhau gausāla, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

168. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, makkhali gausālau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘natthi mahārāja haitu natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya, ahaitū ahaitu (katthaci) apac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā, ahaitū apac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| natthi attakārai, natthi parakārai, natthi purisakārai, natthi balaṃ, natthi vīriyaṃ, natthi purisathāmau, natthi purisaparak‍kamau| sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṅgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ sukhañ‍ca dukkhañ‍ca (syā॰) paṭisaṃvaidainti| cuddasa  khau panimāni yaunipamukhasatasahassāni saṭṭhi ca satāni cha ca satāni pañ‍ca ca kammunau satāni pañ‍ca ca kammāni tīṇi ca kammāni kammai ca aḍḍhakammai ca dvaṭṭhipaṭipadā dvaṭṭhantarakappā  chaḷābhijātiyau aṭṭha purisabhūmiyau aikūnapañ‍ñāsa ājīvakasatai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa paribbājakasatai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa nāgāvāsasatai vīsai indriyasatai tiṃsai nirayasatai chattiṃsa rajaudhātuyau satta sañ‍ñīgabbhā satta asañ‍ñīgabbhā satta nigaṇṭhigabbhā satta daivā satta mānusā satta pisācā satta sarā satta pavuṭā sapuṭā (ka॰), pabuṭā (sī॰) satta pavuṭasatāni satta papātā satta papātasatāni satta supinā satta supinasatāni cul‍lāsīti mahākappinau mahākappunau (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) satasahassāni, yāni bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| tattha natthi  ‘‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā aparipak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ paripācaissāmi, paripak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ phussa phussa byantiṃ karissāmī’ti haivaṃ natthi| dauṇamitai sukhadukkhai pariyantakatai saṃsārai, natthi hāyanavaḍḍhanai, natthi uk‍kaṃsāvakaṃsai| saiyyathāpi nāma suttaguḷai khittai nibbaiṭhiyamānamaiva palaiti, aivamaiva bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissantī’ti|

169. ‘‘itthaṃ khau mai, bhantai, makkhali gausālau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau saṃsārasuddhiṃ byākāsi| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ambaṃ vā puṭṭhau labujaṃ byākaraiyya, labujaṃ vā puṭṭhau ambaṃ byākaraiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, makkhali gausālau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau saṃsārasuddhiṃ byākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, makkhalissa gausālassa  bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā anattamanau, anattamanavācaṃ anicchāraitvā, tamaiva vācaṃ anuggaṇhantau anik‍kuj‍jantau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ|

ajitakaisakambalavādau

170. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ yaina ajitau kaisakambalau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā ajitaina kaisakambalaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, ajitaṃ kaisakambalaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhau ajita, puthusippāyatanāni…pai॰… sak‍kā nu khau, bhau ajita, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

171. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhantai, ajitau kaisakambalau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘natthi, mahārāja, din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau paralaukau (syā॰), natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā samaggatā (ka॰), samaggatā (syā॰) sammāpaṭipan‍nā, yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidainti| cātumahābhūtikau ayaṃ purisau, yadā kālaṅkarauti, pathavī pathavikāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, āpau āpaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, taijau taijaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, vāyau vāyaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, ākāsaṃ indriyāni saṅkamanti| āsandipañ‍camā purisā mataṃ ādāya gacchanti| yāvāḷāhanā padāni pañ‍ñāyanti| kāpautakāni aṭṭhīni bhavanti, bhassantā āhutiyau| dattupañ‍ñattaṃ yadidaṃ dānaṃ| taisaṃ tucchaṃ musā vilāpau yai kaici atthikavādaṃ vadanti| bālai ca paṇḍitai ca kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti, na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’ti|

172. ‘‘itthaṃ khau mai, bhantai, ajitau kaisakambalau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau ucchaidaṃ byākāsi| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ambaṃ vā puṭṭhau labujaṃ byākaraiyya, labujaṃ vā puṭṭhau ambaṃ byākaraiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, ajitau kaisakambalau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau ucchaidaṃ byākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, ajitassa kaisakambalassa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā anattamanau anattamanavācaṃ anicchāraitvā tamaiva vācaṃ anuggaṇhantau anik‍kuj‍jantau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ|

pakudhakac‍cāyanavādau

173. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ yaina pakudhau kac‍cāyanau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā pakudhaina kac‍cāyanaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pakudhaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhau kac‍cāyana, puthusippāyatanāni…pai॰… sak‍kā nu khau, bhau kac‍cāyana, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

174. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, pakudhau kac‍cāyanau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘sattimai, mahārāja, kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā animmitā animmātā vañjhā kūṭaṭṭhā  aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā| tai na iñ‍janti, na vipariṇamanti, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti, nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| katamai satta? pathavikāyau, āpaukāyau, taijaukāyau, vāyaukāyau, sukhai, dukkhai, jīvai sattamai  – imai satta kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā animmitā animmātā vañjhā kūṭaṭṭhā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā| tai na iñ‍janti, na vipariṇamanti, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti, nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| tattha natthi hantā vā ghātaitā vā, sautā vā sāvaitā vā, viñ‍ñātā vā viñ‍ñāpaitā vā| yaupi tiṇhaina satthaina sīsaṃ chindati, na kauci kiñ‍ci kañ‍ci (kaṃ॰) jīvitā vauraupaiti; sattan‍naṃ tvaiva sattan‍naṃ yaiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kāyānamantaraina satthaṃ vivaramanupatatī’ti|

175. ‘‘itthaṃ  khau mai, bhantai, pakudhau kac‍cāyanau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau añ‍ñaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ambaṃ vā puṭṭhau labujaṃ byākaraiyya, labujaṃ vā puṭṭhau ambaṃ byākaraiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, pakudhau kac‍cāyanau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau añ‍ñaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pakudhassa kac‍cāyanassa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ, anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā anattamanau, anattamanavācaṃ anicchāraitvā tamaiva vācaṃ anuggaṇhantau anik‍kuj‍jantau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ|

nigaṇṭhanāṭaputtavādau

176. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaina nāṭaputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhau aggivaissana, puthusippāyatanāni…pai॰… sak‍kā nu khau, bhau aggivaissana, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

177. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idha, mahārāja, nigaṇṭhau cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti| kathañ‍ca, mahārāja, nigaṇṭhau cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti? idha, mahārāja, nigaṇṭhau sabbavārivāritau ca hauti, sabbavāriyuttau ca, sabbavāridhutau ca, sabbavāriphuṭau ca| aivaṃ khau, mahārāja, nigaṇṭhau cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti| yatau khau, mahārāja, nigaṇṭhau aivaṃ cātuyāmasaṃvarasaṃvutau hauti; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mahārāja, nigaṇṭhau nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau (syā॰ ka॰) gatattau ca yatattau ca ṭhitattau cā’ti|

178. ‘‘itthaṃ  khau mai, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau cātuyāmasaṃvaraṃ byākāsi| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ambaṃ vā puṭṭhau labujaṃ byākaraiyya, labujaṃ vā puṭṭhau ambaṃ byākaraiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau cātuyāmasaṃvaraṃ byākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, nigaṇṭhassa nāṭaputtassa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā anattamanau anattamanavācaṃ anicchāraitvā tamaiva vācaṃ anuggaṇhantau anik‍kuj‍jantau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ|

sañ‍cayabailaṭṭhaputtavādau

179. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ yaina sañ‍cayau bailaṭṭhaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā sañ‍cayaina bailaṭṭhaputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau ahaṃ bhantai, sañ‍cayaṃ bailaṭṭhaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhau sañ‍caya, puthusippāyatanāni…pai॰… sak‍kā nu khau, bhau sañ‍caya, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

180. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sañ‍cayau bailaṭṭhaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘atthi parau laukauti iti cai maṃ pucchasi, atthi parau laukauti iti cai mai assa, atthi parau laukauti iti tai naṃ byākaraiyyaṃ| aivantipi mai nau, tathātipi mai nau, añ‍ñathātipi mai nau, nautipi mai  nau, nau nautipi mai nau| natthi parau laukau…pai॰… atthi ca natthi ca parau laukau…pai॰… naivatthi na natthi parau laukau…pai॰… atthi sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… natthi sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… atthi ca natthi ca sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… naivatthi na natthi sattā aupapātikā…pai॰… atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰…  natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰…atthi ca natthi ca sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰… naivatthi na natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau…pai॰… hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇā…pai॰… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā…pai॰… hauti  ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti iti cai maṃ pucchasi, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti iti cai mai assa, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti iti tai naṃ byākaraiyyaṃ| aivantipi mai nau, tathātipi mai nau, añ‍ñathātipi mai nau, nautipi mai nau, nau nautipi mai nau’ti|

181. ‘‘itthaṃ khau mai, bhantai, sañ‍cayau bailaṭṭhaputtau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vikkhaipaṃ byākāsi| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ambaṃ vā puṭṭhau labujaṃ byākaraiyya, labujaṃ vā puṭṭhau ambaṃ byākaraiyya; aivamaiva khau mai, bhantai, sañ‍cayau bailaṭṭhaputtau sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vikkhaipaṃ byākāsi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ayañ‍ca imaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sabbabālau sabbamūḷhau| kathañhi nāma sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ puṭṭhau samānau vikkhaipaṃ byākarissatī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘kathañhi nāma mādisau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā vijitai vasantaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, sañ‍cayassa bailaṭṭhaputtassa bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā anattamanau anattamanavācaṃ  anicchāraitvā tamaiva vācaṃ anuggaṇhantau anik‍kuj‍jantau uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ|

paṭhamasandiṭṭhikasāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ

182. ‘‘sauhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantampi pucchāmi – ‘yathā nu khau imāni, bhantai, puthusippāyatanāni saiyyathidaṃ – hatthārauhā assārauhā rathikā dhanuggahā  cailakā calakā piṇḍadāyakā uggā rājaputtā pakkhandinau mahānāgā sūrā cammayaudhinau dāsikaputtā āḷārikā kappakā nhāpakā sūdā mālākārā rajakā paisakārā naḷakārā kumbhakārā gaṇakā muddikā, yāni vā panañ‍ñānipi aivaṃgatāni puthusippāyatanāni, tai diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sippaphalaṃ upajīvanti, tai taina attānaṃ sukhainti pīṇainti, mātāpitarau sukhainti pīṇainti, puttadāraṃ sukhainti pīṇainti, mittāmac‍cai sukhainti pīṇainti, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhapainti sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| sak‍kā nu khau mai, bhantai, aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’nti?

183. ‘‘sak‍kā, mahārāja| taina hi, mahārāja, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai  khamaiyya, tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, idha tai assa purisau dāsau kammakārau kammakarau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pubbuṭṭhāyī pacchānipātī kiṅkārapaṭissāvī manāpacārī piyavādī mukhul‍laukakau mukhul‍laukikau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| tassa aivamassa – ‘acchariyaṃ, vata bhau, abbhutaṃ, vata bhau, puñ‍ñānaṃ gati, puñ‍ñānaṃ vipākau| ayañhi rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau manussau; ahampi manussau| ayañhi rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti, daivau mañ‍ñai| ahaṃ panamhissa dāsau kammakārau pubbuṭṭhāyī pacchānipātī kiṅkārapaṭissāvī manāpacārī piyavādī mukhul‍laukakau| sau vatassāhaṃ puñ‍ñāni karaiyyaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau kāyaina saṃvutau viharaiyya, vācāya saṃvutau viharaiyya, manasā saṃvutau viharaiyya, ghāsacchādanaparamatāya santuṭṭhau, abhiratau pavivaikai| taṃ cai tai purisā aivamāraucaiyyuṃ – ‘yagghai daiva jānaiyyāsi, yau tai sau purisau yau tai purisau (sī॰ ka॰) dāsau kammakārau pubbuṭṭhāyī pacchānipātī kiṅkārapaṭissāvī manāpacārī piyavādī mukhul‍laukakau; sau, daiva, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau  samānau kāyaina saṃvutau viharati, vācāya saṃvutau viharati, manasā saṃvutau viharati, ghāsacchādanaparamatāya santuṭṭhau, abhiratau pavivaikai’ti| api nu tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘aitu mai, bhau, sau purisau, punadaiva hautu dāsau kammakārau pubbuṭṭhāyī pacchānipātī kiṅkārapaṭissāvī manāpacārī piyavādī mukhul‍laukakau’ti?

184. ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| atha  khau naṃ mayamaiva abhivādaiyyāmapi, pac‍cuṭṭhaiyyāmapi, āsanainapi nimantaiyyāma, abhinimantaiyyāmapi naṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi, dhammikampissa rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahaiyyāmā’’ti|

185. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, yadi aivaṃ santai hauti vā sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ nau vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai hauti sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphala’’nti| ‘‘idaṃ khau tai, mahārāja, mayā paṭhamaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti|

dutiyasandiṭṭhikasāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ

186. ‘‘sak‍kā  pana, bhantai, añ‍ñampi aivamaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaitu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, mahārāja| taina hi, mahārāja, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya, tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja, idha tai assa purisau kassakau gahapatikau karakārakau rāsivaḍḍhakau| tassa aivamassa – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, puñ‍ñānaṃ gati, puñ‍ñānaṃ vipākau| ayañhi rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau manussau, ahampi manussau| ayañhi rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti, daivau mañ‍ñai| ahaṃ panamhissa kassakau gahapatikau karakārakau rāsivaḍḍhakau| sau vatassāhaṃ puñ‍ñāni karaiyyaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti|

‘‘sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ  vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā  anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau kāyaina saṃvutau viharaiyya, vācāya saṃvutau viharaiyya, manasā saṃvutau viharaiyya, ghāsacchādanaparamatāya santuṭṭhau, abhiratau pavivaikai| taṃ cai tai purisā aivamāraucaiyyuṃ – ‘yagghai, daiva jānaiyyāsi, yau tai sau purisau yau tai purisau (sī॰) kassakau gahapatikau karakārakau rāsivaḍḍhakau; sau daiva kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau kāyaina saṃvutau viharati, vācāya saṃvutau viharati, manasā saṃvutau viharati, ghāsacchādanaparamatāya  santuṭṭhau, abhiratau pavivaikai’’ti| api nu tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyāsi – ‘aitu mai, bhau, sau purisau, punadaiva hautu kassakau gahapatikau karakārakau rāsivaḍḍhakau’ti?

187. ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| atha khau naṃ mayamaiva abhivādaiyyāmapi, pac‍cuṭṭhaiyyāmapi, āsanainapi nimantaiyyāma, abhinimantaiyyāmapi naṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi, dhammikampissa rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahaiyyāmā’’ti|

188. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, mahārāja? yadi aivaṃ santai hauti vā sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ nau vā’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai hauti sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphala’’nti| ‘‘idaṃ khau tai, mahārāja, mayā dutiyaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti|

paṇītatarasāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ

189. ‘‘sak‍kā  pana, bhantai, añ‍ñampi diṭṭhaiva dhammai sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ imaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍cā’’ti? ‘‘sak‍kā, mahārāja| taina hi, mahārāja, suṇauhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

190. bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, mahārāja, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ  laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti|

191. ‘‘taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā  tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajaupathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajaiyya’nti|

192. ‘‘sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

193. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, kāyakammavacīkammaina  saman‍nāgatau kusalaina, parisuddhājīvau sīlasampan‍nau, indriyaisu guttadvārau guttadvārau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū (ka॰), satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, santuṭṭhau|

cūḷasīlaṃ

194. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahārāja, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, mahārāja, bhikkhu pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti| nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ  pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ  bhaidāya; amutra  vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā, amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

‘‘bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā  paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti| mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti| jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā  paṭiviratau hauti| āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| hatthigavassavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti| tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti| uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

cūḷasīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

majjhimasīlaṃ

195. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – mūlabījaṃ khandhabījaṃ phaḷubījaṃ aggabījaṃ bījabījamaiva pañ‍camaṃ, iti aivarūpā bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā  paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

196. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ san‍nidhikāraparibhaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – an‍nasan‍nidhiṃ pānasan‍nidhiṃ vatthasan‍nidhiṃ yānasan‍nidhiṃ sayanasan‍nidhiṃ gandhasan‍nidhiṃ āmisasan‍nidhiṃ, iti vā iti aivarūpā san‍nidhikāraparibhaugā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

197. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ  – nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vāditaṃ paikkhaṃ akkhānaṃ  pāṇissaraṃ vaitāḷaṃ kumbhathūṇaṃ saubhanakaṃ caṇḍālaṃ vaṃsaṃ dhauvanaṃ hatthiyuddhaṃ assayuddhaṃ mahiṃsayuddhaṃ usabhayuddhaṃ ajayuddhaṃ maiṇḍayuddhaṃ kuk‍kuṭayuddhaṃ vaṭṭakayuddhaṃ daṇḍayuddhaṃ muṭṭhiyuddhaṃ nibbuddhaṃ uyyaudhikaṃ balaggaṃ sainābyūhaṃ anīkadassanaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpā visūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

198. ‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ jūtappamādaṭṭhānānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhapadaṃ dasapadaṃ ākāsaṃ parihārapathaṃ santikaṃ khalikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ salākahatthaṃ akkhaṃ paṅgacīraṃ vaṅkakaṃ maukkhacikaṃ ciṅgulikaṃ pattāḷhakaṃ rathakaṃ dhanukaṃ akkharikaṃ manaisikaṃ yathāvaj‍jaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpā jūtappamādaṭṭhānānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

199. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – āsandiṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ gaunakaṃ cittakaṃ paṭikaṃ paṭalikaṃ tūlikaṃ vikatikaṃ uddalaumiṃ aikantalaumiṃ kaṭṭissaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kuttakaṃ hatthattharaṃ assattharaṃ rathattharaṃ ajinappavaiṇiṃ kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ sauttaracchadaṃ ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpā  uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā  paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

200. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ maṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānānuyaugaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – ucchādanaṃ parimaddanaṃ nhāpanaṃ sambāhanaṃ ādāsaṃ añ‍janaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ mukhacuṇṇaṃ mukhalaipanaṃ hatthabandhaṃ sikhābandhaṃ daṇḍaṃ nāḷikaṃ asiṃ khaggaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), asiṃ khaggaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), khaggaṃ asiṃ (ka॰) chattaṃ citrupāhanaṃ uṇhīsaṃ maṇiṃ vālabījaniṃ audātāni vatthāni dīghadasāni iti vā iti aivarūpā maṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

201. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ kumārakathaṃ kumārikathaṃ (ka॰) sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ  nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ  samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānakathāya paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

202. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ viggāhikakathaṃ anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ  – na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi, kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi, micchā paṭipan‍nau tvamasi, ahamasmi sammā paṭipan‍nau, sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai, purai vacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchā vacanīyaṃ purai avaca, adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattaṃ, āraupitau tai vādau, niggahitau tvamasi, cara vādappamaukkhāya, nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausīti iti vā iti aivarūpāya viggāhikakathāya paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

203. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugaṃ  anuyuttā viharanti| saiyyathidaṃ – rañ‍ñaṃ, rājamahāmattānaṃ, khattiyānaṃ, brāhmaṇānaṃ, gahapatikānaṃ, kumārānaṃ – ‘idha gaccha, amutrāgaccha, idaṃ hara, amutra idaṃ āharā’ti iti vā iti aivarūpā dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

204. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai kuhakā ca haunti lapakā ca naimittikā ca nippaisikā ca lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīṃsitārau ca| iti aivarūpā kuhanalapanā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ’’|

majjhimasīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

mahāsīlaṃ

205. ‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – aṅgaṃ nimittaṃ uppātaṃ supinaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ mūsikacchin‍naṃ aggihaumaṃ dabbihaumaṃ thusahaumaṃ kaṇahaumaṃ taṇḍulahaumaṃ sappihaumaṃ  tailahaumaṃ  mukhahaumaṃ lauhitahaumaṃ aṅgavij‍jā vatthuvij‍jā khattavij‍jā sivavij‍jā bhūtavij‍jā bhūrivij‍jā ahivij‍jā visavij‍jā vicchikavij‍jā mūsikavij‍jā sakuṇavij‍jā vāyasavij‍jā pak‍kajjhānaṃ saraparittāṇaṃ migacak‍kaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

206. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – maṇilakkhaṇaṃ vatthalakkhaṇaṃ daṇḍalakkhaṇaṃ satthalakkhaṇaṃ asilakkhaṇaṃ usulakkhaṇaṃ dhanulakkhaṇaṃ āvudhalakkhaṇaṃ itthilakkhaṇaṃ purisalakkhaṇaṃ kumāralakkhaṇaṃ kumārilakkhaṇaṃ dāsalakkhaṇaṃ dāsilakkhaṇaṃ hatthilakkhaṇaṃ assalakkhaṇaṃ mahiṃsalakkhaṇaṃ usabhalakkhaṇaṃ gaulakkhaṇaṃ ajalakkhaṇaṃ maiṇḍalakkhaṇaṃ kuk‍kuṭalakkhaṇaṃ vaṭṭakalakkhaṇaṃ gaudhālakkhaṇaṃ kaṇṇikalakkhaṇaṃ kacchapalakkhaṇaṃ migalakkhaṇaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

207. ‘‘yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya  micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – rañ‍ñaṃ niyyānaṃ bhavissati, rañ‍ñaṃ aniyyānaṃ bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ upayānaṃ bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ apayānaṃ bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ upayānaṃ bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ apayānaṃ bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ jayau bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ parājayau bhavissati, bāhirānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ jayau bhavissati, abbhantarānaṃ rañ‍ñaṃ parājayau bhavissati, iti imassa jayau bhavissati, imassa parājayau bhavissati iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

208. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – candaggāhau bhavissati, sūriyaggāhau bhavissati, nakkhattaggāhau bhavissati, candimasūriyānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, candimasūriyānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, nakkhattānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, nakkhattānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, uk‍kāpātau bhavissati, disāḍāhau bhavissati, bhūmicālau bhavissati, daivadudrabhi bhavissati, candimasūriyanakkhattānaṃ uggamanaṃ augamanaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau  candaggāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau sūriyaggāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau  nakkhattaggāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ nakkhattānaṃ pathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ nakkhattānaṃ uppathagamanaṃ bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau uk‍kāpātau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau disāḍāhau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau bhūmicālau bhavissati, aivaṃvipākau daivadudrabhi bhavissati, aivaṃvipākaṃ candimasūriyanakkhattānaṃ uggamanaṃ augamanaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ bhavissati iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

209. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya  micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – suvuṭṭhikā bhavissati, dubbuṭṭhikā bhavissati, subhikkhaṃ bhavissati, dubbhikkhaṃ bhavissati, khaimaṃ bhavissati, bhayaṃ bhavissati, raugau bhavissati, āraugyaṃ bhavissati, muddā, gaṇanā, saṅkhānaṃ, kāvaiyyaṃ, laukāyataṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

210. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – āvāhanaṃ vivāhanaṃ saṃvaraṇaṃ vivaraṇaṃ saṅkiraṇaṃ  vikiraṇaṃ subhagakaraṇaṃ dubbhagakaraṇaṃ viruddhagabbhakaraṇaṃ jivhānibandhanaṃ hanusaṃhananaṃ hatthābhijappanaṃ hanujappanaṃ kaṇṇajappanaṃ ādāsapañhaṃ kumārikapañhaṃ daivapañhaṃ ādic‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ mahatupaṭṭhānaṃ abbhuj‍jalanaṃ sirivhāyanaṃ iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

211. ‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – santikammaṃ paṇidhikammaṃ bhūtakammaṃ bhūrikammaṃ vassakammaṃ vaussakammaṃ vatthukammaṃ vatthuparikammaṃ ācamanaṃ nhāpanaṃ juhanaṃ vamanaṃ viraicanaṃ uddhaṃviraicanaṃ adhauviraicanaṃ sīsaviraicanaṃ kaṇṇatailaṃ naittatappanaṃ natthukammaṃ añ‍janaṃ pac‍cañ‍janaṃ sālākiyaṃ sal‍lakattiyaṃ dārakatikicchā, mūlabhaisaj‍jānaṃ anuppadānaṃ, ausadhīnaṃ paṭimaukkhau iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

212. ‘‘sa  khau sau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ sīlasaṃvaratau| saiyyathāpi – mahārāja, rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau nihatapac‍cāmittau  na  kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ pac‍catthikatau; aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ sīlasaṃvaratau| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivaṃ khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti|

mahāsīlaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

indriyasaṃvarau

213. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahārāja, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idha, mahārāja, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhā daumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhā daumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai  saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivaṃ khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

satisampajañ‍ñaṃ

214. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahārāja, bhikkhu satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, mahārāja, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī  hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| aivaṃ khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

santausau

215. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, mahārāja, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti? idha, mahārāja, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti, sapattabhārauva ḍaiti| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| aivaṃ khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti|

nīvaraṇappahānaṃ

216. ‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau, vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

217. ‘‘sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau  viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī, satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati, ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati, akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

218. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau iṇaṃ ādāya kammantai payaujaiyya| tassa tai kammantā samijjhaiyyuṃ| sau yāni ca paurāṇāni iṇamūlāni, tāni ca byantiṃ karaiyya byantīkaraiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), siyā cassa uttariṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ dārabharaṇāya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai iṇaṃ ādāya kammantai  payaujaisiṃ| tassa  mai tai kammantā samijjhiṃsu| sauhaṃ yāni ca paurāṇāni iṇamūlāni, tāni ca byantiṃ akāsiṃ, atthi ca mai uttariṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ dārabharaṇāyā’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

219. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau ābādhikau assa dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau; bhattañ‍cassa nacchādaiyya, na cassa kāyai balamattā| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā ābādhā muc‍caiyya; bhattaṃ cassa chādaiyya, siyā cassa kāyai balamattā| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai ābādhikau ahausiṃ dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau; bhattañ‍ca mai nacchādaisi, na ca mai āsi na cassa mai (ka॰) kāyai balamattā| saumhi aitarahi tamhā ābādhā muttau; bhattañ‍ca mai chādaiti, atthi ca mai kāyai balamattā’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

220. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau bandhanāgārai baddhau assa| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā bandhanāgārā muc‍caiyya sautthinā abbhayaina ubbayaina (sī॰ ka॰), na cassa kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai bandhanāgārai baddhau ahausiṃ, saumhi aitarahi tamhā bandhanāgārā muttau  sautthinā abbhayaina| natthi  ca mai kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

221. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau dāsau assa anattādhīnau parādhīnau na yainakāmaṃgamau| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā dāsabyā muc‍caiyya attādhīnau aparādhīnau bhujissau yainakāmaṃgamau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai dāsau ahausiṃ anattādhīnau parādhīnau na yainakāmaṃgamau| saumhi aitarahi tamhā dāsabyā muttau attādhīnau aparādhīnau bhujissau yainakāmaṃgamau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ  labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

222. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau sadhanau sabhaugau kantāraddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya dubbhikkhaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ| sau aparaina samayaina taṃ kantāraṃ nittharaiyya sautthinā, gāmantaṃ anupāpuṇaiyya khaimaṃ appaṭibhayaṃ| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai sadhanau sabhaugau kantāraddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃ dubbhikkhaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ| saumhi aitarahi taṃ kantāraṃ nitthiṇṇau sautthinā, gāmantaṃ anuppattau khaimaṃ appaṭibhaya’nti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

223. ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu yathā iṇaṃ yathā raugaṃ yathā bandhanāgāraṃ yathā dāsabyaṃ yathā kantāraddhānamaggaṃ, aivaṃ imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai appahīnai attani samanupassati|

224. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, yathā āṇaṇyaṃ yathā āraugyaṃ yathā bandhanāmaukkhaṃ yathā bhujissaṃ yathā khaimantabhūmiṃ; aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahīnai attani samanupassati|

225. ‘‘tassimai  pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahīnai attani samanupassatau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati|

paṭhamajjhānaṃ

226. ‘‘sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi, vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva  kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

227. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, dakkhau nhāpakau vā nhāpakantaivāsī vā kaṃsathālai nhānīyacuṇṇāni ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ san‍naiyya, sāyaṃ nhānīyapiṇḍi snaihānugatā snaihaparaitā santarabāhirā phuṭā snaihaina, na ca paggharaṇī; aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

dutiyajjhānaṃ

228. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahārāja, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa  kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

229. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, udakarahadau gambhīrau ubbhidaudakau ubbhitaudakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tassa naivassa puratthimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na dakkhiṇāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na pacchimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na uttarāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, daivau ca na kālainakālaṃ sammādhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| atha khau tamhāva udakarahadā sītā vāridhārā ubbhij‍jitvā tamaiva udakarahadaṃ sītaina vārinā abhisandaiyya parisandaiyya paripūraiyya parippharaiyya, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau udakarahadassa sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti  paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ  hauti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

tatiyajjhānaṃ

230. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahārāja, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

231. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā  udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, tāni yāva caggā yāva ca mūlā sītaina vārinā abhisan‍nāni parisan‍nāni abhisandāni parisandāni (ka॰) paripūrāni paripphuṭāni paripphuṭṭhāni (pī॰), nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvataṃ uppalānaṃ vā padumānaṃ vā puṇḍarīkānaṃ vā sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ  sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

catutthajjhānaṃ

232. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahārāja, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā  nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

233. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau audātaina vatthaina sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisin‍nau assa, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa audātaina vatthaina apphuṭaṃ assa; aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi  sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

vipassanāñāṇaṃ

234. ‘‘sau puna caparaṃ mahārāja bhikkhu sau (ka॰) aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ayaṃ khau mai kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādana-parimaddana-bhaidana-viddhaṃsana-dhammau; idañ‍ca pana mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aittha sitaṃ aittha paṭibaddha’nti|

235. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau sabbākārasampan‍nau| tatrāssa suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā pītakaṃ vā lauhitakaṃ vā (ka॰) audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ‘ayaṃ khau maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau sabbākārasampan‍nau; tatridaṃ suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ  vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā’ti| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ayaṃ khau mai kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau; idañ‍ca  pana mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aittha sitaṃ aittha paṭibaddha’nti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

manaumayiddhiñāṇaṃ

236. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmānāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmināti rūpiṃ manaumayaṃ sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ|

237. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau muñ‍jamhā īsikaṃ pavāhaiyya pabbāhaiyya (syā॰ ka॰)| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ muñ‍jau, ayaṃ īsikā, añ‍ñau muñ‍jau, añ‍ñā īsikā, muñ‍jamhā tvaiva īsikā pavāḷhā’ti pabbāḷhāti (syā॰ ka॰)| saiyyathā vā pana, mahārāja, purisau asiṃ kausiyā pavāhaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ asi, ayaṃ kausi, añ‍ñau asi, añ‍ñā kausi, kausiyā tvaiva asi pavāḷhau’’ti| saiyyathā vā pana, mahārāja, purisau ahiṃ karaṇḍā uddharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ ahi, ayaṃ karaṇḍau| añ‍ñau ahi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍau, karaṇḍā tvaiva ahi ubbhatau’ti uddharitau (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmānāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmināti rūpiṃ manaumayaṃ sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca  paṇītatarañ‍ca|

iddhividhañāṇaṃ

238. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai iddhividhāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati saiyyathāpi ākāsai| pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti saiyyathāpi udakai| udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati abhij‍jamānau (sī॰ ka॰) saiyyathāpi pathaviyā| ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau| imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimaj‍jati| yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

239. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, dakkhau kumbhakārau vā kumbhakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatāya mattikāya yaṃ yadaiva bhājanavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya, taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| saiyyathā vā pana, mahārāja, dakkhau dantakārau vā dantakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatasmiṃ dantasmiṃ yaṃ yadaiva dantavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya, taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| saiyyathā vā pana, mahārāja, dakkhau suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatasmiṃ suvaṇṇasmiṃ yaṃ yadaiva suvaṇṇavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya, taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai iddhividhāya  cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati saiyyathāpi ākāsai| pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti saiyyathāpi udakai| udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati saiyyathāpi pathaviyā| ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau| imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai  pāṇinā parāmasati parimaj‍jati| yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

dibbasautañāṇaṃ

240. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai dibbāya sautadhātuyā cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca|

241. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau| sau suṇaiyya bhairisaddampi mudiṅgasaddampi mutiṅgasaddampi (sī॰ pī॰) saṅkhapaṇavadindimasaddampi saṅkhapaṇavadaiṇḍimasaddampi (sī॰ pī॰), saṅkhasaddaṃpi paṇavasaddaṃpi daindimasaddaṃpi (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tassa aivamassa – ‘bhairisaddau’ itipi, ‘mudiṅgasaddau’ itipi, ‘saṅkhapaṇavadindimasaddau’ itipi saṅkhasaddau itipi paṇavasaddau itipi daindimasaddau itipi (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai dibbāya sautadhātuyā  cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

caitaupariyañāṇaṃ

242. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai caitaupariyañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, saṅkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘saṅkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘mahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘anuttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti|

243. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā  maṇḍanajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udakapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sakaṇikaṃ vā ‘sakaṇika’nti jānaiyya, akaṇikaṃ vā ‘akaṇika’nti jānaiyya; aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai caitaupariyañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, saṅkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘saṅkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘mahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘anuttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ

244. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau  aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī  aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

245. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, purisau sakamhā gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya, tamhāpi gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya| sau tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgacchaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau sakamhā gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ agacchiṃ agañchiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tatrāpi aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ, aivaṃ nisīdiṃ, aivaṃ  abhāsiṃ, aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ, tamhāpi gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ agacchiṃ, tatrāpi aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ, aivaṃ nisīdiṃ, aivaṃ abhāsiṃ, aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ, saumhi tamhā gāmā  sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgatau’ti| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai, ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti, iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ

246. ‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai  hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina  sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

247. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, majjhai siṅghāṭakai pāsādau| tattha cakkhumā purisau ṭhitau passaiyya manussai gaihaṃ pavisantaipi nikkhamantaipi rathikāyapi vīthiṃ sañ‍carantai rathiyāpi rathiṃ sañ‍carantai (sī॰), rathiyāya vithiṃ sañ‍carantaipi (syā॰) majjhai siṅghāṭakai nisin‍naipi| tassa aivamassa – ‘aitai manussā gaihaṃ pavisanti, aitai nikkhamanti, aitai rathikāya vīthiṃ sañ‍caranti, aitai majjhai siṅghāṭakai nisin‍nā’ti| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati  cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai  vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai; yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| ‘idampi khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

āsavakkhayañāṇaṃ

248. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| imai āsavāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavasamudayauti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, ‘vimuttasmiṃ vimuttami’ti ñāṇaṃ hauti, ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

249. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, mahārāja, pabbatasaṅkhaipai udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau| tattha cakkhumā purisau tīrai ṭhitau passaiyya sippisambukampi sakkharakathalampi macchagumbampi carantampi tiṭṭhantampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau| tatrimai sippisambukāpi sakkharakathalāpi macchagumbāpi carantipi tiṭṭhantipī’ti| aivamaiva khau, mahārāja, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| ‘sau idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘imai āsavāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ  vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, ‘vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti, ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| idaṃ  khau, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ purimaihi sandiṭṭhikaihi sāmañ‍ñaphalaihi abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca| imasmā ca pana, mahārāja, sandiṭṭhikā sāmañ‍ñaphalā añ‍ñaṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā natthī’’ti|

ajātasattuupāsakattapaṭivaidanā

250. aivaṃ vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ pitaraṃ dhammikaṃ dhammarājānaṃ issariyakāraṇā jīvitā vauraupaisiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti|

251. ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, mahārāja, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ  yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ pitaraṃ dhammikaṃ dhammarājānaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, mahārāja, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhihaisā, mahārāja, ariyassa vinayai, yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti|

252. aivaṃ  vuttai, rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhantai, gacchāma bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, mahārāja, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

253. atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantassa rañ‍ñau māgadhassa  ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘khatāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā| upahatāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā| sacāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā pitaraṃ dhammikaṃ dhammarājānaṃ jīvitā na vauraupaissatha, imasmiñ‍ñaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ uppaj‍jissathā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

sāmañ‍ñaphalasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|



3. ambaṭṭhasuttaṃ

254. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi yaina icchānaṅgalaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai|

paukkharasātivatthu

255. taina khau pana samayaina brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti uk‍kaṭṭhaṃ paukkharasātī (sī॰), paukkharasādi (pī॰) ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| assausi khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppattau icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ bhagavāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), uparisauṇadaṇḍasuttādīsupi buddhaguṇakathāyaṃ aivamaiva dissati| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ  pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

ambaṭṭhamāṇavau

256. taina khau pana samayaina brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa ambaṭṭhau nāma māṇavau antaivāsī hauti ajjhāyakau mantadharau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ baidānaṃ (ka॰) pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ  itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau anuñ‍ñātapaṭiñ‍ñātau sakai ācariyakai taivij‍jakai pāvacanai  – ‘‘yamahaṃ jānāmi, taṃ tvaṃ jānāsi; yaṃ tvaṃ jānāsi tamahaṃ jānāmī’’ti|

257. atha khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ayaṃ, tāta ambaṭṭha, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppattau icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautīti| aihi tvaṃ tāta ambaṭṭha, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā  samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jānāhi, yadi vā taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ tathāsantaṃyaiva saddau abbhuggatau, yadi vā nau tathā| yadi vā sau bhavaṃ gautamau tādisau, yadi vā na tādisau, tathā mayaṃ taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ vaidissāmā’’ti|

258. ‘‘yathā kathaṃ panāhaṃ, bhau, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ jānissāmi – ‘yadi vā taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ tathāsantaṃyaiva saddau abbhuggatau, yadi vā nau tathā| yadi vā sau bhavaṃ gautamau tādisau, yadi vā na tādisau’’’ti?

‘‘āgatāni khau, tāta ambaṭṭha, amhākaṃ mantaisu dvattiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiyaiva gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā hauti cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassimāni satta ratanāni bhavanti| saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ, hatthiratanaṃ, assaratanaṃ, maṇiratanaṃ, itthiratanaṃ, gahapatiratanaṃ, pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā bhavanti  sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasati| sacai khau pana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammāsambuddhau laukai vivaṭṭacchadau| ahaṃ khau pana, tāta ambaṭṭha, mantānaṃ dātā; tvaṃ mantānaṃ paṭiggahaitā’’ti|

259. ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā brāhmaṇaṃ paukkharasātiṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā vaḷavārathamāruyha  sambahulaihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ yaina icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍau taina pāyāsi| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva ārāmaṃ pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū abbhaukāsai caṅkamanti| atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhau, aitarahi sau bhavaṃ gautamau viharati? tañhi mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya idhūpasaṅkantā’’ti|

260. atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau abhiñ‍ñātakaulañ‍ñau caiva abhiñ‍ñātassa ca brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa antaivāsī| agaru khau pana bhagavatau aivarūpaihi kulaputtaihi saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau hautī’’ti| tai ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aisau ambaṭṭha vihārau saṃvutadvārau, taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau āḷindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaihi, vivarissati tai bhagavā dvāra’’nti|

261. atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau yaina sau vihārau saṃvutadvārau, taina appasaddau upasaṅkamitvā ataramānau āḷindaṃ pavisitvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaisi| vivari bhagavā dvāraṃ| pāvisi ambaṭṭhau māṇavau| māṇavakāpi pavisitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| ambaṭṭhau pana māṇavau caṅkamantaupi nisin‍naina bhagavatā kañ‍ci  kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci kiñ‍ci (ka॰) kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraiti, ṭhitaupi nisin‍naina bhagavatā kiñ‍ci kiñ‍ci kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraiti|

262. atha khau bhagavā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivaṃ nu tai, ambaṭṭha, brāhmaṇaihi vuddhaihi mahal‍lakaihi ācariyapācariyaihi saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau hauti, yathayidaṃ caraṃ tiṭṭhaṃ nisin‍naina mayā kiñ‍ci kiñ‍ci kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitī’’ti?

paṭhamaibbhavādau

263. ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama| gacchantau vā hi, bhau gautama, gacchantaina brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ sal‍lapitumarahati, ṭhitau vā hi, bhau gautama, ṭhitaina brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ sal‍lapitumarahati, nisin‍nau vā hi, bhau gautama, nisin‍naina brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ sal‍lapitumarahati, sayānau vā hi, bhau gautama, sayānaina brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇaina saddhiṃ sal‍lapitumarahati| yai ca khau tai, bhau gautama, muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kaṇhā kiṇhā (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) bandhupādāpac‍cā, taihipi mai saddhiṃ aivaṃ kathāsal‍lāpau hauti, yathariva bhautā gautamainā’’ti| ‘‘atthikavatau khau pana tai, ambaṭṭha, idhāgamanaṃ ahausi, yāyaiva khau panatthāya āgacchaiyyātha āgacchaiyyāthau (sī॰ pī॰), tamaiva atthaṃ sādhukaṃ manasi karaiyyātha manasikaraiyyāthau (sī॰ pī॰)| avusitavāyaiva khau pana bhau ayaṃ ambaṭṭhau māṇavau vusitamānī kimañ‍ñatra avusitattā’’ti|

264. atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhagavatā avusitavādaina vuc‍camānau kupitau anattamanau bhagavantaṃyaiva khuṃsaintau bhagavantaṃyaiva vambhaintau bhagavantaṃyaiva upavadamānau – ‘‘samaṇau ca mai, bhau, gautamau pāpitau bhavissatī’’ti bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘caṇḍā, bhau gautama, sakyajāti; pharusā, bhau gautama, sakyajāti; lahusā, bhau  gautama, sakyajāti; bhassā, bhau gautama, sakyajāti; ibbhā santā ibbhā samānā na brāhmaṇai sak‍karaunti, na brāhmaṇai garuṃ karaunti garukaraunti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), na brāhmaṇai  mānainti, na brāhmaṇai pūjainti, na brāhmaṇai apacāyanti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, nacchan‍naṃ, tayidaṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yadimai sakyā ibbhā santā ibbhā samānā na brāhmaṇai sak‍karaunti, na brāhmaṇai garuṃ karaunti, na brāhmaṇai mānainti, na brāhmaṇai pūjainti, na brāhmaṇai apacāyantī’’ti| itiha ambaṭṭhau māṇavau idaṃ paṭhamaṃ sakyaisu ibbhavādaṃ nipātaisi|

dutiyaibbhavādau

265. ‘‘kiṃ pana tai, ambaṭṭha, sakyā aparaddhu’’nti? ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhau gautama, samayaṃ ācariyassa brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina kapilavatthuṃ agamāsiṃ| yaina sakyānaṃ sandhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā sakyā caiva sakyakumārā ca sandhāgārai santhāgārai (sī॰ pī॰) uc‍caisu āsanaisu nisin‍nā haunti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ aṅgulipataudakaihi aṅgulipataudakaina (pī॰) sañ‍jagghantā saṃkīḷantā, añ‍ñadatthu mamañ‍ñaiva mañ‍ñai anujagghantā, na maṃ kauci āsanainapi nimantaisi| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, nacchan‍naṃ, tayidaṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yadimai sakyā ibbhā santā ibbhā  samānā na brāhmaṇai sak‍karaunti, na brāhmaṇai garuṃ karaunti, na brāhmaṇai mānainti, na brāhmaṇai pūjainti, na brāhmaṇai apacāyantī’’ti| itiha ambaṭṭhau māṇavau idaṃ dutiyaṃ sakyaisu ibbhavādaṃ nipātaisi|

tatiyaibbhavādau

266. ‘‘laṭukikāpi  khau, ambaṭṭha, sakuṇikā sakai kulāvakai kāmalāpinī hauti| sakaṃ khau panaitaṃ, ambaṭṭha, sakyānaṃ yadidaṃ kapilavatthuṃ, nārahatāyasmā ambaṭṭhau imāya appamattāya abhisaj‍jitu’’nti| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhau gautama, vaṇṇā – khattiyā brāhmaṇā vaissā suddā| imaisañhi, bhau gautama, catun‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ tayau vaṇṇā – khattiyā ca vaissā ca suddā ca – añ‍ñadatthu brāhmaṇassaiva paricārakā sampaj‍janti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, nacchan‍naṃ, tayidaṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yadimai sakyā ibbhā santā ibbhā samānā na brāhmaṇai sak‍karaunti, na brāhmaṇai garuṃ karaunti, na brāhmaṇai mānainti, na brāhmaṇai pūjainti, na brāhmaṇai apacāyantī’’ti| itiha ambaṭṭhau māṇavau idaṃ tatiyaṃ sakyaisu ibbhavādaṃ nipātaisi|

dāsiputtavādau

267. atha  khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atibāḷhaṃ khau ayaṃ ambaṭṭhau māṇavau sakyaisu ibbhavādaina nimmādaiti, yaṃnūnāhaṃ gauttaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ gauttausi, ambaṭṭhā’’ti? ‘‘kaṇhāyanauhamasmi, bhau gautamā’’ti| paurāṇaṃ khau pana tai ambaṭṭha mātāpaittikaṃ nāmagauttaṃ anussaratau ayyaputtā sakyā bhavanti; dāsiputtau tvamasi sakyānaṃ| sakyā khau pana, ambaṭṭha, rājānaṃ auk‍kākaṃ pitāmahaṃ dahanti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, ambaṭṭha, rājā auk‍kākau yā sā mahaisī piyā  manāpā, tassā puttassa raj‍jaṃ pariṇāmaitukāmau jaiṭṭhakumārai raṭṭhasmā pabbājaisi – auk‍kāmukhaṃ karakaṇḍaṃ uk‍kāmukhaṃ karakaṇḍuṃ (sī॰ syā॰) hatthinikaṃ sinisūraṃ sinipuraṃ (sī॰ syā॰)| tai raṭṭhasmā pabbājitā himavantapassai paukkharaṇiyā tīrai mahāsākasaṇḍau, tattha vāsaṃ kappaisuṃ| tai jātisambhaidabhayā sakāhi bhaginīhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaisuṃ|
