‘‘atha khau, ambaṭṭha, rājā auk‍kākau amac‍cai pārisaj‍jai āmantaisi – ‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhau, aitarahi kumārā sammantī’ti? ‘atthi, daiva, himavantapassai paukkharaṇiyā tīrai mahāsākasaṇḍau, tatthaitarahi kumārā sammanti| tai jātisambhaidabhayā sakāhi bhaginīhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaintī’ti| atha khau, ambaṭṭha, rājā auk‍kākau udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘sakyā  vata, bhau, kumārā, paramasakyā vata, bhau, kumārā’ti| tadaggai khau pana ambaṭṭha sakyā pañ‍ñāyanti; sau ca naisaṃ pubbapurisau|

‘‘rañ‍ñau khau pana, ambaṭṭha, auk‍kākassa disā nāma dāsī ahausi| sā kaṇhaṃ nāma sā kaṇhaṃ (pī॰) janaisi| jātau kaṇhau pabyāhāsi – ‘dhauvatha maṃ, amma, nahāpaitha maṃ amma, imasmā maṃ asucismā parimaucaitha, atthāya vau bhavissāmī’ti| yathā khau pana ambaṭṭha aitarahi manussā pisācai disvā ‘pisācā’ti sañ‍jānanti; aivamaiva khau, ambaṭṭha, taina khau pana samayaina manussā pisācai ‘kaṇhā’ti sañ‍jānanti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayaṃ jātau pabyāhāsi, kaṇhau jātau, pisācau jātau’ti| tadaggai khau pana, ambaṭṭha kaṇhāyanā pañ‍ñāyanti, sau ca  kaṇhāyanānaṃ pubbapurisau| iti khau tai, ambaṭṭha, paurāṇaṃ mātāpaittikaṃ nāmagauttaṃ anussaratau ayyaputtā sakyā bhavanti, dāsiputtau tvamasi sakyāna’’nti|

268. aivaṃ vuttai, tai māṇavakā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā bhavaṃ gautamau ambaṭṭhaṃ atibāḷhaṃ dāsiputtavādaina nimmādaisi| sujātau ca, bhau gautama ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, kulaputtau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, bahussutau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, paṇḍitau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, pahauti ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’’nti|

269. atha khau bhagavā tai māṇavakai aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai khau tumhākaṃ māṇavakānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘duj‍jātau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, akulaputtau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, appassutau  ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, akalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, duppañ‍ñau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, na ca pahauti ambaṭṭhau māṇavau samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’nti, tiṭṭhatu ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, tumhai mayā saddhiṃ mantavhau asmiṃ vacanai| sacai pana tumhākaṃ māṇavakānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘sujātau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, kulaputtau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, bahussutau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, paṇḍitau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, pahauti ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’nti, tiṭṭhatha  tumhai; ambaṭṭhau māṇavau mayā saddhiṃ paṭimantaitū’’ti|

‘‘sujātau ca, bhau gautama, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, kulaputtau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, bahussutau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau  ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, paṇḍitau ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, pahauti ca ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaituṃ, tuṇhī mayaṃ bhavissāma, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitū’’ti|

270. atha khau bhagavā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ khau pana tai, ambaṭṭha, sahadhammikau pañhau āgacchati, akāmā byākātabbau| sacai tvaṃ na byākarissasi, añ‍ñaina vā añ‍ñaṃ paṭicarissasi, tuṇhī vā bhavissasi, pak‍kamissasi  vā aitthaiva tai sattadhā muddhā phalissati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, kinti tai sutaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuddhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ kutaupabhutikā kaṇhāyanā, kau ca kaṇhāyanānaṃ pubbapurisau’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, kinti tai sutaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuddhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ kutaupabhutikā kaṇhāyanā, kau ca kaṇhāyanānaṃ  pubbapurisau’’ti? dutiyampi khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau bhagavā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākarauhi dāni ambaṭṭha, na dāni, tai tuṇhībhāvassa kālau| yau  khau, ambaṭṭha, tathāgataina yāvatatiyakaṃ sahadhammikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na byākarauti, aitthaivassa sattadhā muddhā phalissatī’’ti|

271. taina khau pana samayaina vajirapāṇī yakkhau mahantaṃ ayaukūṭaṃ ādāya ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ sañ‍jautibhūtaṃ (syā॰) ambaṭṭhassa māṇavassa upari vaihāsaṃ ṭhitau hauti – ‘‘sacāyaṃ ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhagavatā yāvatatiyakaṃ sahadhammikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na byākarissati, aitthaivassa sattadhā muddhaṃ phālaissāmī’’ti| taṃ khau pana vajirapāṇiṃ yakkhaṃ bhagavā caiva passati ambaṭṭhau ca māṇavau|

272. atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau bhagavantaṃyaiva tāṇaṃ gavaisī bhagavantaṃyaiva laiṇaṃ gavaisī bhagavantaṃyaiva saraṇaṃ gavaisī – upanisīditvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kimaitaṃ kiṃ mai taṃ (ka॰) bhavaṃ gautamau āha? punabhavaṃ gautamau bravitū’’ti brūtu (syā॰)|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, kinti tai sutaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuddhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ  bhāsamānānaṃ kutaupabhutikā kaṇhāyanā, kau ca kaṇhāyanānaṃ pubbapurisau’’ti? ‘‘aivamaiva mai, bhau gautama, sutaṃ yathaiva bhavaṃ gautamau āha| tataupabhutikā kaṇhāyanā; sau ca kaṇhāyanānaṃ pubbapurisau’’ti|

ambaṭṭhavaṃsakathā

273. aivaṃ  vuttai, tai māṇavakā un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā ahaisuṃ – ‘‘duj‍jātau kira, bhau, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau; akulaputtau kira, bhau, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau; dāsiputtau kira, bhau, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau sakyānaṃ| ayyaputtā kira, bhau, ambaṭṭhassa māṇavassa sakyā  bhavanti| dhammavādiṃyaiva kira mayaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ apasādaitabbaṃ amañ‍ñimhā’’ti|

274. atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atibāḷhaṃ khau imai  māṇavakā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ dāsiputtavādaina nimmādainti, yaṃnūnāhaṃ parimaucaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā tai māṇavakai aitadavauca – ‘‘mā khau tumhai, māṇavakā, ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ atibāḷhaṃ dāsiputtavādaina nimmādaitha| uḷārau sau kaṇhau isi ahausi| sau dakkhiṇajanapadaṃ gantvā brahmamantai adhīyitvā rājānaṃ auk‍kākaṃ upasaṅkamitvā maddarūpiṃ dhītaraṃ yāci| tassa rājā auk‍kākau – ‘kau naivaṃ rai ayaṃ mayhaṃ dāsiputtau samānau maddarūpiṃ dhītaraṃ yācatī’’’ ti, kupitau anattamanau khurappaṃ san‍nayhi san‍nahi (ka॰)| sau taṃ khurappaṃ naiva asakkhi muñ‍cituṃ, nau paṭisaṃharituṃ|

‘‘atha khau, māṇavakā, amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā kaṇhaṃ isiṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘sautthi, bhaddantai bhadantai (sī॰ syā॰), hautu rañ‍ñau; sautthi, bhaddantai, hautu rañ‍ñau’ti| ‘sautthi bhavissati rañ‍ñau, api ca rājā yadi adhau khurappaṃ muñ‍cissati, yāvatā rañ‍ñau vijitaṃ, aittāvatā pathavī undriyissatī’ti| ‘sautthi, bhaddantai, hautu rañ‍ñau, sautthi janapadassā’ti| ‘sautthi bhavissati rañ‍ñau, sautthi janapadassa, api ca rājā yadi uddhaṃ khurappaṃ muñ‍cissati, yāvatā rañ‍ñau vijitaṃ, aittāvatā satta vassāni daivau na vassissatī’ti| ‘sautthi, bhaddantai, hautu rañ‍ñau sautthi janapadassa daivau ca vassatū’ti| ‘sautthi bhavissati rañ‍ñau sautthi janapadassa  daivau ca vassissati, api ca rājā jaiṭṭhakumārai khurappaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitu, sautthi kumārau pal‍laumau bhavissatī’ti| atha khau, māṇavakā, amac‍cā auk‍kākassa āraucaisuṃ – ‘auk‍kākau jaiṭṭhakumārai khurappaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitu| sautthi kumārau pal‍laumau bhavissatī’ti| atha khau rājā auk‍kākau  jaiṭṭhakumārai khurappaṃ patiṭṭhapaisi, sautthi kumārau pal‍laumau  samabhavi| atha khau tassa rājā auk‍kākau bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau brahmadaṇḍaina  taj‍jitau maddarūpiṃ dhītaraṃ adāsi| mā khau tumhai, māṇavakā, ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ atibāḷhaṃ dāsiputtavādaina nimmādaitha, uḷārau sau kaṇhau isi ahausī’’ti|

khattiyasaiṭṭhabhāvau

275. atha khau bhagavā ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, idha khattiyakumārau brāhmaṇakañ‍ñāya saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaiyya, taisaṃ saṃvāsamanvāya puttau jāyaitha| yau sau khattiyakumāraina brāhmaṇakañ‍ñāya puttau uppan‍nau, api nu sau labhaitha brāhmaṇaisu āsanaṃ vā udakaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘labhaitha, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā’’ti? ‘‘bhaujaiyyuṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā mantai vācaiyyuṃ vā nau vā’’ti? ‘‘vācaiyyuṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinussa itthīsu āvaṭaṃ vā assa anāvaṭaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anāvaṭaṃ hissa, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ khattiyā khattiyābhisaikaina abhisiñ‍caiyyu’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘mātitau hi, bhau gautama, anupapan‍nau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, idha brāhmaṇakumārau khattiyakañ‍ñāya  saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaiyya, taisaṃ saṃvāsamanvāya puttau jāyaitha| yau sau brāhmaṇakumāraina khattiyakañ‍ñāya puttau uppan‍nau, apinu sau labhaitha brāhmaṇaisu āsanaṃ vā udakaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘labhaitha, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā’’ti? ‘‘bhaujaiyyuṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā mantai vācaiyyuṃ vā nau vā’’ti? ‘‘vācaiyyuṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinussa  itthīsu āvaṭaṃ vā assa anāvaṭaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anāvaṭaṃ hissa, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ khattiyā khattiyābhisaikaina abhisiñ‍caiyyu’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘pititau hi, bhau gautama, anupapan‍nau’’ti|

276. ‘‘iti khau, ambaṭṭha, itthiyā vā itthiṃ karitvā purisaina vā purisaṃ karitvā khattiyāva saiṭṭhā, hīnā brāhmaṇā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, idha brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ kismiñ‍cidaiva pakaraṇai khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā bhassapuṭaina  vadhitvā raṭṭhā vā nagarā vā pabbājaiyyuṃ| apinu sau labhaitha brāhmaṇaisu āsanaṃ vā udakaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā mantai vācaiyyuṃ vā nau vā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinussa itthīsu āvaṭaṃ vā assa anāvaṭaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘āvaṭaṃ hissa, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, idha khattiyā khattiyaṃ kismiñ‍cidaiva pakaraṇai  khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā bhassapuṭaina vadhitvā raṭṭhā vā nagarā vā pabbājaiyyuṃ| apinu sau labhaitha brāhmaṇaisu āsanaṃ vā udakaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘labhaitha, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā bhaujaiyyuṃ saddhai vā thālipākai vā yañ‍ñai vā pāhunai vā’’ti? ‘‘bhaujaiyyuṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinu naṃ brāhmaṇā mantai vācaiyyuṃ vā nau vā’’ti? ‘‘vācaiyyuṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘apinussa itthīsu āvaṭaṃ vā assa anāvaṭaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anāvaṭaṃ hissa, bhau gautama’’|

277. ‘‘aittāvatā khau, ambaṭṭha, khattiyau paramanihīnataṃ pattau  hauti, yadaiva naṃ khattiyā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā bhassapuṭaina vadhitvā raṭṭhā vā nagarā vā pabbājainti| iti khau, ambaṭṭha, yadā khattiyau paramanihīnataṃ pattau hauti, tadāpi khattiyāva saiṭṭhā, hīnā brāhmaṇā| brahmunā paisā, ambaṭṭha brahmunāpi ambaṭṭha (ka॰), brahmunāpi aisau ambaṭṭha (pī॰), sanaṅkumāraina gāthā bhāsitā –

‘khattiyau saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ,

yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau,

sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai’ti||

‘‘sā khau panaisā, ambaṭṭha, brahmunā sanaṅkumāraina gāthā sugītā nau duggītā, subhāsitā nau dubbhāsitā, atthasaṃhitā nau anatthasaṃhitā, anumatā mayā| ahampi hi, ambaṭṭha, aivaṃ vadāmi –

‘khattiyau  saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ,

yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau,

sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai’ti||

bhāṇavārau paṭhamau|

vij‍jācaraṇakathā

278. ‘‘katamaṃ  pana taṃ, bhau gautama, caraṇaṃ, katamā ca pana sā vij‍jā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya jātivādau vā vuc‍cati, gauttavādau vā vuc‍cati, mānavādau vā vuc‍cati – ‘arahasi vā maṃ tvaṃ, na vā maṃ tvaṃ arahasī’ti| yattha khau, ambaṭṭha, āvāhau vā hauti, vivāhau vā hauti, āvāhavivāhau vā hauti, aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati jātivādau vā itipi gauttavādau vā itipi mānavādau vā itipi – ‘arahasi vā maṃ tvaṃ, na vā maṃ tvaṃ arahasī’ti| yai hi kaici ambaṭṭha jātivādavinibaddhā vā gauttavādavinibaddhā vā mānavādavinibaddhā vā āvāhavivāhavinibaddhā vā, ārakā tai anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya| pahāya khau, ambaṭṭha, jātivādavinibaddhañ‍ca gauttavādavinibaddhañ‍ca mānavādavinibaddhañ‍ca āvāhavivāhavinibaddhañ‍ca  anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya sacchikiriyā hautī’’ti|

279. ‘‘katamaṃ pana taṃ, bhau gautama, caraṇaṃ, katamā ca sā vij‍jā’’ti? ‘‘idha, ambaṭṭha, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai  saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina  saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati…pai॰… (yathā 191 ādayau anucchaidā, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|…

‘‘sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi, vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi, savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ambaṭṭha, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ambaṭṭha, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ambaṭṭha, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… idampissa hauti caraṇasmiṃ| idaṃ khau taṃ, ambaṭṭha, caraṇaṃ|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai ñāṇadassanāya  cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰… idampissa hauti vij‍jāya…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti, idampissa hauti vij‍jāya| ayaṃ khau sā, ambaṭṭha, vij‍jā|

‘‘ayaṃ vuc‍cati, ambaṭṭha, bhikkhu ‘vij‍jāsampan‍nau’ itipi, ‘caraṇasampan‍nau’ itipi, ‘vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau’ itipi| imāya ca ambaṭṭha vij‍jāsampadāya caraṇasampadāya ca añ‍ñā vij‍jāsampadā ca caraṇasampadā ca uttaritarā vā paṇītatarā vā natthi|

catuapāyamukhaṃ

280. ‘‘imāya khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya  cattāri apāyamukhāni bhavanti| katamāni cattāri? idha, ambaṭṭha, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā imañ‍ñaiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau khārividhamādāya  khārivividhamādāya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) arañ‍ñāyatanaṃ ajjhaugāhati – ‘pavattaphalabhaujanau bhavissāmī’ti| sau añ‍ñadatthu vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nassaiva paricārakau sampaj‍jati| imāya khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya idaṃ paṭhamaṃ apāyamukhaṃ bhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ambaṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā  imañ‍caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau pavattaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau kudālapiṭakaṃ kuddālapiṭakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ādāya arañ‍ñavanaṃ ajjhaugāhati – ‘kandamūlaphalabhaujanau bhavissāmī’ti| sau añ‍ñadatthu vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nassaiva paricārakau sampaj‍jati| imāya khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya idaṃ dutiyaṃ apāyamukhaṃ bhavati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ambaṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā imañ‍caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau pavattaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau kandamūlaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau gāmasāmantaṃ vā nigamasāmantaṃ vā agyāgāraṃ karitvā aggiṃ paricarantau acchati| sau añ‍ñadatthu vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nassaiva paricārakau sampaj‍jati| imāya khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya idaṃ tatiyaṃ apāyamukhaṃ bhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ambaṭṭha, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā imaṃ caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau pavattaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau kandamūlaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau aggipāricariyañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau cātumahāpathai  catudvāraṃ agāraṃ karitvā acchati – ‘yau imāhi catūhi disāhi āgamissati samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā, tamahaṃ yathāsatti yathābalaṃ paṭipūjaissāmī’ti| sau añ‍ñadatthu vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nassaiva paricārakau sampaj‍jati| imāya khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya idaṃ catutthaṃ apāyamukhaṃ bhavati| imāya khau, ambaṭṭha, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya imāni cattāri apāyamukhāni bhavanti|

281. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, apinu tvaṃ imāya anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya sandissasi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘kaucāhaṃ, bhau  gautama, sācariyakau, kā ca anuttarā vij‍jācaraṇasampadā? ārakāhaṃ, bhau gautama, anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya  sācariyakau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, apinu tvaṃ imañ‍caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau khārividhamādāya arañ‍ñavanamajjhaugāhasi sācariyakau – ‘pavattaphalabhaujanau bhavissāmī’’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, apinu tvaṃ imañ‍caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau pavattaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau kudālapiṭakaṃ ādāya arañ‍ñavanamajjhaugāhasi sācariyakau – ‘kandamūlaphalabhaujanau bhavissāmī’’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, apinu tvaṃ imañ‍caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau pavattaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau kandamūlaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau gāmasāmantaṃ vā nigamasāmantaṃ vā agyāgāraṃ karitvā aggiṃ paricarantau acchasi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, apinu tvaṃ imañ‍caiva anuttaraṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampadaṃ anabhisambhuṇamānau pavattaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau kandamūlaphalabhaujanatañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau aggipāricariyañ‍ca anabhisambhuṇamānau cātumahāpathai catudvāraṃ agāraṃ karitvā acchasi sācariyakau – ‘yau imāhi catūhi disāhi āgamissati samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā, taṃ mayaṃ yathāsatti yathābalaṃ paṭipūjaissāmā’’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

282. ‘‘iti khau, ambaṭṭha, imāya caiva tvaṃ anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya parihīnau sācariyakau| yai cimai anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya cattāri apāyamukhāni bhavanti, tatau ca tvaṃ parihīnau sācariyakau| bhāsitā khau pana tai aisā, ambaṭṭha, ācariyaina brāhmaṇaina paukkharasātinā vācā – ‘kai ca muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kaṇhā bandhupādāpac‍cā, kā ca taivij‍jānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ sākacchā’ti attanā āpāyikaupi aparipūramānau| passa, ambaṭṭha, yāva aparaddhañ‍ca tai idaṃ ācariyassa brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa|

pubbakaisibhāvānuyaugau

283. ‘‘brāhmaṇau  khau pana, ambaṭṭha, paukkharasāti rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa dattikaṃ bhuñ‍jati| tassa rājā pasainadi kausalau sammukhībhāvampi na dadāti| yadāpi taina mantaiti, tiraudussantaina mantaiti| yassa khau pana, ambaṭṭha, dhammikaṃ payātaṃ bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, kathaṃ tassa rājā pasainadi kausalau sammukhībhāvampi na dadaiyya| passa, ambaṭṭha, yāva aparaddhañ‍ca tai idaṃ ācariyassa brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa|

284. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, idha rājā pasainadi kausalau hatthigīvāya vā nisin‍nau assapiṭṭhai vā nisin‍nau rathūpattharai vā ṭhitau uggaihi vā rājañ‍ñaihi vā kiñ‍cidaiva mantanaṃ  mantaiyya| sau tamhā padaisā apak‍kamma aikamantaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| atha āgacchaiyya suddau vā  suddadāsau vā, tasmiṃ padaisai ṭhitau tadaiva mantanaṃ mantaiyya – ‘aivampi rājā pasainadi kausalau āha, aivampi rājā pasainadi kausalau āhā’ti| apinu sau rājabhaṇitaṃ vā bhaṇati rājamantanaṃ vā mantaiti? aittāvatā sau  assa rājā vā rājamattau vā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

285. ‘‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, ambaṭṭha, yai tai ahaisuṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau, yaisamidaṃ aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ, tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti bhāsitamanubhāsanti vācitamanuvācainti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau vāmakau vāmadaivau vaissāmittau yamataggi yamadaggi (ka॰) aṅgīrasau bhāradvājau vāsaiṭṭhau kassapau bhagu – ‘tyāhaṃ mantai adhiyāmi sācariyakau’ti, tāvatā tvaṃ bhavissasi isi vā isitthāya vā paṭipan‍nauti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

286. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ambaṭṭha, kinti tai sutaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuddhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – yai tai ahaisuṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ pubbakā isayau mantānaṃ kattārau mantānaṃ pavattārau, yaisamidaṃ  aitarahi brāhmaṇā paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samihitaṃ, tadanugāyanti tadanubhāsanti bhāsitamanubhāsanti vācitamanuvācainti, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakau vāmakau vāmadaivau vaissāmittau yamataggi aṅgīrasau bhāradvājau vāsaiṭṭhau kassapau bhagu, aivaṃ su tai sunhātā suvilittā kappitakaisamassū āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalābharaṇā āmuttamālābharaṇā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) audātavatthavasanā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārainti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘…pai॰… aivaṃ su tai sālīnaṃ audanaṃ sucimaṃsūpasaicanaṃ vicitakāḷakaṃ anaikasūpaṃ anaikabyañ‍janaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘…pai॰… aivaṃ su tai vaiṭhakanatapassāhi nārīhi paricārainti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘…pai॰… aivaṃ su tai kuttavālaihi vaḷavārathaihi dīghāhi pataudalaṭṭhīhi vāhanai vitudaintā  vipariyāyanti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘…pai॰… aivaṃ su tai uk‍kiṇṇaparikhāsu aukkhittapalighāsu nagarūpakārikāsu dīghāsivudhaihi dīghāsibaddhaihi (syā॰ pī॰) purisaihi rakkhāpainti, saiyyathāpi tvaṃ aitarahi sācariyakau’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘iti khau, ambaṭṭha, naiva tvaṃ isi na isitthāya paṭipan‍nau sācariyakau| yassa khau pana, ambaṭṭha, mayi kaṅkhā vā vimati vā sau maṃ pañhaina, ahaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina saudhissāmī’’ti|

dvailakkhaṇādassanaṃ

287. atha khau bhagavā vihārā nikkhamma caṅkamaṃ abbhuṭṭhāsi| ambaṭṭhaupi māṇavau vihārā nikkhamma caṅkamaṃ abbhuṭṭhāsi| atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ caṅkamantaṃ anucaṅkamamānau bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni saman‍naisi| addasā khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhagavatau kāyai  dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai pahūtajivhatāya ca|

288. atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘passati khau mai ayaṃ ambaṭṭhau māṇavau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu  mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai pahūtajivhatāya cā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi yathā addasa ambaṭṭhau māṇavau bhagavatau kausauhitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ| atha khau bhagavā jivhaṃ nin‍nāmaitvā ubhaupi kaṇṇasautāni anumasi paṭimasi, ubhaupi nāsikasautāni anumasi paṭimasi, kaivalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṃ jivhāya chādaisi| atha khau ambaṭṭhassa māṇavassa aitadahausi – ‘‘saman‍nāgatau khau samaṇau gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi paripuṇṇaihi, nau aparipuṇṇaihī’’ti| bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhau gautama, gacchāma, bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, ambaṭṭha, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau vaḷavārathamāruyha pak‍kāmi|

289. taina khau pana samayaina brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti uk‍kaṭṭhāya nikkhamitvā mahatā brāhmaṇagaṇaina saddhiṃ sakai ārāmai nisin‍nau hauti ambaṭṭhaṃyaiva māṇavaṃ paṭimānaintau| atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau yaina sakau ārāmau taina pāyāsi| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva yaina brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā brāhmaṇaṃ paukkharasātiṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

290. aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ  brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, tāta ambaṭṭha, addasa taṃ bhavantaṃ gautama’’nti? ‘‘addasāma khau mayaṃ, bhau, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautama’’nti| ‘‘kac‍ci, tāta ambaṭṭha, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ tathā santaṃyaiva  saddau abbhuggatau nau añ‍ñathā; kac‍ci pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau tādisau nau añ‍ñādisau’’ti? ‘‘tathā santaṃyaiva, bhau, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saddau abbhuggatau nau añ‍ñathā, tādisauva sau bhavaṃ gautamau nau añ‍ñādisau| saman‍nāgatau ca sau bhavaṃ gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi paripuṇṇaihi nau aparipuṇṇaihī’’ti| ‘‘ahu pana tai, tāta ambaṭṭha, samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? ‘‘ahu khau mai, bhau, samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tai, tāta ambaṭṭha, ahu samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? atha khau ambaṭṭhau māṇavau yāvatakau yāvatikau (ka॰ pī॰) ahausi bhagavatā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau, taṃ sabbaṃ brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa āraucaisi|

291. aivaṃ vuttai, brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahau vata rai amhākaṃ paṇḍitaka paṇḍitakā, ahau vata rai amhākaṃ bahussutaka bahussutakā, ahau vata rai amhākaṃ taivij‍jaka taivij‍jakā, aivarūpaina kira, bhau, purisau atthacarakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yadaiva khau tvaṃ, ambaṭṭha, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ āsaj‍ja āsaj‍ja avacāsi, atha khau sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhaipi aivaṃ  upanaiyya upanaiyya avaca| ahau vata rai amhākaṃ paṇḍitaka, ahau vata rai amhākaṃ bahussutaka, ahau vata rai amhākaṃ taivij‍jaka, aivarūpaina kira, bhau, purisau atthacarakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti, kupitau sau kupitau (pī॰) anattamanau ambaṭṭhaṃ māṇavaṃ padasāyaiva pavattaisi| icchati ca tāvadaiva bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

paukkharasātibuddhupasaṅkamanaṃ

292. atha  khau tai brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ paukkharasātiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ativikālau khau, bhau, aj‍ja samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| svaidāni dāni svai (sī॰ ka॰) bhavaṃ paukkharasāti samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti| atha khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā yānai āraupaitvā uk‍kāsu dhāriyamānāsu  uk‍kaṭṭhāya niyyāsi, yaina icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍau taina pāyāsi| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi yānaina gantvā, yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

293. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamā nu khau idha, bhau gautama, amhākaṃ antaivāsī ambaṭṭhau māṇavau’’ti? ‘‘āgamā khau tai taidha (syā॰), tai idha (pī॰), brāhmaṇa, antaivāsī ambaṭṭhau māṇavau’’ti| ‘‘ahu pana tai, bhau gautama, ambaṭṭhaina māṇavaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? ‘‘ahu khau mai, brāhmaṇa, ambaṭṭhaina māṇavaina  saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘yathākathaṃ pana tai, bhau gautama, ahu ambaṭṭhaina māṇavaina saddhiṃ kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti? atha khau bhagavā yāvatakau ahausi ambaṭṭhaina māṇavaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau, taṃ sabbaṃ brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bālau, bhau gautama, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau, khamatu bhavaṃ gautamau ambaṭṭhassa māṇavassā’’ti| ‘‘sukhī hautu, brāhmaṇa, ambaṭṭhau māṇavau’’ti|

294. atha  khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni saman‍naisi| addasā khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavatau kāyai dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai pahūtajivhatāya ca|

295. atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘passati khau mai ayaṃ brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇāni yaibhuyyaina ṭhapaitvā dvai| dvīsu mahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati – kausauhitai ca vatthaguyhai, pahūtajivhatāya cā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi yathā addasa brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavatau kausauhitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ| atha khau  bhagavā jivhaṃ nin‍nāmaitvā ubhaupi kaṇṇasautāni  anumasi paṭimasi, ubhaupi nāsikasautāni anumasi paṭimasi, kaivalampi nalāṭamaṇḍalaṃ jivhāya chādaisi|

296. atha khau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa aitadahausi  – ‘‘saman‍nāgatau khau samaṇau gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi paripuṇṇaihi nau aparipuṇṇaihī’’ti| bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau aj‍jatanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

297. atha khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavantaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi, māṇavakāpi bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| atha khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

298. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nassa khau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa  bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ; kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi brāhmaṇaṃ paukkharasātiṃ kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya; aivamaiva brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa tasmiñ‍ñaiva  āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti|

paukkharasātiupāsakattapaṭivaidanā

299. atha khau brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau  aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama| saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhau gautama, saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| yathā ca bhavaṃ gautamau uk‍kaṭṭhāya añ‍ñāni upāsakakulāni upasaṅkamati, aivamaiva bhavaṃ gautamau paukkharasātikulaṃ upasaṅkamatu| tattha yai tai māṇavakā vā māṇavikā vā bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ abhivādaissanti vā pac‍cuṭṭhissanti pac‍cuṭṭhassanti (pī॰) vā āsanaṃ vā udakaṃ vā dassanti cittaṃ vā pasādaissanti, taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| ‘‘kalyāṇaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇā’’ti|

ambaṭṭhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|



4. sauṇadaṇḍasuttaṃ

campaiyyakabrāhmaṇagahapatikā

300. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅgaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi yaina campā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| taina khau pana samayaina sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau campaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ|

301. assausuṃ khau campaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau aṅgaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi campaṃ anuppattau campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ  hautī’’ti| atha  khau campaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā campāya nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī saṅghā saṅghī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gaṇībhūtā yaina gaggarā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkamanti|

302. taina khau pana samayaina sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau uparipāsādai divāsaiyyaṃ upagatau hauti| addasā khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau campaiyyakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai campāya nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī  saṅghai saṅghī (sī॰ pī॰) saṅghā saṅghī (syā॰) gaṇībhūtai yaina gaggarā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkamantai| disvā khattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau khattai, campaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā campāya  nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtā yaina gaggarā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkamantī’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, bhau, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau aṅgaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi campaṃ anuppattau campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| tamaitai bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhau khattai, yaina campaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tainupasaṅkama, upasaṅkamitvā campaiyyakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘sauṇadaṇḍau, bhau, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – āgamaintu kira bhavantau, sauṇadaṇḍaupi brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sau khattā sauṇadaṇḍassa  brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā yaina campaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā campaiyyakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai aitadavauca – ‘‘sauṇadaṇḍau  bhau brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘āgamaintu kira bhavantau, sauṇadaṇḍaupi brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti|

sauṇadaṇḍaguṇakathā

303. taina khau pana samayaina nānāvairaj‍jakānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ pañ‍camattāni brāhmaṇasatāni campāyaṃ paṭivasanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ khau tai brāhmaṇā – ‘‘sauṇadaṇḍau kira brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti| atha khau tai brāhmaṇā yaina sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sauṇadaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, bhau, hauti – ‘ahampi samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissāmī’’’ti|

‘‘mā bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkami| na arahati bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| sacai bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissati, bhautau  sauṇadaṇḍassa yasau hāyissati, samaṇassa gautamassa yasau abhivaḍḍhissati| yampi bhautau sauṇadaṇḍassa yasau hāyissati, samaṇassa gautamassa yasau abhivaḍḍhissati, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ sauṇadaṇḍaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| yampi bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ sauṇadaṇḍaṃ dassanāya  upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau ajjhāyakau, mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī brahmaḍḍhī (sī॰), brahmavac‍casī (pī॰) akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau sīlavā vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau kalyāṇavācau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya anaiḷagalāya (sī॰ pī॰), anailagaḷāya (ka) atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau bahūnaṃ ācariyapācariyau tīṇi māṇavakasatāni mantai vācaiti| bahū khau pana nānādisā nānājanapadā māṇavakā āgacchanti  bhautau sauṇadaṇḍassa santikai mantatthikā mantai adhiyitukāmā  …pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau; samaṇau gautamau taruṇau caiva taruṇapabbajitau ca…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi  sauṇadaṇḍau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi sauṇadaṇḍau campaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ, rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina din‍naṃ, rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| yampi bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau campaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ, rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina din‍naṃ, rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ sauṇadaṇḍaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

buddhaguṇakathā

304. aivaṃ vuttai, sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca –

‘‘taina  hi, bhau, mamapi suṇātha, yathā mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; natvaiva arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā, akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| yampi bhau samaṇau gautamau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā  pitāmahayugā, akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, imināpaṅgaina na arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ; atha khau mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau mahantaṃ ñātisaṅghaṃ auhāya pabbajitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau pahūtaṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ auhāya pabbajitau bhūmigatañ‍ca vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ ca…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau  khalu, bhau, gautamau daharauva samānau yuvā susukāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, brahmavaṇṇī, brahmavacchasī, akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sīlavā ariyasīlī kusalasīlī kusalasīlaina saman‍nāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau kalyāṇavācau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau bahūnaṃ ācariyapācariyau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau khīṇakāmarāgau vigatacāpal‍lau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau kammavādī kiriyavādī apāpapuraikkhārau brahmañ‍ñāya pajāya…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau uc‍cā kulā pabbajitau asambhin‍nakhattiyakulā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau aḍḍhā  kulā pabbajitau mahaddhanā mahābhaugā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ  khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ tirauraṭṭhā tiraujanapadā pañhaṃ pucchituṃ āgacchanti…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ anaikāni daivatāsahassāni pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatāni…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ  khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ ti…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau aihisvāgatavādī sakhilau sammaudakau abbhākuṭikau uttānamukhau pubbabhāsī…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau catun‍naṃ parisānaṃ sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇai khalu, bhau, gautamai bahū daivā ca manussā ca abhippasan‍nā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau yasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā paṭivasati, na tasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā amanussā manussai vihaiṭhainti…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau saṅghī gaṇī gaṇācariyau puthutitthakarānaṃ aggamakkhāyati| yathā khau pana, bhau, aitaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ yathā vā tathā vā yasau samudāgacchati, na haivaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa yasau samudāgatau| atha khau anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya samaṇassa gautamassa yasau samudāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau saputtau sabhariyau saparisau  sāmac‍cau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ  khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau  khalu, bhau, gautamau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau  khalu, bhau, gautamau campaṃ anuppattau, campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| yai khau pana, bhau, kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā amhākaṃ gāmakhaittaṃ āgacchanti atithī nau tai haunti| atithī khau panamhaihi sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā apacaitabbā| yampi, bhau, samaṇau gautamau campaṃ anuppattau campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai, atithimhākaṃ samaṇau gautamau; atithi khau panamhaihi sak‍kātabbau garukātabbau mānaitabbau pūjaitabbau apacaitabbau| imināpaṅgaina na arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| atha khau mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| aittakai khau ahaṃ, bhau, tassa bhautau gautamassa vaṇṇai pariyāpuṇāmi, nau ca khau sau bhavaṃ gautamau aittakavaṇṇau| aparimāṇavaṇṇau hi sau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti|

305. aivaṃ  vuttai, tai brāhmaṇā sauṇadaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yathā khau bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇassa gautamassa vaṇṇai bhāsati itau caipi sau bhavaṃ gautamau yaujanasatai viharati, alamaiva saddhaina kulaputtaina dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ api puṭausainā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhau, sabbaiva mayaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissāmā’’ti|

sauṇadaṇḍaparivitak‍kau

306. atha  khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau mahatā brāhmaṇagaṇaina saddhiṃ yaina gaggarā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkami| atha khau sauṇadaṇḍassa brāhmaṇassa tirauvanasaṇḍagatassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘ahañ‍caiva khau pana samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ; tatra cai maṃ samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na khau aisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau aivaṃ pucchitabbau, aivaṃ nāmaisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau pucchitabbau’ti, taina maṃ ayaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya  – ‘bālau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau abyattau, nāsakkhi samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ yaunisau pañhaṃ pucchitu’nti| yaṃ khau panāyaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha| yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ| yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā| mamañ‍caiva khau pana samaṇau gautamau pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, tassa cāhaṃ pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ na ārādhaiyyaṃ; tatra cai maṃ samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na khau aisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau aivaṃ byākātabbau, aivaṃ nāmaisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau byākātabbau’ti, taina maṃ ayaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya – ‘bālau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau abyattau, nāsakkhi  samaṇassa gautamassa pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaitu’nti| yaṃ khau panāyaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha| yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ| yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā| ahañ‍caiva khau pana aivaṃ samīpagatau samānau adisvāva samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ nivattaiyyaṃ, taina maṃ ayaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya – ‘bālau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau abyattau mānathaddhau bhītau ca, nau visahati samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ, kathañhi nāma aivaṃ samīpagatau samānau adisvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ nivattissatī’ti| yaṃ khau panāyaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha| yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ, yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā’’ti|

307. atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| campaiyyakāpi khau brāhmaṇagahapatikā  appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu; sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

308. tatrapi  sudaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau aitadaiva bahulamanuvitak‍kaintau nisin‍nau hauti – ‘‘ahañ‍caiva khau  pana samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ; tatra cai maṃ samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na khau aisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau aivaṃ pucchitabbau, aivaṃ nāmaisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau pucchitabbau’ti, taina maṃ ayaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya – ‘bālau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau abyattau, nāsakkhi samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ yaunisau pañhaṃ pucchitu’nti| yaṃ khau panāyaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha| yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ| yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā| mamañ‍caiva khau pana samaṇau gautamau pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, tassa cāhaṃ pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina  cittaṃ na ārādhaiyyaṃ; tatra cai maṃ samaṇau gautamau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na khau aisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau aivaṃ byākātabbau, aivaṃ nāmaisa, brāhmaṇa, pañhau byākātabbau’ti, taina maṃ ayaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya – ‘bālau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau abyattau, nāsakkhi samaṇassa gautamassa pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaitu’nti| yaṃ khau panāyaṃ parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha| yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ| yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā| ahau vata maṃ samaṇau gautamau sakai ācariyakai taivij‍jakai pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, addhā vatassāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaiyyaṃ pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇainā’’ti|

brāhmaṇapañ‍ñatti

309. atha khau bhagavatau sauṇadaṇḍassa brāhmaṇassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya aitadahausi – ‘‘vihañ‍ñati khau ayaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau  sakaina cittaina| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sauṇadaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sakai ācariyakai taivij‍jakai pañhaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā sauṇadaṇḍaṃ  brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katihi pana, brāhmaṇa, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti?

310. atha khau sauṇadaṇḍassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃ  vata nau ahausi icchitaṃ, yaṃ ākaṅkhitaṃ, yaṃ adhippaitaṃ, yaṃ abhipatthitaṃ – ‘ahau vata maṃ samaṇau gautamau sakai ācariyakai taivij‍jakai pañhaṃ pucchaiyya, addhā vatassāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaiyyaṃ pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇainā’ti, tatra maṃ samaṇau gautamau sakai ācariyakai taivij‍jakai pañhaṃ pucchati| addhā vatassāhaṃ cittaṃ ārādhaissāmi pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇainā’’ti|

311. atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau abbhun‍nāmaitvā kāyaṃ anuvilaukaitvā parisaṃ bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhau gautama, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; ajjhāyakau hauti mantadharau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau; abhirūpau hauti  dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya; sīlavā hauti vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau; paṇḍitau ca hauti maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhau gautama, pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘imaisaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, pañ‍can‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ sak‍kā aikaṃ aṅgaṃ ṭhapayitvā catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhau gautama| imaisañhi, bhau gautama, pañ‍can‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ṭhapayāma| kiñhi vaṇṇau karissati? yatau khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā  akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; ajjhāyakau ca hauti mantadharau ca tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau; sīlavā ca hauti vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau; paṇḍitau ca hauti maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| imaihi khau bhau gautama catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau  sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

312. ‘‘imaisaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, catun‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ sak‍kā aikaṃ aṅgaṃ ṭhapayitvā tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhau gautama| imaisañhi, bhau gautama, catun‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ mantai ṭhapayāma| kiñhi mantā karissanti? yatau khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; sīlavā ca hauti vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau; paṇḍitau ca hauti maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhau gautama, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘imaisaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, tiṇṇaṃ aṅgānaṃ sak‍kā aikaṃ aṅgaṃ ṭhapayitvā dvīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana  musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhau gautama| imaisañhi, bhau gautama, tiṇṇaṃ aṅgānaṃ jātiṃ ṭhapayāma| kiñhi jāti karissati? yatau khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau sīlavā hauti vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau; paṇḍitau ca hauti  maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| imaihi  khau, bhau gautama, dvīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

313. aivaṃ  vuttai, tai brāhmaṇā sauṇadaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mā bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau aivaṃ avaca, mā bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau aivaṃ avaca| apavadataiva bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau vaṇṇaṃ, apavadati mantai, apavadati jātiṃ aikaṃsaina| bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau samaṇassaiva gautamassa vādaṃ anupakkhandatī’’ti|

314. atha khau bhagavā tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai khau tumhākaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘appassutau ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, akalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, duppañ‍ñau ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, na ca pahauti sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’nti, tiṭṭhatu sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, tumhai mayā saddhiṃ mantavhau asmiṃ vacanai| sacai pana tumhākaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘bahussutau ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, paṇḍitau ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau, pahauti ca sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ asmiṃ vacanai paṭimantaitu’nti, tiṭṭhatha tumhai, sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau mayā saddhiṃ paṭimantaitū’’ti|

315. aivaṃ vuttai, sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu bhavaṃ gautamau, tuṇhī bhavaṃ gautamau hautu, ahamaiva taisaṃ sahadhammaina  paṭivacanaṃ karissāmī’’ti| atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca – ‘‘mā bhavantau aivaṃ avacuttha, mā bhavantau aivaṃ avacuttha – ‘apavadataiva bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau vaṇṇaṃ, apavadati mantai, apavadati jātiṃ aikaṃsaina| bhavaṃ sauṇadaṇḍau  samaṇassaiva gautamassa vādaṃ anupakkhandatī’ti| nāhaṃ, bhau, apavadāmi vaṇṇaṃ vā mantai vā jātiṃ vā’’ti|

316. taina  khau pana samayaina sauṇadaṇḍassa brāhmaṇassa bhāginaiyyau aṅgakau nāma māṇavakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca  – ‘‘passanti nau bhauntau imaṃ aṅgakaṃ māṇavakaṃ amhākaṃ bhāginaiyya’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’| ‘‘aṅgakau khau, bhau, māṇavakau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya, nāssa imissaṃ parisāyaṃ samasamau atthi vaṇṇaina ṭhapaitvā samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ| aṅgakau khau māṇavakau ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau| ahamassa mantai vācaitā| aṅgakau khau māṇavakau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| ahamassa mātāpitarau jānāmi| aṅgakau khau māṇavakau pāṇampi hanaiyya, adin‍nampi ādiyaiyya, paradārampi gacchaiyya, musāvādampi bhaṇaiyya, maj‍jampi pivaiyya, aittha dāni, bhau, kiṃ vaṇṇau karissati, kiṃ mantā, kiṃ jāti? yatau khau, bhau, brāhmaṇau sīlavā ca hauti vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau, paṇḍitau ca hauti maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| imaihi khau, bhau, dvīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

sīlapañ‍ñākathā

317. ‘‘imaisaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, dvin‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ sak‍kā aikaṃ aṅgaṃ ṭhapayitvā aikaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgataṃ brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapaituṃ; ‘brāhmaṇausmī’ti ca vadamānau sammā vadaiyya, na ca pana musāvādaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, bhau gautama| sīlaparidhautā hi, bhau gautama, pañ‍ñā; pañ‍ñāparidhautaṃ sīlaṃ| yattha sīlaṃ tattha pañ‍ñā, yattha pañ‍ñā tattha sīlaṃ| sīlavatau pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñavatau sīlaṃ| sīlapañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca pana laukasmiṃ aggamakkhāyati| saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, hatthaina vā hatthaṃ dhauvaiyya, pādaina vā pādaṃ dhauvaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhau gautama, sīlaparidhautā pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñāparidhautaṃ  sīlaṃ| yattha sīlaṃ tattha pañ‍ñā, yattha pañ‍ñā tattha sīlaṃ| sīlavatau pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñavatau sīlaṃ| sīlapañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca pana laukasmiṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivamaitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sīlaparidhautā hi, brāhmaṇa, pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñāparidhautaṃ sīlaṃ| yattha sīlaṃ tattha pañ‍ñā, yattha pañ‍ñā tattha sīlaṃ| sīlavatau pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñavatau sīlaṃ| sīlapañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca pana laukasmiṃ aggamakkhāyati| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, hatthaina vā hatthaṃ dhauvaiyya, pādaina vā pādaṃ dhauvaiyya; aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, sīlaparidhautā pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñāparidhautaṃ  sīlaṃ| yattha sīlaṃ tattha pañ‍ñā, yattha pañ‍ñā tattha sīlaṃ| sīlavatau pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñavatau sīlaṃ| sīlapañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca pana laukasmiṃ aggamakkhāyati|

318. ‘‘katamaṃ pana taṃ, brāhmaṇa, sīlaṃ? katamā sā pañ‍ñā’’ti? ‘‘aittakaparamāva mayaṃ, bhau gautama, aitasmiṃ atthai| sādhu vata bhavantaṃyaiva gautamaṃ paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇauhi; sādhukaṃ manasikarauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… (yathā 190-212 anucchaidaisu tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti| idaṃ khau taṃ, brāhmaṇa, sīlaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati, abhinin‍nāmaiti| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti, idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya ayaṃ khau sā, brāhmaṇa, pañ‍ñā’’ti|

sauṇadaṇḍaupāsakattapaṭivaidanā

319. aivaṃ vuttai, sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama| saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| adhivāsaitu ca mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

320. atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina sauṇadaṇḍassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi|

321. atha khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau sauṇadaṇḍau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañ‍caiva khau pana, bhau gautama, parisagatau samānau āsanā vuṭṭhahitvā bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ abhivādaiyyaṃ, taina maṃ sā parisā paribhavaiyya| yaṃ khau pana sā parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha| yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ| yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā| ahañ‍caiva khau pana, bhau gautama, parisagatau samānau añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhaiyyaṃ, āsanā mai taṃ bhavaṃ gautamau pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ dhāraitu| ahañ‍caiva  khau pana, bhau gautama, parisagatau samānau vaiṭhanaṃ aumuñ‍caiyyaṃ, sirasā mai taṃ bhavaṃ gautamau abhivādanaṃ dhāraitu| ahañ‍caiva khau pana, bhau gautama, yānagatau samānau yānā pac‍caurauhitvā bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ abhivādaiyyaṃ, taina maṃ sā parisā paribhavaiyya| yaṃ khau pana sā parisā paribhavaiyya, yasaupi tassa hāyaitha, yassa khau pana yasau hāyaitha, bhaugāpi tassa hāyaiyyuṃ| yasauladdhā khau panamhākaṃ bhaugā| ahañ‍caiva khau pana, bhau gautama, yānagatau samānau pataudalaṭṭhiṃ abbhun‍nāmaiyyaṃ, yānā mai taṃ bhavaṃ gautamau pac‍caurauhanaṃ dhāraitu| ahañ‍caiva  khau pana, bhau gautama, yānagatau samānau chattaṃ apanāmaiyyaṃ, sirasā mai taṃ bhavaṃ gautamau abhivādanaṃ dhāraitū’’ti|

322. atha khau bhagavā sauṇadaṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti|

sauṇadaṇḍasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|



5. kūṭadantasuttaṃ

khāṇumatakabrāhmaṇagahapatikā

323. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi yaina khāṇumataṃ nāma magadhānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā khāṇumatai viharati ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau khāṇumataṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa mahāyañ‍ñau upakkhaṭau hauti| satta ca usabhasatāni satta ca vacchatarasatāni satta ca vacchatarīsatāni satta ca ajasatāni satta ca urabbhasatāni thūṇūpanītāni haunti yañ‍ñatthāya|

324. assausuṃ khau khāṇumatakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau magadhaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi khāṇumataṃ anuppattau khāṇumatai viharati ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ  sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

325. atha  khau khāṇumatakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā khāṇumatā nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtā yaina ambalaṭṭhikā tainupasaṅkamanti|

326. taina  khau pana samayaina kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau uparipāsādai divāsaiyyaṃ upagatau hauti| addasā khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau khāṇumatakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai khāṇumatā nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtai yaina ambalaṭṭhikā tainupasaṅkamantai| disvā khattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau khattai, khāṇumatakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā khāṇumatā nikkhamitvā saṅghasaṅghī gaṇībhūtā yaina ambalaṭṭhikā tainupasaṅkamantī’’ti?

327. ‘‘atthi khau, bhau, samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau magadhaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi khāṇumataṃ anuppattau, khāṇumatai viharati ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| tamaitai bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamantī’’ti|

328. atha khau kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘sutaṃ khau pana maitaṃ – ‘samaṇau  gautamau tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ jānātī’ti| na khau panāhaṃ jānāmi tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ| icchāmi cāhaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ  samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ pucchaiyya’’nti|

329. atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau khattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhau khattai, yaina khāṇumatakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tainupasaṅkama| upasaṅkamitvā khāṇumatakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘kūṭadantau, bhau, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘‘āgamaintu kira bhavantau, kūṭadantaupi brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sau khattā kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā yaina khāṇumatakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā khāṇumatakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai aitadavauca – ‘‘kūṭadantau, bhau, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘āgamaintu kira bhauntau, kūṭadantaupi brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’’ti|

kūṭadantaguṇakathā

330. taina  khau pana samayaina anaikāni brāhmaṇasatāni khāṇumatai paṭivasanti – ‘‘kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa mahāyañ‍ñaṃ anubhavissāmā’’ti| assausuṃ khau tai brāhmaṇā – ‘‘kūṭadantau kira brāhmaṇau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti| atha khau tai brāhmaṇā yaina kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu|

331. upasaṅkamitvā kūṭadantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau mai, bhau, hauti – ‘ahampi samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya  upasaṅkamissāmī’’’ti|

‘‘mā bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkami| na arahati bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| sacai bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissati, bhautau kūṭadantassa yasau hāyissati, samaṇassa gautamassa yasau abhivaḍḍhissati| yampi bhautau kūṭadantassa yasau hāyissati, samaṇassa gautamassa yasau abhivaḍḍhissati, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| samaṇau tvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ kūṭadantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| yampi bhavaṃ kūṭadantau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| samaṇau tvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ kūṭadantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau pahūtavittūpakaraṇau pahūtajātarūparajatau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau ajjhāyakau mantadharau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi  kūṭadantau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau  dassanāya…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau sīlavā vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau kalyāṇavācau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau bahūnaṃ ācariyapācariyau tīṇi māṇavakasatāni mantai vācaiti, bahū khau pana nānādisā nānājanapadā māṇavakā āgacchanti bhautau kūṭadantassa santikai mantatthikā mantai adhiyitukāmā…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| samaṇau gautamau taruṇau caiva taruṇapabbajitau ca…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi kūṭadantau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘bhavañhi  kūṭadantau khāṇumataṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ| yampi bhavaṃ kūṭadantau khāṇumataṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ, rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ brahmadaiyyaṃ, imināpaṅgaina na arahati bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| samaṇautvaiva gautamau arahati bhavantaṃ kūṭadantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

buddhaguṇakathā

332. aivaṃ  vuttai kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau tai brāhmaṇai aitadavauca –

‘‘taina  hi, bhau, mamapi suṇātha, yathā mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ  gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ, na tvaiva arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| yampi, bhau, samaṇau gautamau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, imināpaṅgaina na arahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| atha khau mayamaiva arahāma taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau mahantaṃ ñātisaṅghaṃ auhāya pabbajitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau pahūtaṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ auhāya pabbajitau bhūmigatañ‍ca vaihāsaṭṭhaṃ ca…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau daharauva samānau yuvā susukāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya  …pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sīlavā ariyasīlī kusalasīlī kusalasīlaina saman‍nāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau  khalu, bhau, gautamau kalyāṇavācau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya  anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau  khalu, bhau, gautamau bahūnaṃ ācariyapācariyau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau khīṇakāmarāgau vigatacāpal‍lau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau kammavādī kiriyavādī apāpapuraikkhārau brahmañ‍ñāya pajāya…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau uc‍cā kulā pabbajitau asambhin‍nakhattiyakulā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau aḍḍhā kulā pabbajitau mahaddhanā mahābhaugā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ tirauraṭṭhā tiraujanapadā pañhaṃ pucchituṃ āgacchanti…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ anaikāni daivatāsahassāni pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatāni…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ ti…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau aihisvāgatavādī sakhilau sammaudakau abbhākuṭikau uttānamukhau pubbabhāsī…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau catun‍naṃ parisānaṃ sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇai  khalu, bhau, gautamai bahū daivā ca manussā ca abhippasan‍nā…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau yasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā paṭivasati na tasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā amanussā manussai vihaiṭhainti…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau saṅghī gaṇī gaṇācariyau puthutitthakarānaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yathā khau pana, bhau, aitaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ yathā  vā tathā vā yasau samudāgacchati, na  haivaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa yasau samudāgatau| atha khau anuttarāya vij‍jācaraṇasampadāya samaṇassa gautamassa yasau samudāgatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau saputtau sabhariyau saparisau  sāmac‍cau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇaṃ khalu, bhau, gautamaṃ brāhmaṇau paukkharasāti saputtau sabhariyau saparisau sāmac‍cau pāṇaihi saraṇaṃ gatau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau brāhmaṇassa paukkharasātissa sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau…pai॰…

‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau khāṇumataṃ anuppattau khāṇumatai viharati ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ| yai khau pana, bhau, kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā amhākaṃ gāmakhaittaṃ āgacchanti, atithī nau  tai haunti| atithī khau panamhaihi sak‍kātabbā garukātabbā mānaitabbā pūjaitabbā apacaitabbā| yampi, bhau, samaṇau gautamau khāṇumataṃ anuppattau khāṇumatai viharati ambalaṭṭhikāyaṃ, atithimhākaṃ samaṇau gautamau| atithi khau panamhaihi sak‍kātabbau garukātabbau mānaitabbau pūjaitabbau apacaitabbau| imināpaṅgaina nārahati sau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| atha khau mayamaiva arahāma  taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| aittakai khau ahaṃ, bhau, tassa bhautau gautamassa vaṇṇai pariyāpuṇāmi, nau ca khau sau bhavaṃ gautamau aittakavaṇṇau| aparimāṇavaṇṇau hi sau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti|

333. aivaṃ  vuttai, tai brāhmaṇā kūṭadantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yathā khau bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇassa gautamassa vaṇṇai bhāsati, itau caipi sau bhavaṃ gautamau yaujanasatai viharati, alamaiva saddhaina kulaputtaina dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ api puṭausainā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhau, sabbaiva mayaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissāmā’’ti|

mahāvijitarājayañ‍ñakathā

334. atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau mahatā brāhmaṇagaṇaina saddhiṃ yaina ambalaṭṭhikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| khāṇumatakāpi khau brāhmaṇagahapatikā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

335. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama – ‘samaṇau gautamau tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ jānātī’ti| na khau panāhaṃ jānāmi tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ| icchāmi cāhaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ  yajituṃ| sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tividhaṃ yañ‍ñasampadaṃ sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ daisaitū’’ti|

336. ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, brāhmaṇa, rājā mahāvijitau nāma ahausi aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau pahūtajātarūparajatau pahūtavittūpakaraṇau pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñau paripuṇṇakausakauṭṭhāgārau| atha khau, brāhmaṇa, rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘adhigatā khau mai vipulā mānusakā bhaugā, mahantaṃ pathavimaṇḍalaṃ abhivijiya ajjhāvasāmi, yaṃnūnāhaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajaiyyaṃ, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti|

337. ‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa, rājā mahāvijitau purauhitaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ āmantaitvā aitadavauca – ‘idha mayhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – adhigatā khau mai vipulā mānusakā bhaugā, mahantaṃ pathavimaṇḍalaṃ abhivijiya ajjhāvasāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajaiyyaṃ yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| icchāmahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ| anusāsatu maṃ bhavaṃ yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’’ti|

338. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rājānaṃ mahāvijitaṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhautau khau rañ‍ñau janapadau sakaṇṭakau sauppīḷau, gāmaghātāpi dissanti, nigamaghātāpi dissanti, nagaraghātāpi dissanti, panthaduhanāpi dissanti| bhavaṃ khau pana rājā aivaṃ sakaṇṭakai janapadai sauppīḷai balimuddharaiyya, akic‍cakārī assa taina bhavaṃ rājā| siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau aivamassa – ‘‘ahamaitaṃ dassukhīlaṃ vadhaina vā bandhaina vā jāniyā vā garahāya vā pabbājanāya vā samūhanissāmī’’ti, na khau panaitassa dassukhīlassa aivaṃ sammā samugghātau hauti| yai tai hatāvasaisakā bhavissanti, tai pacchā rañ‍ñau janapadaṃ vihaiṭhaissanti| api ca khau idaṃ saṃvidhānaṃ āgamma aivamaitassa dassukhīlassa sammā samugghātau hauti| taina hi bhavaṃ rājā yai bhautau rañ‍ñau janapadai ussahanti kasigaurakkhai, taisaṃ bhavaṃ rājā bījabhattaṃ anuppadaitu| yai bhautau rañ‍ñau janapadai ussahanti vāṇij‍jāya, taisaṃ bhavaṃ rājā pābhataṃ anuppadaitu| yai bhautau rañ‍ñau janapadai ussahanti rājapaurisai, taisaṃ bhavaṃ rājā bhattavaitanaṃ pakappaitu| tai ca manussā sakammapasutā rañ‍ñau janapadaṃ na vihaiṭhaissanti; mahā ca rañ‍ñau rāsikau bhavissati| khaimaṭṭhitā janapadā akaṇṭakā anuppīḷā| manussā mudā maudamānā urai puttai nac‍caintā apārutagharā mañ‍ñai viharissantī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhau’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, rājā mahāvijitau purauhitassa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā yai rañ‍ñau janapadai ussahiṃsu  kasigaurakkhai, taisaṃ rājā mahāvijitau bījabhattaṃ anuppadāsi| yai  ca rañ‍ñau janapadai ussahiṃsu vāṇij‍jāya, taisaṃ rājā mahāvijitau pābhataṃ anuppadāsi| yai ca rañ‍ñau janapadai  ussahiṃsu  rājapaurisai, taisaṃ rājā mahāvijitau bhattavaitanaṃ pakappaisi| tai ca manussā sakammapasutā rañ‍ñau janapadaṃ na vihaiṭhiṃsu, mahā ca rañ‍ñau rāsikau ahausi| khaimaṭṭhitā janapadā akaṇṭakā anuppīḷā manussā mudā maudamānā urai puttai nac‍caintā apārutagharā mañ‍ñai vihariṃsu| atha khau, brāhmaṇa, rājā mahāvijitau purauhitaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ āmantaitvā aitadavauca – ‘samūhatau khau mai bhautau dassukhīlau, bhautau saṃvidhānaṃ āgamma mahā ca mai rāsikau| khaimaṭṭhitā janapadā akaṇṭakā anuppīḷā manussā mudā maudamānā urai puttai nac‍caintā apārutagharā mañ‍ñai viharanti| icchāmahaṃ brāhmaṇa mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ| anusāsatu maṃ bhavaṃ yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti|

catuparikkhāraṃ

339. ‘‘taina hi bhavaṃ rājā yai bhautau rañ‍ñau janapadai khattiyā ānuyantā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca tai bhavaṃ rājā āmantayataṃ – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhau, mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ, anujānantu mai bhavantau yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| yai bhautau rañ‍ñau janapadai amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca…pai॰… brāhmaṇamahāsālā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca…pai॰… gahapatinaicayikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, tai bhavaṃ rājā āmantayataṃ – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhau, mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ, anujānantu mai bhavantau yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhau’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, rājā mahāvijitau purauhitassa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā  yai rañ‍ñau janapadai khattiyā ānuyantā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, tai rājā mahāvijitau āmantaisi  – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhau, mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ, anujānantu mai bhavantau yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| ‘yajataṃ bhavaṃ rājā yañ‍ñaṃ, yañ‍ñakālau mahārājā’ti| yai rañ‍ñau janapadai amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca…pai॰… brāhmaṇamahāsālā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca…pai॰… gahapatinaicayikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, tai rājā mahāvijitau āmantaisi – ‘icchāmahaṃ, bhau, mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajituṃ| anujānantu mai bhavantau yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| ‘yajataṃ bhavaṃ rājā yañ‍ñaṃ, yañ‍ñakālau mahārājā’ti| itimai cattārau anumatipakkhā tassaiva yañ‍ñassa parikkhārā bhavanti|

aṭṭha parikkhārā

340. ‘‘rājā  mahāvijitau aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau, ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya; aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau pahūtajātarūparajatau pahūtavittūpakaraṇau pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñau paripuṇṇakausakauṭṭhāgārau; balavā caturaṅginiyā saināya saman‍nāgatau assavāya auvādapaṭikarāya sahati patapati (sī॰ pī॰), tapati (syā॰) mañ‍ñai pac‍catthikai yasasā; saddhau dāyakau dānapati anāvaṭadvārau samaṇabrāhmaṇakapaṇaddhikavaṇibbakayācakānaṃ aupānabhūtau puñ‍ñāni karauti; bahussutau tassa tassa sutajātassa, tassa tassaiva  khau pana bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau’ti; paṇḍitau, viyattau, maidhāvī, paṭibalau, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nai atthai cintaituṃ| rājā mahāvijitau imaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau| iti imānipi aṭṭhaṅgāni tassaiva yañ‍ñassa parikkhārā bhavanti|

catuparikkhāraṃ

341. ‘‘purauhitau  purauhitaupi (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰) brāhmaṇau catuhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau| ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; ajjhāyakau mantadharau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau; sīlavā vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina  saman‍nāgatau; paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| purauhitau brāhmaṇau imaihi catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau| iti imāni cattāri aṅgāni tassaiva yañ‍ñassa parikkhārā bhavanti|

tissau vidhā

342. ‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa pubbaiva yañ‍ñā tissau vidhā daisaisi| siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ  yiṭṭhukāmassa yiṭṭhakāmassa (ka॰) kaucidaiva vippaṭisārau – ‘mahā vata mai bhaugakkhandhau vigacchissatī’ti, sau bhautā rañ‍ñā vippaṭisārau na karaṇīyau| siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vippaṭisārau – ‘mahā  vata mai bhaugakkhandhau vigacchatī’ti, sau bhautā rañ‍ñā vippaṭisārau na karaṇīyau| siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yiṭṭhassa kaucidaiva vippaṭisārau – ‘mahā vata mai bhaugakkhandhau vigatau’ti, sau bhautā rañ‍ñā vippaṭisārau na karaṇīyau’’ti| imā khau, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa pubbaiva yañ‍ñā tissau vidhā daisaisi|

dasa ākārā

343. ‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa pubbaiva yañ‍ñā dasahākāraihi paṭiggāhakaisu vippaṭisāraṃ paṭivinaisi| ‘āgamissanti khau bhautau yañ‍ñaṃ pāṇātipātinaupi pāṇātipātā paṭiviratāpi| yai tattha pāṇātipātinau, taisañ‍ñaiva taina| yai tattha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā, tai ārabbha yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitu| āgamissanti khau bhautau yañ‍ñaṃ adin‍nādāyinaupi adin‍nādānā paṭiviratāpi…pai॰…  kāmaisu micchācārinaupi kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratāpi… musāvādinaupi musāvādā paṭiviratāpi… pisuṇavācinaupi pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratāpi… pharusavācinaupi pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratāpi… samphappalāpinaupi samphappalāpā  paṭiviratāpi … abhijjhālunaupi anabhijjhālunaupi… byāpan‍nacittāpi abyāpan‍nacittāpi… micchādiṭṭhikāpi sammādiṭṭhikāpi…| yai tattha micchādiṭṭhikā, taisañ‍ñaiva taina| yai tattha sammādiṭṭhikā, tai ārabbha yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitū’ti| imaihi khau, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa pubbaiva yañ‍ñā dasahākāraihi paṭiggāhakaisu vippaṭisāraṃ paṭivinaisi|

sauḷasa ākārā
