344. ‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa sauḷasahākāraihi cittaṃ sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vattā – ‘rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati, nau ca khau tassa āmantitā khattiyā ānuyantā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca; atha ca pana bhavaṃ rājā aivarūpaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajatī’ti| aivampi bhautau rañ‍ñau vattā dhammatau natthi| bhautā khau pana rañ‍ñā āmantitā khattiyā ānuyantā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| imināpaitaṃ bhavaṃ rājā jānātu, yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitu|

‘‘siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vattā – ‘rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati, nau ca khau tassa āmantitā amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca…pai॰… brāhmaṇamahāsālā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca…pai॰… gahapatinaicayikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, atha ca pana bhavaṃ rājā aivarūpaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajatī’ti| aivampi bhautau rañ‍ñau vattā dhammatau natthi| bhautā khau pana rañ‍ñā āmantitā gahapatinaicayikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| imināpaitaṃ bhavaṃ rājā jānātu, yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitu|

‘‘siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vattā – ‘rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati, nau ca khau ubhatau  sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, atha ca pana bhavaṃ rājā aivarūpaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajatī’ti| aivampi bhautau rañ‍ñau vattā dhammatau natthi| bhavaṃ khau pana rājā ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā  akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| imināpaitaṃ bhavaṃ rājā jānātu, yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitu|

‘‘siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vattā – ‘rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati nau ca khau abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau brahmavaṇṇī brahmavacchasī akhuddāvakāsau dassanāya…pai॰… nau ca khau aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau pahūtajātarūparajatau pahūtavittūpakaraṇau pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñau paripuṇṇakausakauṭṭhāgārau…pai॰… nau ca khau balavā caturaṅginiyā saināya saman‍nāgatau assavāya auvādapaṭikarāya sahati mañ‍ñai pac‍catthikai yasasā…pai॰… nau ca khau saddhau dāyakau dānapati anāvaṭadvārau samaṇabrāhmaṇakapaṇaddhikavaṇibbakayācakānaṃ aupānabhūtau puñ‍ñāni karauti…pai॰… nau ca khau bahussutau tassa tassa sutajātassa…pai॰… nau ca khau tassa tassaiva khau pana bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti ‘‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau’’ti…pai॰… nau ca khau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭibalau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nai atthai cintaituṃ, atha ca pana bhavaṃ rājā aivarūpaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajatī’ti| aivampi bhautau rañ‍ñau vattā dhammatau natthi| bhavaṃ khau pana rājā paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭibalau atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nai atthai cintaituṃ| imināpaitaṃ bhavaṃ rājā jānātu, yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitu|

‘‘siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vattā – ‘rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati| nau ca khvassa purauhitau brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau  yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina; atha ca pana bhavaṃ rājā aivarūpaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajatī’ti| aivampi  bhautau rañ‍ñau vattā dhammatau natthi| bhautau khau pana rañ‍ñau purauhitau brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina| imināpaitaṃ bhavaṃ rājā jānātu, yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitu|

‘‘siyā khau pana bhautau rañ‍ñau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa kaucidaiva vattā – ‘rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati| nau ca khvassa purauhitau brāhmaṇau ajjhāyakau mantadharau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ padakau vaiyyākaraṇau laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayau…pai॰… nau ca khvassa purauhitau brāhmaṇau sīlavā vuddhasīlī vuddhasīlaina saman‍nāgatau…pai॰… nau ca khvassa purauhitau brāhmaṇau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ, atha ca pana bhavaṃ rājā  aivarūpaṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajatī’ti| aivampi bhautau rañ‍ñau vattā dhammatau natthi| bhautau khau pana rañ‍ñau purauhitau brāhmaṇau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī paṭhamau vā dutiyau vā sujaṃ paggaṇhantānaṃ| imināpaitaṃ bhavaṃ rājā jānātu, yajataṃ bhavaṃ, saj‍jataṃ bhavaṃ, maudataṃ bhavaṃ, cittamaiva bhavaṃ antaraṃ pasādaitūti| imaihi khau, brāhmaṇa, purauhitau brāhmaṇau rañ‍ñau mahāvijitassa mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajamānassa sauḷasahi ākāraihi cittaṃ sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi|

345. ‘‘tasmiṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñai naiva gāvau hañ‍ñiṃsu, na ajaiḷakā hañ‍ñiṃsu, na kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hañ‍ñiṃsu, na vividhā pāṇā saṃghātaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, na rukkhā chij‍jiṃsu yūpatthāya, na dabbhā lūyiṃsu barihisatthāya parihiṃsatthāya (syā॰ ka॰ sī॰ ka॰), parahiṃsatthāya (ka॰)| yaipissa ahaisuṃ dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā, taipi na daṇḍataj‍jitā na  bhayataj‍jitā na assumukhā rudamānā parikammāni  akaṃsu| atha khau yai icchiṃsu, tai akaṃsu, yai na icchiṃsu, na tai akaṃsu; yaṃ icchiṃsu, taṃ akaṃsu, yaṃ na icchiṃsu, na taṃ akaṃsu| sappitailanavanītadadhimadhuphāṇitaina caiva sau yañ‍ñau niṭṭhānamagamāsi|

346. ‘‘atha  khau, brāhmaṇa, khattiyā ānuyantā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, brāhmaṇamahāsālā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca, gahapatinaicayikā naigamā  caiva jānapadā ca pahūtaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ ādāya rājānaṃ mahāvijitaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idaṃ, daiva, pahūtaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ daivañ‍ñaiva uddissābhataṃ, taṃ daivau paṭiggaṇhātū’ti| ‘alaṃ, bhau, mamāpidaṃ pahūtaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ dhammikaina balinā abhisaṅkhataṃ; tañ‍ca vau hautu, itau ca bhiyyau harathā’ti| tai rañ‍ñā paṭikkhittā aikamantaṃ apak‍kamma aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘na khau aitaṃ amhākaṃ patirūpaṃ, yaṃ mayaṃ imāni sāpataiyyāni punadaiva sakāni gharāni paṭiharaiyyāma| rājā khau mahāvijitau mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajati, handassa mayaṃ anuyāginau haumā’ti|

347. ‘‘atha khau, brāhmaṇa, puratthimaina yañ‍ñavāṭassa yañ‍ñāvāṭassa (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) khattiyā ānuyantā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca dānāni paṭṭhapaisuṃ| dakkhiṇaina yañ‍ñavāṭassa amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca dānāni paṭṭhapaisuṃ| pacchimaina yañ‍ñavāṭassa brāhmaṇamahāsālā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca dānāni paṭṭhapaisuṃ| uttaraina yañ‍ñavāṭassa gahapatinaicayikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca dānāni paṭṭhapaisuṃ|

‘‘taisupi khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñaisu naiva gāvau hañ‍ñiṃsu, na ajaiḷakā hañ‍ñiṃsu, na kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hañ‍ñiṃsu, na vividhā pāṇā saṃghātaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, na rukkhā chij‍jiṃsu yūpatthāya, na dabbhā lūyiṃsu barihisatthāya| yaipi naisaṃ ahaisuṃ dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā, taipi na daṇḍataj‍jitā na bhayataj‍jitā na assumukhā rudamānā parikammāni  akaṃsu| atha khau yai icchiṃsu, tai akaṃsu, yai na icchiṃsu, na tai akaṃsu; yaṃ icchiṃsu, taṃ akaṃsu, yaṃ na icchiṃsu na taṃ akaṃsu| sappitailanavanītadadhimadhuphāṇitaina caiva tai yañ‍ñā niṭṭhānamagamaṃsu|

‘‘iti  cattārau ca anumatipakkhā, rājā mahāvijitau aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau, purauhitau  brāhmaṇau catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau; tissau ca vidhā ayaṃ vuc‍cati brāhmaṇa tividhā yañ‍ñasampadā sauḷasaparikkhārā’’ti|

348. aivaṃ vuttai, tai brāhmaṇā un‍nādinau uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā ahaisuṃ – ‘‘ahau yañ‍ñau, ahau yañ‍ñasampadā’’ti! kūṭadantau pana brāhmaṇau tuṇhībhūtauva nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau tai brāhmaṇā kūṭadantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kasmā pana bhavaṃ kūṭadantau samaṇassa gautamassa subhāsitaṃ subhāsitatau nābbhanumaudatī’’ti? ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhau, samaṇassa gautamassa subhāsitaṃ subhāsitatau nābbhanumaudāmi| muddhāpi tassa vipataiyya, yau samaṇassa gautamassa subhāsitaṃ subhāsitatau nābbhanumaudaiyya| api ca mai, bhau, aivaṃ hauti – samaṇau gautamau na aivamāha – ‘aivaṃ mai suta’nti vā ‘aivaṃ arahati bhavitu’nti vā; api ca samaṇau gautamau – ‘aivaṃ tadā āsi, itthaṃ tadā āsi’ tvaiva bhāsati| tassa mayhaṃ bhau aivaṃ hauti – ‘addhā samaṇau gautamau taina samayaina rājā vā ahausi mahāvijitau yañ‍ñassāmi purauhitau vā brāhmaṇau tassa yañ‍ñassa yājaitā’ti| abhijānāti pana bhavaṃ gautamau aivarūpaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ yajitvā vā  yājaitvā vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jitāti’’? ‘‘abhijānāmahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivarūpaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ yajitvā vā yājaitvā vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jitā, ahaṃ taina samayaina purauhitau brāhmaṇau ahausiṃ tassa yañ‍ñassa yājaitā’’ti|

nic‍cadānaanukulayañ‍ñaṃ

349. ‘‘atthi pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya tividhayañ‍ñasampadāya (ka॰) sauḷasaparikkhārāya appaṭṭhatarau appatthatarau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ca appasamārambhatarau appasamārabbhatarau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘atthi  khau, brāhmaṇa, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana sau, bhau gautama, yañ‍ñau imāya tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya  appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘yāni khau pana tāni, brāhmaṇa, nic‍cadānāni anukulayañ‍ñāni sīlavantai pabbajitai uddissa diyyanti; ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau imāya tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina taṃ nic‍cadānaṃ anukulayañ‍ñaṃ imāya tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya appaṭṭhatarañ‍ca appasamārambhatarañ‍ca mahapphalatarañ‍ca mahānisaṃsatarañ‍cā’’ti?

‘‘na khau, brāhmaṇa, aivarūpaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā| taṃ kissa haitu? dissanti haittha, brāhmaṇa, daṇḍappahārāpi galaggahāpi, tasmā aivarūpaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā| yāni khau pana tāni, brāhmaṇa, nic‍cadānāni anukulayañ‍ñāni sīlavantai pabbajitai uddissa diyyanti; aivarūpaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñaṃ upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā| taṃ kissa haitu? na haittha, brāhmaṇa, dissanti daṇḍappahārāpi galaggahāpi, tasmā aivarūpaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina taṃ nic‍cadānaṃ anukulayañ‍ñaṃ imāya tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya appaṭṭhatarañ‍ca appasamārambhatarañ‍ca mahapphalatarañ‍ca mahānisaṃsatarañ‍cā’’ti|

350. ‘‘atthi pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau  ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘atthi  khau, brāhmaṇa, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau  pana sau, bhau gautama, yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘yau khau, brāhmaṇa, cātuddisaṃ saṅghaṃ uddissa vihāraṃ karauti, ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

351. ‘‘atthi pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘atthi khau, brāhmaṇa, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana sau, bhau gautama, yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘yau khau, brāhmaṇa, pasan‍nacittau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchati, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchati, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchati; ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina appaṭṭhatarau  ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

352. ‘‘atthi  pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca saraṇagamanaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘atthi  khau, brāhmaṇa, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca saraṇagamanaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana sau, bhau gautama, yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca saraṇagamanaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘yau khau, brāhmaṇa, pasan‍nacittau sikkhāpadāni samādiyati – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇiṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiṃ| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau  imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca saraṇagamanaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

353. ‘‘atthi pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca saraṇagamanaihi imaihi ca sikkhāpadaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘atthi khau, brāhmaṇa, añ‍ñau yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca saraṇagamanaihi imaihi ca sikkhāpadaihi appaṭṭhatarau  ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti|

‘‘katamau  pana sau, bhau gautama, yañ‍ñau imāya ca tividhāya yañ‍ñasampadāya sauḷasaparikkhārāya iminā ca nic‍cadānaina anukulayañ‍ñaina iminā ca vihāradānaina imaihi ca  saraṇagamanaihi imaihi ca sikkhāpadaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… (yathā 190-212 anucchaidaisu, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau purimaihi yañ‍ñaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau  ca…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau purimaihi yañ‍ñaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau cāti| ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰… ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau purimaihi yañ‍ñaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau ca…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñau purimaihi yañ‍ñaihi appaṭṭhatarau ca appasamārambhatarau ca mahapphalatarau ca mahānisaṃsatarau ca| imāya ca, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñasampadāya añ‍ñā yañ‍ñasampadā uttaritarā vā paṇītatarā vā natthī’’ti|

kūṭadantaupāsakattapaṭivaidanā

354. aivaṃ vuttai, kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau  pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| aisāhaṃ bhau gautama  satta ca usabhasatāni satta ca vacchatarasatāni satta ca vacchatarīsatāni satta ca ajasatāni satta ca urabbhasatāni muñ‍cāmi, jīvitaṃ daimi, haritāni caiva tiṇāni khādantu, sītāni ca pānīyāni pivantu, sītau ca naisaṃ vātau upavāyatū’’ti|

sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyā

355. atha khau bhagavā kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ, dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ; kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ  naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kūṭadantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā, taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva  kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa tasmiñ‍ñaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti|

356. atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

357. atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai yañ‍ñavāṭai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama; niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

358. atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina kūṭadantassa brāhmaṇassa yañ‍ñavāṭau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi|

atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau  buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau kūṭadantau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kūṭadantaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti|

kūṭadantasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|



6. mahālisuttaṃ

brāhmaṇadūtavatthu

359. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā vaisāliyaṃ paṭivasanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ khau tai kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

360. atha khau tai kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nāgitau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau tai kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā yaināyasmā nāgitau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhau nāgita, aitarahi sau bhavaṃ gautamau viharati? dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ taṃ bhavantaṃ gautama’’nti| ‘‘akālau  khau, āvusau, bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, paṭisal‍līnau bhagavā’’ti| atha khau tai kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā tatthaiva aikamantaṃ  nisīdiṃsu – ‘‘disvāva mayaṃ taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ gamissāmā’’ti|

auṭṭhaddhalicchavīvatthu

361. auṭṭhaddhaupi  licchavī mahatiyā licchavīparisāya saddhiṃ yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā yaināyasmā nāgitau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau auṭṭhaddhaupi licchavī āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhantai nāgita, aitarahi sau bhagavā viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| ‘‘akālau khau, mahāli, bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, paṭisal‍līnau bhagavā’’ti| auṭṭhaddhaupi licchavī tatthaiva aikamantaṃ nisīdi – ‘‘disvāva ahaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ gamissāmi arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti|

362. atha khau sīhau samaṇuddaisau yaināyasmā nāgitau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sīhau samaṇuddaisau āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitai, bhantai kassapa, sambahulā kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā idhūpasaṅkantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya; auṭṭhaddhaupi licchavī mahatiyā licchavīparisāya saddhiṃ idhūpasaṅkantau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, sādhu, bhantai kassapa, labhataṃ aisā janatā bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, sīha, tvañ‍ñaiva bhagavatau āraucaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sīhau samaṇuddaisau āyasmatau nāgitassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau sīhau samaṇuddaisau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitai, bhantai, sambahulā kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā idhūpasaṅkantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, auṭṭhaddhaupi licchavī mahatiyā licchavīparisāya  saddhiṃ idhūpasaṅkantau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| sādhu, bhantai, labhataṃ aisā janatā bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, sīha, vihārapacchāyāyaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sīhau samaṇuddaisau bhagavatau paṭissutvā vihārapacchāyāyaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi|

363. atha  khau bhagavā vihārā nikkhamma vihārapacchāyāyaṃ pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau tai kausalakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā māgadhakā ca brāhmaṇadūtā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| auṭṭhaddhaupi licchavī mahatiyā licchavīparisāya saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

364. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau auṭṭhaddhau licchavī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘purimāni, bhantai, divasāni purimatarāni sunakkhattau licchaviputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘yadaggai ahaṃ, mahāli, bhagavantaṃ upanissāya viharāmi, na ciraṃ tīṇi vassāni, dibbāni hi khau rūpāni passāmi piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau ca khau dibbāni saddāni suṇāmi piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyānī’ti| santānaiva nu khau, bhantai, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau dibbāni saddāni nāssausi  piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, udāhu asantānī’’ti?

aikaṃsabhāvitasamādhi

365. ‘‘santānaiva khau, mahāli, sunakkhattau licchaviputtau dibbāni saddāni nāssausi piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau asantānī’’ti| ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina santānaiva sunakkhattau licchaviputtau dibbāni saddāni nāssausi piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau asantānī’’ti?

366. ‘‘idha, mahāli, bhikkhunau puratthimāya disāya aikaṃsabhāvitau samādhi hauti dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| sau puratthimāya disāya aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| puratthimāya disāya dibbāni rūpāni passati piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau ca khau dibbāni saddāni suṇāti piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, mahāli, hauti bhikkhunau  puratthimāya disāya aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ|

367. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahāli, bhikkhunau dakkhiṇāya disāya…pai॰… pacchimāya  disāya … uttarāya disāya… uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ aikaṃsabhāvitau samādhi hauti dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| sau uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ dibbāni rūpāni passati piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau ca khau dibbāni saddāni suṇāti  piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, mahāli, hauti bhikkhunau uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ|

368. ‘‘idha, mahāli, bhikkhunau puratthimāya disāya aikaṃsabhāvitau samādhi hauti dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| sau puratthimāya disāya aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| puratthimāya disāya dibbāni saddāni suṇāti piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau ca khau dibbāni rūpāni passati piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, mahāli, hauti bhikkhunau puratthimāya disāya aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ|

369. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahāli, bhikkhunau dakkhiṇāya disāya…pai॰… pacchimāya disāya… uttarāya disāya… uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ aikaṃsabhāvitau samādhi  hauti dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| sau uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ dibbāni saddāni suṇāti piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau ca khau dibbāni rūpāni passati piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, mahāli, hauti bhikkhunau uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ  aikaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānaṃ saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, nau ca khau dibbānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ|

370. ‘‘idha, mahāli, bhikkhunau puratthimāya disāya ubhayaṃsabhāvitau samādhi hauti dibbānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ dibbānañ‍ca  saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| sau puratthimāya disāya ubhayaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, dibbānañ‍ca saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| puratthimāya disāya dibbāni ca rūpāni passati piyarūpāni  kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, dibbāni ca saddāni suṇāti piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, mahāli, hauti bhikkhunau puratthimāya disāya ubhayaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ dibbānañ‍ca saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ|

371. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahāli, bhikkhunau dakkhiṇāya disāya…pai॰… pacchimāya disāya… uttarāya disāya… uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ ubhayaṃsabhāvitau samādhi hauti dibbānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, dibbānañ‍ca saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| sau uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ ubhayaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ dibbānañ‍ca saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ dibbāni ca rūpāni passati piyarūpāni  kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, dibbāni ca saddāni suṇāti piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, mahāli, hauti bhikkhunau uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ ubhayaṃsabhāvitai samādhimhi dibbānañ‍ca rūpānaṃ dassanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, dibbānañ‍ca saddānaṃ savanāya piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ| ayaṃ khau mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina santānaiva sunakkhattau licchaviputtau dibbāni saddāni nāssausi piyarūpāni kāmūpasaṃhitāni rajanīyāni, nau asantānī’’ti|

372. ‘‘aitāsaṃ nūna, bhantai, samādhibhāvanānaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, mahāli, aitāsaṃ samādhibhāvanānaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu  bhikkhū mayi  brahmacariyaṃ caranti| atthi khau, mahāli, añ‍ñaiva dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca, yaisaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti|

catuariyaphalaṃ

373. ‘‘katamai pana tai, bhantai, dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca, yaisaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti? ‘‘idha, mahāli, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| ayampi khau, mahāli, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau  ca, yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahāli, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva sakiṃdaiva (ka॰) imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| ayampi khau, mahāli, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca, yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahāli, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti, tattha parinibbāyī, anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayampi khau, mahāli, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca, yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, mahāli, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, mahāli, dhammau uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca, yassa sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ caranti| imai khau tai, mahāli, dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca, yaisaṃ sacchikiriyāhaitu bhikkhū mayi brahmacariyaṃ carantī’’ti|

ariyaaṭṭhaṅgikamaggau

374. ‘‘atthi pana, bhantai, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘atthi khau, mahāli, maggau atthi paṭipadā aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

375. ‘‘katamau  pana, bhantai, maggau katamā paṭipadā aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāyā’’ti? ‘‘ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, mahāli, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya|

dvaipabbajitavatthu

376. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, mahāli, samayaṃ kausambiyaṃ viharāmi ghausitārāmai| atha khau dvai pabbajitā – muṇḍiyau ca paribbājakau jāliyau ca dārupattikantaivāsī yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| upasaṅkamitvā mayā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tai dvai pabbajitā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau gautama, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, udāhu añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti?

377. ‘‘‘taina hāvusau, suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasi karautha bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘aivamāvusau’ti khau tai dvai pabbajitā mama pac‍cassausuṃ| ahaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – idhāvusau tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… (yathā 190-212 anucchaidaisu aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā  ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti? yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti? yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā…pai॰… ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰… yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti? yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ na kal‍laṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti| yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti? yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati na kal‍laṃ tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau auṭṭhaddhau licchavī bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahālisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|



7. jāliyasuttaṃ

dvaipabbajitavatthu

378. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina dvai pabbajitā – muṇḍiyau ca paribbājakau jāliyau ca dārupattikantaivāsī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tai dvai pabbajitā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau gautama, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, udāhu añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti?

379. ‘‘taina hāvusau, suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai dvai pabbajitā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāvusau, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ, sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… (yathā 190-212 anucchaidaisu aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti? yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati kal‍laṃ, tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā…pai॰… ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ  abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰… yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati kal‍laṃ tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā…pai॰…|

380. …pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti| yau khau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, kal‍laṃ nu khau tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti? yau sau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivaṃ jānāti aivaṃ passati, na kal‍laṃ tassaitaṃ vacanāya – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vāti| ahaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ jānāmi aivaṃ passāmi| atha ca panāhaṃ na vadāmi – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai dvai pabbajitā bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

jāliyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|



8. mahāsīhanādasuttaṃ

acailakassapavatthu

381. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruñ‍ñāyaṃ ujuñ‍ñāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) viharati kaṇṇakatthalai migadāyai| atha khau acailau kassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ aikaṃsaina upak‍kausati upavadatī’ti| yai tai, bhau gautama, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ aikaṃsaina upak‍kausati upavadatī’ti, kac‍ci tai bhautau gautamassa vuttavādinau, na ca bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati? anabbhakkhātukāmā hi mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautama’’nti|

382. ‘‘yai tai, kassapa, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ aikaṃsaina upak‍kausati upavadatī’ti, na mai tai vuttavādinau, abbhācikkhanti ca pana maṃ tai asatā abhūtaina| idhāhaṃ, kassapa, aikac‍caṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ passāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| idha panāhaṃ, kassapa, aikac‍caṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ passāmi dibbaina cakkhunā  visuddhaina  atik‍kantamānusakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ|

383. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, kassapa, aikac‍caṃ tapassiṃ appadukkhavihāriṃ passāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ  nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| idha panāhaṃ, kassapa, aikac‍caṃ tapassiṃ appadukkhavihāriṃ passāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ| yauhaṃ, kassapa, imaisaṃ tapassīnaṃ aivaṃ āgatiñ‍ca gatiñ‍ca cutiñ‍ca upapattiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmi, sauhaṃ kiṃ sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahissāmi, sabbaṃ vā tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ aikaṃsaina upak‍kausissāmi upavadissāmi?

384. ‘‘santi, kassapa, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā paṇḍitā nipuṇā kataparappavādā vālavaidhirūpā| tai bhindantā mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni| taihipi mai saddhiṃ aikac‍caisu ṭhānaisu samaiti, aikac‍caisu ṭhānaisu na samaiti| yaṃ tai aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘sādhū’ti, mayampi taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘sādhū’ti| yaṃ tai aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘na sādhū’ti, mayampi taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘na sādhū’ti| yaṃ tai aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘sādhū’ti, mayaṃ taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘na sādhū’ti| yaṃ tai aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘na sādhū’ti, mayaṃ taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘sādhū’ti|

‘‘yaṃ mayaṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘sādhū’ti, paraipi taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘sādhū’ti| yaṃ  mayaṃ  aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘na sādhū’ti, paraipi taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘na sādhū’ti| yaṃ mayaṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘na sādhū’ti, parai taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘sādhū’ti| yaṃ mayaṃ aikac‍caṃ vadaima ‘sādhū’ti, parai taṃ aikac‍caṃ vadanti ‘na sādhū’ti|

samanuyuñ‍jāpanakathā

385. ‘‘tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – yaisu nau, āvusau, ṭhānaisu na samaiti, tiṭṭhantu tāni ṭhānāni| yaisu ṭhānaisu samaiti, tattha viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantaṃ samanugāhantaṃ samanubhāsantaṃ satthārā vā satthāraṃ saṅghaina vā saṅghaṃ – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā, sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā, asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā, na alamariyā na alamariyasaṅkhātā, kaṇhā kaṇhasaṅkhātā| kau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ pahāya vattati, samaṇau vā gautamau, parai vā pana bhauntau gaṇācariyā’ti?

386. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā, sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā, asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā, na alamariyā na alamariyasaṅkhātā, kaṇhā kaṇhasaṅkhātā| samaṇau gautamau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ pahāya vattati, yaṃ vā pana bhauntau parai gaṇācariyā’ti| itiha, kassapa, viñ‍ñū  samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā amhaiva tattha yaibhuyyaina pasaṃsaiyyuṃ|

387. ‘‘aparampi nau, kassapa, viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantaṃ samanugāhantaṃ samanubhāsantaṃ satthārā vā satthāraṃ saṅghaina vā saṅghaṃ – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ  dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā, anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā, saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā, alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā, suk‍kā suk‍kasaṅkhātā| kau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ samādāya vattati, samaṇau vā gautamau, parai vā pana bhauntau gaṇācariyā’ ti?

388. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ  – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā, anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā, saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā, alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā, suk‍kā suk‍kasaṅkhātā| samaṇau gautamau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ samādāya vattati, yaṃ vā pana bhauntau parai gaṇācariyā’ti| itiha, kassapa, viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā amhaiva tattha yaibhuyyaina pasaṃsaiyyuṃ|

389. ‘‘aparampi nau, kassapa, viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantaṃ samanugāhantaṃ samanubhāsantaṃ satthārā vā satthāraṃ saṅghaina vā saṅghaṃ – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā, sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā, asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā, na alamariyā na alamariyasaṅkhātā, kaṇhā kaṇhasaṅkhātā| kau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ pahāya vattati, gautamasāvakasaṅghau vā, parai vā pana bhauntau gaṇācariyasāvakasaṅghā’ti?

390. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā, sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā, asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā, na alamariyā na alamariyasaṅkhātā, kaṇhā kaṇhasaṅkhātā| gautamasāvakasaṅghau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ pahāya vattati, yaṃ vā pana bhauntau parai gaṇācariyasāvakasaṅghā’ti| itiha, kassapa, viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā amhaiva tattha yaibhuyyaina pasaṃsaiyyuṃ|

391. ‘‘aparampi  nau, kassapa, viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantaṃ samanugāhantaṃ  samanubhāsantaṃ satthārā vā satthāraṃ saṅghaina vā saṅghaṃ| ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā, anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā, saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā, alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā, suk‍kā suk‍kasaṅkhātā| kau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ samādāya vattati, gautamasāvakasaṅghau vā, parai vā pana bhauntau gaṇācariyasāvakasaṅghā’ti?

392. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘yai imaisaṃ bhavataṃ dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā, anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā, saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā, alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā, suk‍kā suk‍kasaṅkhātā| gautamasāvakasaṅghau imai dhammai anavasaisaṃ samādāya vattati, yaṃ vā pana bhauntau parai gaṇācariyasāvakasaṅghā’ti| itiha, kassapa, viñ‍ñū samanuyuñ‍jantā samanugāhantā samanubhāsantā amhaiva tattha yaibhuyyaina pasaṃsaiyyuṃ|

ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau

393. ‘‘atthi, kassapa, maggau atthi paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nau sāmaṃyaiva ñassati sāmaṃ dakkhati dakkhiti (sī॰) – ‘samaṇauva gautamau kālavādī  bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī’ti| katamau ca, kassapa, maggau, katamā ca paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nau sāmaṃyaiva ñassati sāmaṃ dakkhati – ‘samaṇauva gautamau kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī’ti? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, kassapa, maggau, ayaṃ paṭipadā, yathāpaṭipan‍nau sāmaṃyaiva ñassati sāmaṃ dakkhati ‘samaṇauva gautamau kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī’’’ti|

tapaupak‍kamakathā

394. aivaṃ  vuttai, acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imaipi khau, āvusau gautama, tapaupak‍kamā aitaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ  sāmañ‍ñasaṅkhātā ca brahmañ‍ñasaṅkhātā ca| acailakau  hauti, muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau, na aihibhaddantikau, na tiṭṭhabhaddantikau, nābhihaṭaṃ, na uddissakataṃ, na nimantanaṃ sādiyati| sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na aiḷakamantaraṃ, na daṇḍamantaraṃ, na musalamantaraṃ, na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ, na gabbhiniyā, na pāyamānāya, na purisantaragatāya, na saṅkittīsu, na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti, na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī, na macchaṃ, na maṃsaṃ, na suraṃ, na mairayaṃ, na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau…pai॰… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau; aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti… sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti; aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvīhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti| iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati|

395. ‘‘imaipi khau, āvusau gautama, tapaupak‍kamā aitaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sāmañ‍ñasaṅkhātā ca brahmañ‍ñasaṅkhātā ca| sākabhakkhau vā hauti, sāmākabhakkhau vā hauti, nīvārabhakkhau vā hauti, daddulabhakkhau vā hauti, haṭabhakkhau vā hauti, kaṇabhakkhau vā hauti, ācāmabhakkhau vā hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhau vā hauti, tiṇabhakkhau vā hauti, gaumayabhakkhau vā hauti, vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī|

396. ‘‘imaipi khau, āvusau gautama, tapaupak‍kamā aitaisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ sāmañ‍ñasaṅkhātā ca brahmañ‍ñasaṅkhātā ca| sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinampi  dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, vāḷakambalampi dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhikampi dhāraiti, kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ubbhaṭṭhakaupi  ubbhaṭṭhikaupi (ka॰) hauti āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti, phalakasaiyyampi kappaiti, thaṇḍilasaiyyampi kappaiti, aikapassayikaupi hauti rajaujal‍ladharau, abbhaukāsikaupi hauti yathāsanthatikau, vaikaṭikaupi hauti vikaṭabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau, apānakaupi hauti apānakattamanuyuttau, sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharatī’’ti|

tapaupak‍kamaniratthakathā

397. ‘‘acailakau  caipi, kassapa, hauti, muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau…pai॰… iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| tassa cāyaṃ sīlasampadā cittasampadā pañ‍ñāsampadā abhāvitā hauti asacchikatā| atha khau sau ārakāva sāmañ‍ñā ārakāva brahmañ‍ñā| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipi|

‘‘sākabhakkhau caipi, kassapa, hauti, sāmākabhakkhau…pai॰… vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| tassa cāyaṃ sīlasampadā cittasampadā pañ‍ñāsampadā abhāvitā hauti asacchikatā| atha khau sau ārakāva sāmañ‍ñā ārakāva brahmañ‍ñā| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipi|

‘‘sāṇāni caipi, kassapa, dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti…pai॰… sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau  viharati| tassa cāyaṃ sīlasampadā cittasampadā pañ‍ñāsampadā abhāvitā hauti asacchikatā| atha khau sau ārakāva sāmañ‍ñā ārakāva brahmañ‍ñā| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipī’’ti|

398. aivaṃ vuttai, acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘duk‍karaṃ, bhau gautama, sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’’nti| ‘‘pakati khau aisā, kassapa, laukasmiṃ ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti| acailakau caipi, kassapa, hauti, muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau…pai॰… iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| imāya ca, kassapa, mattāya iminā tapaupak‍kamaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vā abhavissa brahmañ‍ñaṃ vā duk‍karaṃ suduk‍karaṃ, naitaṃ abhavissa kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti|

‘‘sak‍kā  ca panaitaṃ abhavissa kātuṃ gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā antamasau kumbhadāsiyāpi – ‘handāhaṃ acailakau haumi, muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau…pai॰… iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharāmī’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatraiva imāya mattāya añ‍ñatra iminā tapaupak‍kamaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vā hauti brahmañ‍ñaṃ vā  duk‍karaṃ suduk‍karaṃ, tasmā aitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipi|

‘‘sākabhakkhau caipi, kassapa, hauti, sāmākabhakkhau…pai॰… vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| imāya ca, kassapa, mattāya iminā tapaupak‍kamaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vā abhavissa brahmañ‍ñaṃ vā duk‍karaṃ suduk‍karaṃ, naitaṃ abhavissa kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti|

‘‘sak‍kā ca panaitaṃ abhavissa kātuṃ gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā antamasau kumbhadāsiyāpi – ‘handāhaṃ sākabhakkhau vā haumi, sāmākabhakkhau vā…pai॰… vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaimi pavattaphalabhaujī’ti|

‘‘yasmā  ca khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatraiva imāya mattāya añ‍ñatra iminā tapaupak‍kamaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vā hauti brahmañ‍ñaṃ vā duk‍karaṃ suduk‍karaṃ, tasmā aitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipi|

‘‘sāṇāni caipi, kassapa, dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti…pai॰… sāyatatiyakampi  udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| imāya ca, kassapa, mattāya iminā tapaupak‍kamaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ  vā abhavissa brahmañ‍ñaṃ vā duk‍karaṃ suduk‍karaṃ, naitaṃ abhavissa kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti|

‘‘sak‍kā ca panaitaṃ abhavissa kātuṃ gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā antamasau kumbhadāsiyāpi – ‘handāhaṃ sāṇānipi dhāraimi, masāṇānipi dhāraimi…pai॰… sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharāmī’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatraiva imāya mattāya añ‍ñatra iminā tapaupak‍kamaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vā hauti brahmañ‍ñaṃ vā duk‍karaṃ suduk‍karaṃ, tasmā aitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duk‍karaṃ sāmañ‍ñaṃ duk‍karaṃ brahmañ‍ña’nti| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipī’’ti|

399. aivaṃ  vuttai, acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘duj‍jānau, bhau gautama, samaṇau, duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’’ti| ‘‘pakati khau aisā, kassapa, laukasmiṃ ‘duj‍jānau samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti| acailakau caipi, kassapa, hauti, muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau…pai॰… iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| imāya ca, kassapa, mattāya iminā tapaupak‍kamaina samaṇau vā abhavissa  brāhmaṇau vā duj‍jānau suduj‍jānau, naitaṃ abhavissa kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duj‍jānau samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā ca panaisau abhavissa ñātuṃ gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā antamasau kumbhadāsiyāpi – ‘ayaṃ acailakau hauti, muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau…pai॰… iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharatī’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatraiva imāya mattāya añ‍ñatra iminā tapaupak‍kamaina samaṇau vā hauti brāhmaṇau vā duj‍jānau suduj‍jānau, tasmā aitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duj‍jānau samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti| yatau khau yatau ca khau (ka॰), kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipi|

‘‘sākabhakkhau caipi, kassapa, hauti sāmākabhakkhau…pai॰… vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| imāya ca, kassapa, mattāya iminā tapaupak‍kamaina samaṇau vā abhavissa  brāhmaṇau vā duj‍jānau suduj‍jānau, naitaṃ abhavissa kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duj‍jānau samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā ca panaisau abhavissa ñātuṃ gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā antamasau kumbhadāsiyāpi – ‘ayaṃ sākabhakkhau vā hauti sāmākabhakkhau…pai॰… vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatraiva imāya mattāya añ‍ñatra iminā tapaupak‍kamaina samaṇau vā hauti brāhmaṇau vā duj‍jānau suduj‍jānau, tasmā aitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duj‍jānau samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipi|

‘‘sāṇāni  caipi, kassapa, dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti…pai॰… sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| imāya ca, kassapa, mattāya iminā tapaupak‍kamaina samaṇau vā abhavissa brāhmaṇau vā duj‍jānau suduj‍jānau, naitaṃ abhavissa kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duj‍jānau samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā ca panaisau abhavissa ñātuṃ gahapatinā vā gahapatiputtaina vā antamasau kumbhadāsiyāpi – ‘ayaṃ sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti…pai॰… sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharatī’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatraiva imāya mattāya añ‍ñatra iminā tapaupak‍kamaina  samaṇau vā hauti brāhmaṇau vā duj‍jānau suduj‍jānau, tasmā aitaṃ kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘duj‍jānau  samaṇau duj‍jānau brāhmaṇau’ti| yatau khau, kassapa, bhikkhu avairaṃ abyāpaj‍jaṃ maittacittaṃ bhāvaiti, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, kassapa, bhikkhu samaṇau itipi brāhmaṇau itipī’’ti|

sīlasamādhipañ‍ñāsampadā

400. aivaṃ vuttai, acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā pana sā, bhau gautama, sīlasampadā, katamā cittasampadā, katamā pañ‍ñāsampadā’’ti? ‘‘idha, kassapa, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ, sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… (yathā 190-193 anucchaidaisu, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ) bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, kāyakammavacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau kusalaina parisuddhājīvau sīlasampan‍nau indriyaisu guttadvārau satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau santuṭṭhau|

401. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, kassapa, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, kassapa, bhikkhu pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| idampissa hauti sīlasampadāya  …pai॰… (yathā 194 yāva 210 anucchaidaisu)

‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti| saiyyathidaṃ – santikammaṃ paṇidhikammaṃ…pai॰… (yathā 211 anucchaidai) ausadhīnaṃ patimaukkhau iti vā iti, aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasampadāya|

‘‘sa khau sau ayaṃ khau (ka॰), kassapa, bhikkhu aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ sīlasaṃvaratau| saiyyathāpi, kassapa, rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau nihatapac‍cāmittau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ pac‍catthikatau| aivamaiva khau, kassapa, bhikkhu aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ sīlasaṃvaratau| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivaṃ khau, kassapa, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ khau, kassapa, sīlasampadā…pai॰…  paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampissa hauti cittasampadāya…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampissa hauti cittasampadāya| ayaṃ khau, kassapa, cittasampadā|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰…  idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāsampadāya…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti…pai॰… idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāsampadāya| ayaṃ khau, kassapa, pañ‍ñāsampadā|

‘‘imāya  ca, kassapa, sīlasampadāya cittasampadāya pañ‍ñāsampadāya añ‍ñā sīlasampadā cittasampadā pañ‍ñāsampadā uttaritarā vā paṇītatarā vā natthi|

sīhanādakathā

402. ‘‘santi, kassapa, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sīlavādā| tai anaikapariyāyaina sīlassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yāvatā, kassapa, ariyaṃ paramaṃ sīlaṃ, nāhaṃ tattha attanau samasamaṃ samanupassāmi, kutau bhiyyau! atha khau ahamaiva  tattha bhiyyau, yadidaṃ adhisīlaṃ|

‘‘santi, kassapa, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā tapaujigucchāvādā| tai anaikapariyāyaina tapaujigucchāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yāvatā, kassapa, ariyā paramā  tapaujigucchā, nāhaṃ tattha attanau samasamaṃ samanupassāmi, kutau bhiyyau! atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau, yadidaṃ adhijaigucchaṃ|

‘‘santi, kassapa, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā pañ‍ñāvādā| tai anaikapariyāyaina pañ‍ñāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yāvatā, kassapa, ariyā paramā pañ‍ñā, nāhaṃ tattha attanau samasamaṃ samanupassāmi, kutau bhiyyau! atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau, yadidaṃ adhipañ‍ñaṃ|

‘‘santi, kassapa, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā vimuttivādā| tai anaikapariyāyaina vimuttiyā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yāvatā, kassapa, ariyā paramā vimutti, nāhaṃ tattha attanau samasamaṃ samanupassāmi, kutau bhiyyau! atha khau ahamaiva tattha bhiyyau, yadidaṃ adhivimutti|

403. ‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘sīhanādaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau nadati, tañ‍ca khau suñ‍ñāgārai nadati, nau parisāsū’ti| tai – ‘mā haiva’ntissu vacanīyā| ‘sīhanādañ‍ca samaṇau gautamau nadati, parisāsu ca nadatī’ti aivamassu, kassapa, vacanīyā|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘sīhanādañ‍ca samaṇau gautamau nadati, parisāsu ca nadati, nau  ca khau visāradau nadatī’ti| tai – ‘mā haiva’ntissu vacanīyā| ‘sīhanādañ‍ca samaṇau gautamau nadati, parisāsu ca nadati, visāradau ca nadatī’’ti aivamassu, kassapa, vacanīyā|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, kassapa, vij‍jati, yaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘sīhanādañ‍ca samaṇau gautamau nadati, parisāsu ca nadati, visāradau ca nadati, nau ca khau naṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti…pai॰… pañhañ‍ca naṃ pucchanti; nau ca khau naisaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti…pai॰… pañhañ‍ca naisaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti; nau ca khau pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiti…pai॰… pañhassa ca vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiti; nau ca khau sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti…pai॰… sautabbañ‍cassa mañ‍ñanti; nau ca khau sutvā pasīdanti…pai॰… sutvā cassa pasīdanti; nau ca khau pasan‍nākāraṃ karaunti…pai॰… pasan‍nākārañ‍ca karaunti; nau ca khau tathattāya paṭipaj‍janti…pai॰… tathattāya ca paṭipaj‍janti; nau ca khau paṭipan‍nā ārādhaintī’ti| tai – ‘mā haiva’ntissu vacanīyā| ‘sīhanādañ‍ca samaṇau gautamau nadati, parisāsu ca nadati, visāradau ca nadati, pañhañ‍ca naṃ pucchanti, pañhañ‍ca naisaṃ puṭṭhau byākarauti, pañhassa ca vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiti, sautabbañ‍cassa mañ‍ñanti, sutvā cassa pasīdanti, pasan‍nākārañ‍ca karaunti, tathattāya ca paṭipaj‍janti, paṭipan‍nā ca ārādhaintī’ti aivamassu, kassapa, vacanīyā|

titthiyaparivāsakathā

404. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, kassapa, samayaṃ rājagahai viharāmi gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| tatra maṃ añ‍ñatarau tapabrahmacārī  nigraudhau nāma adhijaigucchai  pañhaṃ apucchi| tassāhaṃ adhijaigucchai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau byākāsiṃ| byākatai ca pana mai attamanau ahausi paraṃ viya mattāyā’’ti| ‘‘kau hi, bhantai, bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā na attamanau assa paraṃ viya mattāya? ahampi hi, bhantai, bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā attamanau paraṃ viya mattāya| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

405. ‘‘yau  khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, sau cattārau māsai parivasati, catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti, upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| api ca maittha puggalavaimattatā viditā’’ti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhanti pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhanti upasampadaṃ, cattārau māsai parivasanti, catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājainti, upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| ahaṃ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi, catun‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājaintu, upasampādaintu bhikkhubhāvāyā’’ti|

alattha  khau acailau kassapau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau  khau panāyasmā kassapau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau na cirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti – abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā kassapau arahataṃ ahausīti|

mahāsīhanādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|



9. pauṭṭhapādasuttaṃ

pauṭṭhapādaparibbājakavatthu

406. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau samayappavādakai tindukācīrai aikasālakai mal‍likāya ārāmai paṭivasati mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ tiṃsamattaihi paribbājakasataihi| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi|

407. atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samayappavādakau tindukācīrau aikasālakau mal‍likāya ārāmau, yaina pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā yaina samayappavādakau tindukācīrau aikasālakau mal‍likāya ārāmau tainupasaṅkami|

408. taina khau pana samayaina pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ nisin‍nau hauti un‍nādiniyā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddāya anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintiyā| saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ  itthikathaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ iti vā|

409. addasā khau pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ  dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvāna sakaṃ parisaṃ saṇṭhapaisi – ‘‘appasaddā bhauntau hauntu, mā bhauntau saddamakattha| ayaṃ samaṇau gautamau  āgacchati| appasaddakāmau khau sau āyasmā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādī| appaiva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai tai paribbājakā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

410. atha  khau bhagavā yaina pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau, bhantai, bhagavā| svāgataṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau| cirassaṃ khau, bhantai, bhagavā imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi, yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīdatu, bhantai, bhagavā, idaṃ āsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti|

nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| pauṭṭhapādaupi khau paribbājakau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau pauṭṭhapādaṃ paribbājakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kāya nuttha kāya nauttha (syā॰ ka॰), pauṭṭhapāda, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

abhisañ‍ñāniraudhakathā

411. aivaṃ vuttai pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tiṭṭhataisā, bhantai, kathā, yāya mayaṃ aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā| naisā, bhantai, kathā bhagavatau dul‍labhā bhavissati pacchāpi savanāya| purimāni, bhantai, divasāni purimatarāni, nānātitthiyānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ kautūhalasālāya san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ abhisañ‍ñāniraudhai  kathā udapādi – ‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhau, abhisañ‍ñāniraudhau hautī’ti? tatraikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘ahaitū appac‍cayā purisassa sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jantipi nirujjhantipi| yasmiṃ samayai uppaj‍janti, sañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hauti| yasmiṃ samayai nirujjhanti, asañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hautī’ti| itthaikai abhisañ‍ñāniraudhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘na khau pana maitaṃ na khau nāmaitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bhau, aivaṃ bhavissati| sañ‍ñā hi, bhau, purisassa attā| sā ca khau upaitipi apaitipi| yasmiṃ samayai upaiti, sañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hauti| yasmiṃ samayai apaiti, asañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hautī’ti| itthaikai abhisañ‍ñāniraudhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

‘‘tamañ‍ñau  aivamāha – ‘na khau pana maitaṃ, bhau, aivaṃ bhavissati| santi hi, bhau, samaṇabrāhmaṇā mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā| tai imassa purisassa sañ‍ñaṃ upakaḍḍhantipi apakaḍḍhantipi| yasmiṃ samayai upakaḍḍhanti, sañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai  hauti| yasmiṃ samayai apakaḍḍhanti, asañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hautī’ti| itthaikai abhisañ‍ñāniraudhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

‘‘tamañ‍ñau aivamāha – ‘na khau pana maitaṃ, bhau, aivaṃ bhavissati| santi hi, bhau, daivatā mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā| tā imassa purisassa sañ‍ñaṃ upakaḍḍhantipi apakaḍḍhantipi| yasmiṃ samayai upakaḍḍhanti, sañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hauti| yasmiṃ samayai apakaḍḍhanti, asañ‍ñī tasmiṃ samayai hautī’ti| itthaikai abhisañ‍ñāniraudhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva ārabbha sati udapādi – ‘ahau nūna bhagavā, ahau nūna sugatau, yau imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sukusalau’ti| bhagavā, bhantai, kusalau, bhagavā pakatañ‍ñū abhisañ‍ñāniraudhassa| kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, abhisañ‍ñāniraudhau hautī’’ti?

sahaitukasañ‍ñuppādaniraudhakathā

412. ‘‘tatra, pauṭṭhapāda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ahaitū appac‍cayā purisassa sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jantipi nirujjhantipī’ti, āditauva taisaṃ aparaddhaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? sahaitū hi, pauṭṭhapāda, sappac‍cayā purisassa sañ‍ñā  uppaj‍jantipi nirujjhantipi| sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati’’|

413. ‘‘kā ca sikkhā’’ti? bhagavā avauca – ‘‘idha, pauṭṭhapāda, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ, sammāsambuddhau…pai॰…  (yathā 190-212 anucchaidaisu, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| aivaṃ khau, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… tassimai pañ‍canīvaraṇai pahīnai  attani samanupassatau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi, vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi, savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā kāmasañ‍ñā, sā nirujjhati| vivaikajapītisukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ samayai hauti, vivaikajapītisukhasukhuma-sac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayaṃ sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā vivaikajapītisukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā, sā nirujjhati| samādhijapītisukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ  samayai hauti, samādhijapītisukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayampi sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā samādhijapītisukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā, sā nirujjhati| upaikkhāsukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ samayai hauti, upaikkhāsukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayampi sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā upaikkhāsukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā, sā nirujjhati| adukkhamasukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ samayai hauti, adukkhamasukhasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayampi sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā  nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau  ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā rūpasañ‍ñā purimasañ‍ñā (ka॰), sā nirujjhati| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ samayai hauti, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayampi sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā, sā nirujjhati| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ samayai hauti, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayampi sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa yā purimā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā, sā nirujjhati| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñā tasmiṃ samayai hauti, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasukhumasac‍casañ‍ñīyaiva tasmiṃ samayai hauti| aivampi sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jati, sikkhā aikā sañ‍ñā nirujjhati| ayampi sikkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

414. ‘‘yatau khau, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu idha sakasañ‍ñī hauti, sau tatau  amutra tatau amutra anupubbaina sañ‍ñaggaṃ phusati| tassa sañ‍ñaggai ṭhitassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘caitayamānassa mai pāpiyau, acaitayamānassa mai saiyyau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana caitaiyyaṃ, abhisaṅkharaiyyaṃ, imā ca mai sañ‍ñā nirujjhaiyyuṃ, añ‍ñā ca auḷārikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ na caiva caitaiyyaṃ na ca abhisaṅkharaiyya’nti| sau na caiva caitaiti, na ca abhisaṅkharauti| tassa acaitayatau anabhisaṅkharautau tā caiva sañ‍ñā nirujjhanti, añ‍ñā ca auḷārikā sañ‍ñā na uppaj‍janti| sau niraudhaṃ phusati| aivaṃ khau, pauṭṭhapāda, anupubbābhisañ‍ñāniraudha-sampajāna-samāpatti hauti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, api nu tai itau pubbai aivarūpā anupubbābhisañ‍ñāniraudha-sampajāna-samāpatti sutapubbā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yatau khau, pauṭṭhapāda, bhikkhu idha sakasañ‍ñī hauti, sau tatau amutra tatau amutra anupubbaina sañ‍ñaggaṃ phusati, tassa sañ‍ñaggai ṭhitassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘‘caitayamānassa  mai pāpiyau, acaitayamānassa mai saiyyau| ahañ‍caiva khau pana caitaiyyaṃ  abhisaṅkharaiyyaṃ, imā ca mai sañ‍ñā nirujjhaiyyuṃ, añ‍ñā ca auḷārikā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ na caiva caitaiyyaṃ, na ca abhisaṅkharaiyya’’nti| sau na caiva caitaiti, na cābhisaṅkharauti, tassa acaitayatau anabhisaṅkharautau tā caiva sañ‍ñā nirujjhanti, añ‍ñā ca auḷārikā sañ‍ñā na uppaj‍janti| sau niraudhaṃ phusati| aivaṃ khau, pauṭṭhapāda, anupubbābhisañ‍ñāniraudha-sampajāna-samāpatti hautī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, pauṭṭhapādā’’ti|

415. ‘‘aikañ‍ñaiva nu khau, bhantai, bhagavā sañ‍ñaggaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti, udāhu puthūpi sañ‍ñaggai pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti? ‘‘aikampi khau ahaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, sañ‍ñaggaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi, puthūpi sañ‍ñaggai pañ‍ñapaimī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā aikampi sañ‍ñaggaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti, puthūpi sañ‍ñaggai pañ‍ñapaitī’’ti? ‘‘yathā yathā khau, pauṭṭhapāda, niraudhaṃ phusati, tathā tathāhaṃ sañ‍ñaggaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, aikampi sañ‍ñaggaṃ pañ‍ñapaimi, puthūpi sañ‍ñaggai pañ‍ñapaimī’’ti|

416. ‘‘sañ‍ñā nu khau, bhantai, paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati, pacchā ñāṇaṃ, udāhu ñāṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati, pacchā sañ‍ñā, udāhu sañ‍ñā ca ñāṇañ‍ca apubbaṃ acarimaṃ uppaj‍jantī’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñā khau, pauṭṭhapāda, paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati, pacchā ñāṇaṃ, sañ‍ñuppādā ca pana ñāṇuppādau hauti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘idappac‍cayā kira mai ñāṇaṃ udapādī’ti| iminā khau aitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ – yathā sañ‍ñā paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati, pacchā ñāṇaṃ, sañ‍ñuppādā ca pana ñāṇuppādau hautī’’ti|

sañ‍ñāattakathā

417. ‘‘sañ‍ñā nu khau, bhantai, purisassa attā, udāhu añ‍ñā sañ‍ñā añ‍ñau attā’’ti? ‘‘kaṃ pana tvaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, attānaṃ pac‍caisī’’ti? ‘‘auḷārikaṃ  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, attānaṃ pac‍caimi rūpiṃ cātumahābhūtikaṃ kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkha’’nti kabaḷīkārabhakkhanti (syā॰ ka॰)| ‘‘auḷārikau ca hi tai, pauṭṭhapāda, attā abhavissa rūpī cātumahābhūtikau kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhau| aivaṃ santaṃ khau tai, pauṭṭhapāda, añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā| tadamināpaitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ  yathā añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā| tiṭṭhataiva sāyaṃ tiṭṭhataivāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), pauṭṭhapāda, auḷārikau attā rūpī cātumahābhūtikau kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhau, atha imassa purisassa añ‍ñā ca sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, añ‍ñā ca sañ‍ñā nirujjhanti| iminā khau aitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā’’ti|

418. ‘‘manaumayaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, attānaṃ pac‍caimi sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriya’’nti| ‘‘manaumayau ca hi tai, pauṭṭhapāda, attā abhavissa sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgī ahīnindriyau, aivaṃ santampi khau tai, pauṭṭhapāda, añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā| tadamināpaitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā| tiṭṭhataiva sāyaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, manaumayau attā sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgī ahīnindriyau, atha imassa purisassa añ‍ñā ca sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, añ‍ñā ca sañ‍ñā nirujjhanti| imināpi khau aitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā’’ti|

419. ‘‘arūpiṃ  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, attānaṃ pac‍caimi sañ‍ñāmaya’’nti| ‘‘arūpī ca hi tai, pauṭṭhapāda, attā abhavissa sañ‍ñāmayau, aivaṃ santampi khau tai, pauṭṭhapāda, añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā| tadamināpaitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā| tiṭṭhataiva sāyaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, arūpī attā sañ‍ñāmayau, atha imassa purisassa añ‍ñā ca sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, añ‍ñā ca sañ‍ñā nirujjhanti| imināpi khau aitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā bhavissati añ‍ñau attā’’ti|

420. ‘‘sak‍kā panaitaṃ, bhantai, mayā ñātuṃ – ‘sañ‍ñā purisassa attā’ti vā ‘añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā añ‍ñau attāti vā’ti? ‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau aitaṃ aivaṃ (ka॰), pauṭṭhapāda, tayā añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrāyaugaina añ‍ñatrācariyakaina – ‘sañ‍ñā purisassa attā’ti vā, ‘añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā añ‍ñau attāti vā’’’ti|

‘‘sacai  taṃ, bhantai, mayā duj‍jānaṃ añ‍ñadiṭṭhikaina añ‍ñakhantikaina añ‍ñarucikaina añ‍ñatrāyaugaina añ‍ñatrācariyakaina – ‘sañ‍ñā purisassa attā’ti vā, ‘añ‍ñāva sañ‍ñā añ‍ñau attā’ti vā; ‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti? abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā – ‘sassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’nti|

‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhantai, ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘aitampi khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā abyākataṃ – ‘asassatau laukau, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, ‘antavā laukau…pai॰… ‘anantavā laukau … ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ… ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ… ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘aitampi khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā abyākataṃ – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’’nti|

‘‘kasmā panaitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā abyākata’’nti? ‘‘na haitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, atthasaṃhitaṃ na dhammasaṃhitaṃ  nādibrahmacariyakaṃ, na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, tasmā aitaṃ mayā abyākata’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavatā byākata’’nti? ‘‘idaṃ dukkhanti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā byākataṃ| ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā byākataṃ| ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā byākataṃ| ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā byākata’’nti|

‘‘kasmā panaitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā byākata’’nti? ‘‘aitañhi, pauṭṭhapāda, atthasaṃhitaṃ, aitaṃ dhammasaṃhitaṃ, aitaṃ ādibrahmacariyakaṃ, aitaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati; tasmā aitaṃ mayā byākata’’nti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ, sugata| yassadāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

421. atha  khau tai paribbājakā acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau pauṭṭhapādaṃ paribbājakaṃ samantatau vācā vācāya (syā॰ ka॰) san‍nitaudakaina sañjhabbharimakaṃsu – ‘‘aivamaiva panāyaṃ bhavaṃ pauṭṭhapādau yañ‍ñadaiva samaṇau gautamau bhāsati, taṃ tadaivassa abbhanumaudati – ‘aivamaitaṃ bhagavā aivamaitaṃ, sugatā’ti| na khau pana mayaṃ kiñ‍ci kañ‍ci (pī॰) samaṇassa gautamassa aikaṃsikaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘antavā laukau’ti vā, ‘anantavā laukau’ti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti vā, ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti  vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau tai paribbājakai aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhau, na kiñ‍ci samaṇassa gautamassa aikaṃsikaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘sassatau laukau’ti vā, ‘asassatau laukau’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā; api ca samaṇau gautamau bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ tathaṃ paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti dhammaṭṭhitataṃ dhammaniyāmataṃ| bhūtaṃ khau pana tacchaṃ tathaṃ paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñapaintassa dhammaṭṭhitataṃ dhammaniyāmataṃ, kathañhi nāma mādisau viñ‍ñū samaṇassa gautamassa subhāsitaṃ subhāsitatau nābbhanumaudaiyyā’’ti?

cittahatthisāriputtapauṭṭhapādavatthu
