422. atha khau dvīhatīhassa ac‍cayaina cittau ca hatthisāriputtau pauṭṭhapādau ca paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā cittau hatthisāriputtau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| pauṭṭhapādau  pana paribbājakau bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tadā maṃ, bhantai, tai paribbājakā acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau samantatau vācāsan‍nitaudakaina sañjhabbharimakaṃsu – ‘aivamaiva panāyaṃ bhavaṃ pauṭṭhapādau yañ‍ñadaiva samaṇau  gautamau bhāsati, taṃ tadaivassa abbhanumaudati – ‘aivamaitaṃ bhagavā aivamaitaṃ sugatā’’ti| na khau pana mayaṃ kiñ‍ci samaṇassa gautamassa aikaṃsikaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma – ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘antavā laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘anantavā laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti vā, ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti vā, ‘‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā, ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā’ti| aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, tai paribbājakai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘ahampi khau, bhau, na kiñ‍ci samaṇassa gautamassa aikaṃsikaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘‘sassatau laukau’’ti vā, ‘‘asassatau laukau’’ti vā…pai॰… ‘‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti vā; api ca samaṇau gautamau bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ tathaṃ paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti dhammaṭṭhitataṃ dhammaniyāmataṃ| bhūtaṃ  khau pana tacchaṃ tathaṃ paṭipadaṃ pañ‍ñapaintassa dhammaṭṭhitataṃ dhammaniyāmataṃ, kathañhi nāma mādisau viñ‍ñū samaṇassa gautamassa subhāsitaṃ subhāsitatau nābbhanumaudaiyyā’’ti?

423. ‘‘sabbaiva khau aitai, pauṭṭhapāda, paribbājakā andhā acakkhukā; tvaṃyaiva naisaṃ aikau cakkhumā| aikaṃsikāpi hi khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā; anaikaṃsikāpi hi khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā|

‘‘katamai ca tai, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā anaikaṃsikā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā? ‘sassatau laukau’ti laukauti vā (sī॰ ka॰) khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā anaikaṃsikau dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau; ‘asassatau laukau’ti laukauti vā (sī॰ ka॰) khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā anaikaṃsikau dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau; ‘antavā laukau’ti laukauti vā (sī॰ ka॰) khau pauṭṭhapāda…pai॰… ‘anantavā laukau’ti laukauti vā (sī॰ ka॰) khau pauṭṭhapāda… ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’nti khau pauṭṭhapāda… ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’nti khau pauṭṭhapāda… ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau pauṭṭhapāda… na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau pauṭṭhapāda… ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau pauṭṭhapāda… ‘naiva  hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā anaikaṃsikau dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau|

‘‘kasmā ca tai, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā anaikaṃsikā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā? na haitai, pauṭṭhapāda, atthasaṃhitā na dhammasaṃhitā na ādibrahmacariyakā na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattanti| tasmā tai mayā anaikaṃsikā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā’’|

aikaṃsikadhammau

424. ‘‘katamai ca tai, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā aikaṃsikā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā? idaṃ dukkhanti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā aikaṃsikau  dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau| ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā aikaṃsikau dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau| ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā aikaṃsikau dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau| ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti khau, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā aikaṃsikau dhammau daisitau pañ‍ñattau|

‘‘kasmā ca tai, pauṭṭhapāda, mayā aikaṃsikā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā? aitai, pauṭṭhapāda, atthasaṃhitā, aitai dhammasaṃhitā, aitai ādibrahmacariyakā aitai nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattanti| tasmā tai mayā aikaṃsikā dhammā daisitā pañ‍ñattā|

425. ‘‘santi, pauṭṭhapāda, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti? tai cai mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharathā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aikaṃ vā rattiṃ aikaṃ vā divasaṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā rattiṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā divasaṃ aikantasukhiṃ attānaṃ sañ‍jānāthā’ti  sampajānāthāti (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰)? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau  jānātha – ‘‘ayaṃ maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’’’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau yā tā daivatā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā, tāsaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ saddaṃ suṇātha – ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nāttha, mārisā, ujuppaṭipan‍nāttha, mārisā, aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāya; mayampi hi, mārisā, aivaṃpaṭipan‍nā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

426. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, pauṭṭhapāda, purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ yā imasmiṃ janapadai janapadakalyāṇī, taṃ icchāmi taṃ kāmaimī’ti| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ icchasi kāmaisi, jānāsi taṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ khattiyī vā brāhmaṇī vā vaissī vā suddī vā’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ icchasi kāmaisi, jānāsi taṃ janapadakalyāṇiṃ aivaṃnāmā aivaṃgauttāti vā, dīghā vā rassā vā majjhimā vā kāḷī vā sāmā vā maṅguracchavī vāti, amukasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā nagarai vā’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi, taṃ tvaṃ icchasi kāmaisī’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘āmā’ti vadaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, pauṭṭhapāda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau  – ‘‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? tai cai mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharathā’ti? iti  puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aikaṃ vā rattiṃ aikaṃ vā divasaṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā rattiṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā divasaṃ aikantasukhiṃ attānaṃ sañ‍jānāthā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau jānātha – ‘‘ayaṃ maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau yā tā daivatā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā, tāsaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ saddaṃ suṇātha – ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nāttha, mārisā, ujuppaṭipan‍nāttha, mārisā, aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāya; mayampi hi, mārisā, aivaṃpaṭipan‍nā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’’’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ  sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

427. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, pauṭṭhapāda, purisau cātumahāpathai nissaiṇiṃ karaiyya pāsādassa ārauhaṇāya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yassa tvaṃ yaṃ tvaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) pāsādassa ārauhaṇāya nissaiṇiṃ karausi, jānāsi taṃ pāsādaṃ puratthimāya vā disāya dakkhiṇāya vā disāya pacchimāya vā disāya uttarāya vā disāya uc‍cau vā nīcau vā majjhimau vā’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘nau’ti vadaiyya| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yaṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi, tassa tvaṃ pāsādassa ārauhaṇāya nissaiṇiṃ karausī’ti? iti puṭṭhau ‘āmā’ti vadaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, pauṭṭhapāda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti? tai cai mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharathā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aikaṃ  vā rattiṃ aikaṃ vā divasaṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā rattiṃ upaḍḍhaṃ vā divasaṃ aikantasukhiṃ attānaṃ sañ‍jānāthā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau jānātha ayaṃ maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘nau’ti vadanti|

‘‘tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau yā tā daivatā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ saddaṃ suṇātha- ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nāttha, mārisā, ujuppaṭipan‍nāttha, mārisā, aikantasukhassa laukassa sacchikiriyāya; mayampi hi, mārisā, aivaṃ paṭipan‍nā aikantasukhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti? iti puṭṭhā ‘‘nau’’ti vadanti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai taisaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ appāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

tayau attapaṭilābhā

428. ‘‘tayau khau mai, pauṭṭhapāda, attapaṭilābhā – auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau, manaumayau attapaṭilābhau, arūpau attapaṭilābhau| katamau ca, pauṭṭhapāda, auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau? rūpī cātumahābhūtikau kabaḷīkārāhārabhakkhau kabaḷīkārabhakkhau (syā॰ ka॰), ayaṃ auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau| katamau manaumayau attapaṭilābhau? rūpī manaumayau sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgī ahīnindriyau, ayaṃ manaumayau attapaṭilābhau| katamau arūpau attapaṭilābhau? arūpī sañ‍ñāmayau, ayaṃ arūpau attapaṭilābhau|

429. ‘‘auḷārikassapi  khau ahaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, attapaṭilābhassa pahānāya  dhammaṃ daisaimi – yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathāti| siyā khau pana tai, pauṭṭhapāda, aivamassa – saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati, dukkhau ca khau vihārauti, na khau panaitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| saṃkilaisikā caiva dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā ca dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati, pāmuj‍jaṃ caiva bhavissati pīti ca passaddhi ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca sukhau ca vihārau|

430. ‘‘manaumayassapi khau ahaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, attapaṭilābhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaimi yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathāti| siyā khau pana tai, pauṭṭhapāda, aivamassa – ‘saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati, dukkhau ca khau vihārau’ti, na khau panaitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| saṃkilaisikā caiva dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā ca dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati, pāmuj‍jaṃ caiva bhavissati pīti ca passaddhi ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca sukhau ca vihārau|

431. ‘‘arūpassapi khau ahaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, attapaṭilābhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaimi yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathāti| siyā khau pana tai, pauṭṭhapāda, aivamassa – ‘saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati, dukkhau ca khau vihārau’ti, na khau panaitaṃ, pauṭṭhapāda, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| saṃkilaisikā caiva dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā ca dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati, pāmuj‍jaṃ caiva bhavissati pīti ca passaddhi ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca sukhau ca vihārau|

432. ‘‘parai cai, pauṭṭhapāda, amhai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamau pana sau, āvusau, auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau, yassa tumhai pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitha, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti, taisaṃ mayaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaraiyyāma – ‘ayaṃ vā sau, āvusau, auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau, yassa mayaṃ pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaima, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti|

433. ‘‘parai cai, pauṭṭhapāda, amhai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamau pana sau, āvusau, manaumayau attapaṭilābhau, yassa tumhai pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitha, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā  pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti? taisaṃ mayaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaraiyyāma – ‘ayaṃ vā sau, āvusau, manaumayau attapaṭilābhau yassa mayaṃ pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaima, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti|

434. ‘‘parai cai, pauṭṭhapāda, amhai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamau pana sau, āvusau, arūpau attapaṭilābhau, yassa tumhai pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitha, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti, taisaṃ mayaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhā  aivaṃ byākaraiyyāma – ‘ayaṃ vā sau, āvusau, arūpau attapaṭilābhau yassa mayaṃ pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaima, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai sappāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai sappāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

435. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, pauṭṭhapāda, purisau nissaiṇiṃ karaiyya pāsādassa ārauhaṇāya tassaiva pāsādassa haiṭṭhā| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, yassa tvaṃ pāsādassa ārauhaṇāya nissaiṇiṃ karausi, jānāsi taṃ pāsādaṃ, puratthimāya vā disāya dakkhiṇāya vā disāya pacchimāya vā disāya uttarāya vā disāya uc‍cau vā nīcau vā majjhimau vā’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ayaṃ vā sau, āvusau, pāsādau, yassāhaṃ ārauhaṇāya nissaiṇiṃ karaumi, tassaiva pāsādassa haiṭṭhā’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa sappāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai tassa purisassa sappāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

436. ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, pauṭṭhapāda, parai cai amhai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamau pana sau, āvusau, auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau…pai॰… katamau pana sau, āvusau, manaumayau attapaṭilābhau…pai॰… katamau pana sau, āvusau, arūpau attapaṭilābhau, yassa tumhai pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaitha, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti, taisaṃ mayaṃ aivaṃ puṭṭhā aivaṃ byākaraiyyāma – ‘ayaṃ vā sau, āvusau, arūpau attapaṭilābhau, yassa mayaṃ pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaima, yathāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ vau saṃkilaisikā dhammā pahīyissanti, vaudāniyā dhammā abhivaḍḍhissanti, pañ‍ñāpāripūriṃ  vaipul‍lattañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, pauṭṭhapāda, nanu aivaṃ santai sappāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘addhā khau, bhantai, aivaṃ santai sappāṭihīrakataṃ bhāsitaṃ sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

437. aivaṃ vuttai cittau hatthisāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yasmiṃ, bhantai, samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau hauti, maughassa tasmiṃ samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhau hauti, maughau arūpau attapaṭilābhau hauti; auḷārikau vāssa attapaṭilābhau tasmiṃ samayai sac‍cau hauti| yasmiṃ, bhantai, samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhau hauti, maughassa tasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau hauti, maughau arūpau attapaṭilābhau hauti; manaumayau vāssa attapaṭilābhau tasmiṃ samayai sac‍cau hauti| yasmiṃ, bhantai, samayai arūpau attapaṭilābhau hauti, maughassa tasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau hauti, maughau manaumayau attapaṭilābhau hauti; arūpau vāssa attapaṭilābhau tasmiṃ samayai sac‍cau hautī’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, citta, samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ  gacchati, na arūpau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; auḷārikau attapaṭilābhautvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati| yasmiṃ, citta, samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhau hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na arūpau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; manaumayau attapaṭilābhautvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati| yasmiṃ, citta, samayai arūpau attapaṭilābhau hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na manaumayau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; arūpau attapaṭilābhautvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

438. ‘‘sacai  taṃ, citta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘ahausi tvaṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, na tvaṃ nāhausi; bhavissasi tvaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, na tvaṃ na bhavissasi; atthi tvaṃ  aitarahi, na tvaṃ natthī’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, citta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti?

‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘ahausi tvaṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, na tvaṃ na ahausi; bhavissasi tvaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, na tvaṃ na bhavissasi; atthi tvaṃ aitarahi, na tvaṃ natthī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘ahausāhaṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, nāhaṃ na ahausiṃ; bhavissāmahaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, nāhaṃ na bhavissāmi; atthāhaṃ aitarahi, nāhaṃ natthī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sacai pana taṃ, citta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘yau tai ahausi atītau attapaṭilābhau, sauva svaiva (sī॰ pī॰), sauyaiva (syā॰) tai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau anāgatau, maughau pac‍cuppan‍nau? yau yau vā (pī॰) tai bhavissati anāgatau attapaṭilābhau, sauva  tai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau atītau, maughau pac‍cuppan‍nau? yau yau vā (pī॰) tai aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍nau attapaṭilābhau, sauva sau ca (ka॰) tai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau atītau, maughau anāgatau’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, citta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti?

‘‘sacai pana maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘yau tai ahausi  atītau attapaṭilābhau, sauva tai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau anāgatau, maughau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yau tai bhavissati anāgatau attapaṭilābhau, sauva tai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau atītau, maughau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yau tai aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍nau attapaṭilābhau, sauva tai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau atītau, maughau anāgatau’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘yau mai ahausi atītau attapaṭilābhau, sauva mai attapaṭilābhau tasmiṃ samayai sac‍cau ahausi, maughau anāgatau, maughau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yau mai bhavissati anāgatau attapaṭilābhau, sauva mai attapaṭilābhau tasmiṃ samayai sac‍cau bhavissati, maughau atītau, maughau pac‍cuppan‍nau| yau mai aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍nau attapaṭilābhau, sauva mai attapaṭilābhau sac‍cau, maughau atītau, maughau anāgatau’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

439. ‘‘aivamaiva  khau, citta, yasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na arūpau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati| auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau tvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati| yasmiṃ, citta, samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhau  hauti…pai॰… yasmiṃ, citta, samayai arūpau attapaṭilābhau hauti, naiva  tasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na manaumayau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; arūpau attapaṭilābhau tvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

440. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, citta, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau| yasmiṃ samayai khīraṃ hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai dadhīti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na navanītanti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na sappīti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na sappimaṇḍauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; khīraṃ tvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati| yasmiṃ samayai dadhi hauti…pai॰… navanītaṃ hauti… sappi hauti… sappimaṇḍau hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai khīranti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na dadhīti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na navanītanti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na sappīti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; sappimaṇḍau tvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati| aivamaiva  khau, citta, yasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhau hauti…pai॰… yasmiṃ, citta, samayai manaumayau attapaṭilābhau hauti…pai॰… yasmiṃ, citta, samayai arūpau attapaṭilābhau hauti, naiva tasmiṃ samayai auḷārikau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati, na manaumayau attapaṭilābhauti saṅkhaṃ gacchati; arūpau attapaṭilābhau tvaiva tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhaṃ gacchati| imā khau citta, laukasamañ‍ñā laukaniruttiyau laukavauhārā laukapañ‍ñattiyau, yāhi tathāgatau vauharati aparāmasa’’nti|

441. aivaṃ vuttai, pauṭṭhapādau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti| aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

cittahatthisāriputtaupasampadā

442. cittau  pana hatthisāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai; abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti| aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ  saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

443. alattha khau cittau hatthisāriputtau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panāyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau na cirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti – abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā cittau hatthisāriputtau arahataṃ ahausīti|

pauṭṭhapādasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|



10. subhasuttaṃ

subhamāṇavavatthu

444. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai aciraparinibbutai bhagavati| taina khau pana samayaina subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina|

445. atha khau subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau añ‍ñataraṃ māṇavakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, māṇavaka, yaina samaṇau ānandau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina samaṇaṃ ānandaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhavantaṃ ānandaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira bhavaṃ ānandau yaina subhassa māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti|

446. ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sau māṇavakau subhassa māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa paṭissutvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau māṇavakau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhavantaṃ ānandaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘sādhu kira bhavaṃ ānandau yaina subhassa māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa  nivaisanaṃ  tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti|

447. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau taṃ māṇavakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘akālau khau, māṇavaka| atthi mai aj‍ja bhaisaj‍jamattā pītā| appaivanāma svaipi upasaṅkamaiyyāma kālañ‍ca samayañ‍ca upādāyā’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sau māṇavakau āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā subhaṃ māṇavaṃ taudaiyyaputtaṃ aitadavauca, ‘‘avaucumhā khau mayaṃ bhautau vacanaina taṃ bhavantaṃ ānandaṃ – ‘subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau bhavantaṃ ānandaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati, aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘‘sādhu kira bhavaṃ ānandau yaina subhassa māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhau, samaṇau ānandau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘akālau khau, māṇavaka| atthi mai aj‍ja bhaisaj‍jamattā pītā| appaivanāma svaipi upasaṅkamaiyyāma kālañ‍ca samayañ‍ca upādāyā’ti| aittāvatāpi khau, bhau, katamaiva aitaṃ, yatau khau sau bhavaṃ ānandau aukāsamakāsi svātanāyapi upasaṅkamanāyā’’ti|

448. atha khau āyasmā ānandau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya caitakaina bhikkhunā pacchāsamaṇaina yaina subhassa māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi|

atha khau subhau  māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau subhau māṇavau taudaiyyaputtau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhavañhi  ānandau tassa bhautau gautamassa dīgharattaṃ upaṭṭhākau santikāvacarau samīpacārī| bhavamaitaṃ ānandau jānaiyya, yaisaṃ sau bhavaṃ gautamau dhammānaṃ vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisi| katamaisānaṃ khau, bhau ānanda, dhammānaṃ sau bhavaṃ gautamau vaṇṇavādī ahausi; kattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisī’’ti?

449. ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ khau, māṇava, khandhānaṃ sau bhagavā vaṇṇavādī ahausi; aittha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisi| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? ariyassa sīlakkhandhassa, ariyassa samādhikkhandhassa, ariyassa pañ‍ñākkhandhassa| imaisaṃ khau, māṇava, tiṇṇaṃ khandhānaṃ sau bhagavā vaṇṇavādī ahausi; aittha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisī’’ti|

sīlakkhandhau

450. ‘‘katamau  pana sau, bhau ānanda, ariyau sīlakkhandhau, yassa sau bhavaṃ gautamau vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisī’’ti?

‘‘idha, māṇava, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ  suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajaupathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā, nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati, ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, anumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, kāyakammavacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau kusalaina, parisuddhājīvau, sīlasampan‍nau, indriyaisu guttadvārau, satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, santuṭṭhau|

451. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, māṇava, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, māṇava, bhikkhu pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī  viharati; idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ| (yathā 194 yāva 210 anucchaidaisu aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

‘‘yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya  tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ – santikammaṃ paṇidhikammaṃ bhūtakammaṃ bhūrikammaṃ vassakammaṃ vaussakammaṃ vatthukammaṃ vatthuparikammaṃ ācamanaṃ nhāpanaṃ juhanaṃ vamanaṃ viraicanaṃ uddhaṃviraicanaṃ adhauviraicanaṃ sīsaviraicanaṃ kaṇṇatailaṃ naittatappanaṃ natthukammaṃ añ‍janaṃ pac‍cañ‍janaṃ sālākiyaṃ sal‍lakattiyaṃ dārakatikicchā mūlabhaisaj‍jānaṃ anuppadānaṃ ausadhīnaṃ paṭimaukkhau iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti, saiyyathidaṃ, santikammaṃ paṇidhikammaṃ…pai॰… ausadhīnaṃ paṭimaukkhau iti vā iti aivarūpāya tiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvā paṭiviratau hauti| idampissa hauti sīlasmiṃ|

452. ‘‘sa khau sau ayaṃ khau sau (ka॰), māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ sīlasaṃvaratau| saiyyathāpi, māṇava, rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau nihatapac‍cāmittau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ pac‍catthikatau| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ sīlasampan‍nau na kutauci bhayaṃ samanupassati, yadidaṃ sīlasaṃvaratau| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivaṃ khau, māṇava, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti|

453. ‘‘ayaṃ khau sau, māṇava, ariyau sīlakkhandhau yassa sau bhagavā vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisi| atthi caivaittha uttarikaraṇīya’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ, bhau ānanda! sau cāyaṃ, bhau ānanda, ariyau sīlakkhandhau paripuṇṇau, nau aparipuṇṇau| aivaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ cāhaṃ, bhau, ānanda, ariyaṃ sīlakkhandhaṃ  itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu na  samanupassāmi| aivaṃ paripuṇṇañ‍ca, bhau ānanda, ariyaṃ sīlakkhandhaṃ itau bahiddhā añ‍ñai samaṇabrāhmaṇā attani samanupassaiyyuṃ, tai tāvatakainaiva attamanā assu – ‘alamaittāvatā, katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīya’nti| atha ca pana bhavaṃ ānandau aivamāha – ‘atthi caivaittha uttarikaraṇīya’’’nti imassa anantaraṃ sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu ‘‘paṭhamabhāṇavāraṃ’’ti pāṭhau dissati|

samādhikkhandhau

454. ‘‘katamau  pana sau, bhau ānanda, ariyau samādhikkhandhau, yassa sau bhavaṃ gautamau vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisī’’ti?

‘‘kathañ‍ca, māṇava, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idha, māṇava, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina  saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivaṃ khau, māṇava, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

455. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, māṇava, bhikkhu satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, māṇava, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai  suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| aivaṃ khau, māṇava, bhikkhu satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

456. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, māṇava, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti? idha, māṇava, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi, māṇava, pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti, sapattabhārauva ḍaiti; aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| aivaṃ khau, māṇava, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti|

457. ‘‘sau  iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

458. ‘‘sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

459. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau iṇaṃ ādāya kammantai payaujaiyya| tassa tai kammantā samijjhaiyyuṃ| sau yāni ca paurāṇāni iṇamūlāni tāni ca byantiṃ karaiyya, siyā cassa uttariṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ  dārabharaṇāya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai iṇaṃ ādāya kammantai payaujaisiṃ| tassa mai tai kammantā samijjhiṃsu| sauhaṃ yāni ca paurāṇāni iṇamūlāni tāni ca byantiṃ akāsiṃ, atthi ca mai uttariṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ dārabharaṇāyā’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

460. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau ābādhikau assa dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau; bhattañ‍cassa nacchādaiyya, na cassa kāyai balamattā| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā ābādhā muc‍caiyya, bhattañ‍cassa chādaiyya, siyā cassa kāyai balamattā| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai ābādhikau ahausiṃ dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau, bhattañ‍ca mai nacchādaisi, na ca mai āsi kāyai balamattā| saumhi aitarahi tamhā ābādhā muttau bhattañ‍ca mai chādaiti, atthi ca mai kāyai balamattā’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

461. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau bandhanāgārai baddhau assa| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā bandhanāgārā muc‍caiyya sautthinā abbhayaina, na cassa kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai bandhanāgārai baddhau ahausiṃ| saumhi aitarahi tamhā bandhanāgārā muttau  sautthinā abbhayaina, natthi ca mai kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

462. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau dāsau assa anattādhīnau parādhīnau na yainakāmaṃgamau| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā dāsabyā muc‍caiyya, attādhīnau  aparādhīnau bhujissau yainakāmaṃgamau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai dāsau ahausiṃ anattādhīnau parādhīnau na yainakāmaṃgamau| saumhi aitarahi tamhā dāsabyā muttau attādhīnau aparādhīnau bhujissau yainakāmaṃgamau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

463. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau sadhanau sabhaugau kantāraddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya dubbhikkhaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ| sau aparaina samayaina taṃ kantāraṃ nittharaiyya, sautthinā gāmantaṃ anupāpuṇaiyya khaimaṃ appaṭibhayaṃ| tassa aivamassa  – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai sadhanau sabhaugau kantāraddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃ dubbhikkhaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ| saumhi aitarahi kantāraṃ nitthiṇṇau, sautthinā gāmantaṃ anuppattau khaimaṃ appaṭibhaya’nti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

464. ‘‘aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu yathā iṇaṃ yathā raugaṃ yathā bandhanāgāraṃ yathā dāsabyaṃ yathā kantāraddhānamaggaṃ, aivaṃ imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai appahīnai attani samanupassati|

465. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, yathā āṇaṇyaṃ yathā āraugyaṃ yathā bandhanāmaukkhaṃ yathā bhujissaṃ yathā khaimantabhūmiṃ| aivamaiva bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahīnai attani samanupassati|

466. ‘‘tassimai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahīnai attani samanupassatau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati|

467. ‘‘sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau  imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina  abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, dakkhau nhāpakau vā nhāpakantaivāsī vā kaṃsathālai nhānīyacuṇṇāni ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ sandaiyya| sāyaṃ nhānīyapiṇḍi snaihānugatā snaihaparaitā santarabāhirā phuṭā snaihaina, na ca paggharaṇī| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa  vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampissa hauti samādhismiṃ|

468. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, māṇava, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, udakarahadau gambhīrau ubbhidaudakau| tassa naivassa puratthimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na dakkhiṇāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na pacchimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na uttarāya disāya udakassa  āyamukhaṃ, daivau ca na kālaina kālaṃ sammā dhāraṃ anupavaicchaiyya| atha khau tamhāva udakarahadā sītā vāridhārā ubbhij‍jitvā tamaiva udakarahadaṃ sītaina vārinā abhisandaiyya parisandaiyya paripūraiyya parippharaiyya, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau udakarahadassa sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā… pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampissa hauti samādhismiṃ|

469. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, māṇava, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, tāni yāva caggā yāva ca mūlā sītaina vārinā abhisan‍nāni parisan‍nāni paripūrāni paripphuṭāni, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvataṃ uppalānaṃ vā padumānaṃ vā puṇḍarīkānaṃ vā  sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampissa hauti samādhismiṃ|

470. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, māṇava, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau audātaina vatthaina sasīsaṃ pārupitvā nisin‍nau assa, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa audātaina vatthaina apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti; nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| idampissa hauti samādhismiṃ|

471. ‘‘ayaṃ khau sau, māṇava, ariyau samādhikkhandhau yassa sau bhagavā vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisi| atthi caivaittha uttarikaraṇīya’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ, bhau  ānanda! sau cāyaṃ, bhau ānanda, ariyau samādhikkhandhau paripuṇṇau, nau aparipuṇṇau| aivaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ cāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, ariyaṃ samādhikkhandhaṃ itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu na samanupassāmi| aivaṃ paripuṇṇañ‍ca, bhau ānanda, ariyaṃ samādhikkhandhaṃ itau bahiddhā añ‍ñai samaṇabrāhmaṇā attani samanupassaiyyuṃ, tai tāvatakainaiva attamanā assu – ‘alamaittāvatā, katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīya’nti| atha ca pana bhavaṃ ānandau aivamāha – ‘atthi caivaittha uttarikaraṇīya’’’nti|

pañ‍ñākkhandhau

472. ‘‘katamau  pana sau, bhau ānanda, ariyau pañ‍ñākkhandhau, yassa bhau bhavaṃ gautamau vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisī’’ti?

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ayaṃ khau mai kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammau; idañ‍ca pana mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aittha sitaṃ aittha paṭibaddha’nti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau sabbākārasampan‍nau| tatrāssa suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau hatthai karitvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ‘ayaṃ khau maṇi vaiḷuriyau subhau jātimā aṭṭhaṃsau  suparikammakatau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau sabbākārasampan‍nau| tatridaṃ suttaṃ āvutaṃ nīlaṃ vā pītaṃ vā lauhitaṃ vā audātaṃ vā paṇḍusuttaṃ vā’ti| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘ayaṃ khau mai kāyau rūpī cātumahābhūtikau  mātāpaittikasambhavau audanakummāsūpacayau anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidana-viddhaṃsanadhammau| idañ‍ca pana mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aittha sitaṃ aittha paṭibaddha’nti| yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰…  ānaiñ‍jappattai ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… aittha paṭibaddhanti| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

473. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmānāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmināti rūpiṃ manaumayaṃ sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ ahīnindriyaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau muñ‍jamhā īsikaṃ pavāhaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ muñ‍jau ayaṃ īsikā; añ‍ñau muñ‍jau añ‍ñā īsikā; muñ‍jamhā tvaiva īsikā pavāḷhā’ti| saiyyathā vā pana, māṇava, purisau asiṃ kausiyā pavāhaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ asi, ayaṃ kausi; añ‍ñau asi, añ‍ñā kausi; kausiyā tvaiva asi pavāḷhau’ti| saiyyathā vā pana, māṇava, purisau ahiṃ karaṇḍā uddharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ ahi, ayaṃ karaṇḍau; añ‍ñau ahi, añ‍ñau karaṇḍau; karaṇḍā tvaiva ahi ubbhatau’ti| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmānāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰…| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

474. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai iddhividhāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti| āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati saiyyathāpi ākāsai| pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai| udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ| ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau| imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃ mahiddhikai aivaṃ mahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimaj‍jati| yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, dakkhau kumbhakārau vā kumbhakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatāya mattikāya yañ‍ñadaiva bhājanavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya, taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| saiyyathā vā pana, māṇava, dakkhau dantakārau vā dantakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatasmiṃ dantasmiṃ yañ‍ñadaiva dantavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya, taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| saiyyathā vā pana, māṇava, dakkhau suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā suparikammakatasmiṃ suvaṇṇasmiṃ  yañ‍ñadaiva suvaṇṇavikatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya, taṃ tadaiva karaiyya abhinipphādaiyya| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu  …pai॰… yampi māṇava bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai iddhividhāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti …pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

475. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai dibbāya sautadhātuyā cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca| saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau| sau suṇaiyya bhairisaddampi mudiṅgasaddampi saṅkhapaṇavadindimasaddampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘bhairisaddau itipi mudiṅgasaddau itipi saṅkhapaṇavadindimasaddau iti’pi itipīti (ka॰)| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰…| yampi māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai dibbāya sautadhātuyā cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāti  dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

476. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai caitaupariyañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti, ‘sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ samauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘saṅkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ saṅkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ mahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ anuttaraṃ citta’nti  pajānāti, ‘samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘vimuttaṃ  vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, ‘avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udakapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau sakaṇikaṃ vā sakaṇikanti  jānaiyya, akaṇikaṃ vā akaṇikanti jānaiyya| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai caitaupariyañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti, sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāti…pai॰… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāti| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

477. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau| sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau; sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, purisau sakamhā gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya; tamhāpi gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya; sau tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgacchaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau sakamhā gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ agacchiṃ, tatra aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ aivaṃ nisīdiṃ aivaṃ abhāsiṃ aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ| sau tamhāpi gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ gacchiṃ, tatrāpi aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ aivaṃ nisīdiṃ aivaṃ abhāsiṃ aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ| saumhi tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgatau’ti| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ  samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

478. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā  vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, majjhaisiṅghāṭakai pāsādau, tattha cakkhumā purisau ṭhitau passaiyya manussai gaihaṃ pavisantaipi nikkhamantaipi rathikāyapi vīthiṃ sañ‍carantai majjhaisiṅghāṭakai nisin‍naipi| tassa aivamassa – ‘aitai manussā gaihaṃ pavisanti, aitai nikkhamanti, aitai rathikāya vīthiṃ sañ‍caranti, aitai majjhaisiṅghāṭakai nisin‍nā’ti| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

479. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya  cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; imai āsavāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavasamudayauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, māṇava, pabbatasaṅkhaipai udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau| tattha cakkhumā purisau tīrai ṭhitau passaiyya sippikasambukampi sakkharakathalampi macchagumbampi carantampi tiṭṭhantampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau| tatrimai sippikasambukāpi sakkharakathalāpi macchagumbāpi carantipi tiṭṭhantipī’ti| aivamaiva khau, māṇava, bhikkhu…pai॰… yampi, māṇava, bhikkhu aivaṃ samāhitai cittai…pai॰… ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti, ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| idampissa hauti pañ‍ñāya|

480. ‘‘ayaṃ khau, sau māṇava, ariyau pañ‍ñākkhandhau yassa sau bhagavā vaṇṇavādī ahausi, yattha ca imaṃ janataṃ samādapaisi nivaisaisi patiṭṭhāpaisi| natthi caivaittha uttarikaraṇīya’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau ānanda, abbhutaṃ, bhau ānanda! sau cāyaṃ, bhau ānanda, ariyau pañ‍ñākkhandhau paripuṇṇau, nau aparipuṇṇau| aivaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ cāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, ariyaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu na samanupassāmi| natthi  caivaittha na samanupassāmi…pai॰… natthi nau kiñ‍ci (syā॰ ka॰) uttarikaraṇīyaṃ uttariṃ karaṇīyanti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) uttarikaraṇīyanti (ka॰)| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau ānanda, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau ānanda! saiyyathāpi, bhau ānanda, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti| aivamaivaṃ bhautā ānandaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhau ānanda, taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ ānandau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

subhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|



11. kaivaṭṭasuttaṃ

kaivaṭṭagahapatiputtavatthu

481. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nāḷandāyaṃ viharati pāvārikambavanai| atha khau kaivaṭṭau gahapatiputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaivaṭṭau gahapatiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, nāḷandā iddhā caiva phītā ca bahujanā ākiṇṇamanussā bhagavati abhippasan‍nā| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ samādisatu, yau uttarimanussadhammā, iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati; aivāyaṃ nāḷandā bhiyyausau mattāya bhagavati abhippasīdissatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā kaivaṭṭaṃ gahapatiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, gihīnaṃ audātavasanānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karauthā’’ti|

482. dutiyampi khau kaivaṭṭau gahapatiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ dhaṃsaimi; api ca, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ayaṃ, bhantai, nāḷandā iddhā caiva phītā ca bahujanā ākiṇṇamanussā bhagavati abhippasan‍nā| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ samādisatu, yau uttarimanussadhammā  iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati; aivāyaṃ nāḷandā bhiyyausau mattāya bhagavati abhippasīdissatī’’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā kaivaṭṭaṃ gahapatiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi – aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, gihīnaṃ audātavasanānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karauthā’’’ti|

tatiyampi khau kaivaṭṭau gahapatiputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ dhaṃsaimi; api ca, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ayaṃ, bhantai, nāḷandā iddhā caiva phītā ca bahujanā ākiṇṇamanussā  bhagavati abhippasan‍nā| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ samādisatu, yau uttarimanussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ karissati| aivāyaṃ nāḷandā bhiyyausau mattāya bhagavati abhippasīdissatī’ti|

iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ

483. ‘‘tīṇi  khau imāni, kaivaṭṭa, pāṭihāriyāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni tīṇi? iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaṃ, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ|

484. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, kaivaṭṭa, iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ? idha, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti| aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃ mahiddhikai aivaṃ mahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasati parimaj‍jati; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau saddhau pasan‍nau passati taṃ bhikkhuṃ anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauntaṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauntaṃ, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauntaṃ; āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānaṃ gacchantaṃ saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauntaṃ saiyyathāpi  udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchantaṃ saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamantaṃ saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃ mahiddhikai aivaṃ mahānubhāvai pāṇinā parāmasantaṃ parimaj‍jantaṃ yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaintaṃ|

‘‘tamainaṃ sau saddhau pasan‍nau añ‍ñatarassa assaddhassa appasan‍nassa āraucaiti – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau, samaṇassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā| amāhaṃ bhikkhuṃ addasaṃ anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauntaṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauntaṃ, bahudhāpi  hutvā aikau hauntaṃ…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattainta’nti|

‘‘tamainaṃ  sau assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthi khau, bhau, gandhārī nāma vij‍jā| tāya sau bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaitī’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kaivaṭṭa, api nu sau assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘vadaiyya, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, iddhipāṭihāriyai ādīnavaṃ sampassamānau iddhipāṭihāriyaina aṭṭīyāmi harāyāmi jigucchāmi’’|

ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaṃ

485. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, kaivaṭṭa, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaṃ? idha, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cittampi ādisati, caitasikampi ādisati, vitak‍kitampi ādisati, vicāritampi ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti|

‘‘tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau saddhau pasan‍nau passati taṃ bhikkhuṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cittampi ādisantaṃ, caitasikampi ādisantaṃ, vitak‍kitampi ādisantaṃ, vicāritampi ādisantaṃ – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| tamainaṃ sau saddhau pasan‍nau añ‍ñatarassa assaddhassa appasan‍nassa āraucaiti – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ  vata, bhau, samaṇassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā| amāhaṃ bhikkhuṃ addasaṃ parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cittampi ādisantaṃ, caitasikampi ādisantaṃ, vitak‍kitampi ādisantaṃ, vicāritampi ādisantaṃ – ‘‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’’’nti|

‘‘tamainaṃ sau  assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘atthi khau, bhau, maṇikā nāma vij‍jā; tāya sau bhikkhu parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cittampi ādisati, caitasikampi ādisati, vitak‍kitampi ādisati, vicāritampi ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kaivaṭṭa, api nu sau assaddhau appasan‍nau taṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘vadaiyya, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyai ādīnavaṃ sampassamānau ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaina aṭṭīyāmi harāyāmi jigucchāmi’’|

anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ

486. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, kaivaṭṭa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ? idha, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu aivamanusāsati – ‘aivaṃ vitak‍kaitha, mā aivaṃ vitak‍kayittha, aivaṃ manasikarautha, mā  aivaṃ manasākattha, idaṃ pajahatha, idaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, kaivaṭṭa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, idha tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  …pai॰… (yathā 190-212 anucchaidaisu aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)| aivaṃ khau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampi vuc‍cati, kaivaṭṭa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampi vuc‍cati, kaivaṭṭa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ…pai॰… ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati  abhinin‍nāmaiti…pai॰… idampi vuc‍cati, kaivaṭṭa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti…pai॰… idampi vuc‍cati, kaivaṭṭa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ|

‘‘imāni khau, kaivaṭṭa, tīṇi pāṭihāriyāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā pavaiditāni’’|

bhūtaniraudhaisakabhikkhuvatthu

487. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, imasmiñ‍ñaiva bhikkhusaṅghai añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘kattha nu khau imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti?

488. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu tathārūpaṃ samādhiṃ samāpaj‍ji, yathāsamāhitai cittai daivayāniyau maggau pāturahausi| atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina cātumahārājikā daivā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā cātumahārājikai daivai aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, cātumahārājikā daivā taṃ bhikkhuṃ  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mayampi khau, bhikkhu, na jānāma, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātūti vāyaudhātu| atthi khau (pī॰ aivamuparipi)| atthi khau vāyaudhātu| atthi khau (pī॰ aivamuparipi), bhikkhu, cattārau mahārājānau amhaihi abhik‍kantatarā  ca paṇītatarā ca| tai khau aitaṃ jānaiyyuṃ, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

489. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina cattārau mahārājānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā cattārau mahārājai aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā  nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu  āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, cattārau mahārājānau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mayampi khau, bhikkhu, na jānāma, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātūti| atthi khau, bhikkhu, tāvatiṃsā nāma daivā amhaihi abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā ca| tai khau aitaṃ jānaiyyuṃ, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

490. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina tāvatiṃsā daivā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tāvatiṃsai daivai aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, tāvatiṃsā daivā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mayampi khau, bhikkhu, na jānāma, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātūti| atthi khau, bhikkhu, sak‍kau nāma daivānamindau amhaihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| sau khau aitaṃ jānaiyya, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

491. ‘‘atha  khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina sak‍kau daivānamindau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, sak‍kau daivānamindau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na  jānāmi, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātūti| atthi khau, bhikkhu, yāmā nāma daivā…pai॰… suyāmau nāma daivaputtau…  tusitā nāma daivā… santussitau nāma daivaputtau… nimmānaratī  nāma daivā … sunimmitau nāma daivaputtau… paranimmitavasavattī nāma daivā… vasavattī nāma daivaputtau amhaihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| sau khau aitaṃ jānaiyya, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

492. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina vasavattī daivaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vasavattiṃ daivaputtaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, vasavattī daivaputtau taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na jānāmi yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātūti| atthi khau, bhikkhu, brahmakāyikā nāma daivā amhaihi abhik‍kantatarā ca paṇītatarā ca| tai khau aitaṃ jānaiyyuṃ, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

493. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu tathārūpaṃ samādhiṃ samāpaj‍ji, yathāsamāhitai cittai brahmayāniyau maggau pāturahausi| atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina brahmakāyikā daivā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā brahmakāyikai daivai aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, brahmakāyikā daivā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mayampi khau, bhikkhu, na  jānāma, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātūti| atthi khau, bhikkhu, brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau  kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ amhaihi abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| sau khau aitaṃ jānaiyya, yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’’ti|

‘‘‘kahaṃ panāvusau, aitarahi sau mahābrahmā’ti? ‘mayampi khau, bhikkhu, na jānāma, yattha vā brahmā yaina vā brahmā yahiṃ vā brahmā; api ca, bhikkhu, yathā nimittā dissanti, ālaukau sañ‍jāyati, aubhāsau pātubhavati, brahmā pātubhavissati, brahmunau haitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ pātubhāvāya, yadidaṃ ālaukau sañ‍jāyati, aubhāsau pātubhavatī’ti| atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, mahābrahmā nacirassaiva pāturahausi|

494. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu yaina sau mahābrahmā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ mahābrahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, kaivaṭṭa, sau mahābrahmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘ahamasmi, bhikkhu, brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī  issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyāna’nti|

‘‘dutiyampi  khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu taṃ mahābrahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘na khauhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ pucchāmi – ‘‘tvamasi brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyāna’’nti| aivañ‍ca khau ahaṃ taṃ, āvusau, pucchāmi – ‘‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’’’ti?

‘‘dutiyampi khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, mahābrahmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘ahamasmi, bhikkhu, brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyāna’nti| tatiyampi khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu taṃ mahābrahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘na khauhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ pucchāmi – ‘‘tvamasi brahmā mahābrahmā abhibhū  anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā vasī pitā bhūtabhabyāna’’nti| aivañ‍ca khau ahaṃ taṃ, āvusau, pucchāmi – ‘‘kattha nu khau, āvusau, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’’’ti?

495. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, mahābrahmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ apanaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca  – ‘imai khau maṃ, bhikkhu, brahmakāyikā daivā aivaṃ jānanti, ‘‘natthi kiñ‍ci brahmunau añ‍ñātaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci brahmunau adiṭṭhaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci brahmunau aviditaṃ, natthi kiñ‍ci brahmunau asacchikata’’nti| tasmāhaṃ taisaṃ sammukhā na byākāsiṃ| ahampi khau, bhikkhu, na jānāmi yatthimai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu  vāyaudhātūti| tasmātiha, bhikkhu, tuyhaivaitaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ, tuyhaivaitaṃ aparaddhaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ atidhāvitvā bahiddhā pariyaiṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jasi imassa pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāya| gaccha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, tamaiva bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā imaṃ pañhaṃ puccha, yathā ca tai bhagavā byākarauti, tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāsī’ti|

496. ‘‘atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva brahmalaukai antarahitau mama puratau pāturahausi| atha khau sau, kaivaṭṭa, bhikkhu maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi, aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau, kaivaṭṭa, sau bhikkhu maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kattha nu khau, bhantai, imai cattārau  mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti?

tīradassisakuṇupamā

497. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, kaivaṭṭa, taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhu, sāmuddikā vāṇijā tīradassiṃ sakuṇaṃ gahaitvā nāvāya samuddaṃ ajjhaugāhanti| tai atīradakkhiniyā nāvāya tīradassiṃ sakuṇaṃ muñ‍canti| sau gacchataiva puratthimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ, gacchati pacchimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uttaraṃ disaṃ, gacchati uddhaṃ disaṃ, gacchati anudisaṃ| sacai sau samantā tīraṃ  passati, tathāgatakauva tathāpak‍kantauva (syā॰) hauti| sacai pana sau samantā tīraṃ na passati, tamaiva nāvaṃ pac‍cāgacchati| aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, yatau yāva brahmalaukā  pariyaisamānau imassa pañhassa  vaiyyākaraṇaṃ nājjhagā, atha mamañ‍ñaiva santikai pac‍cāgatau| na khau aisau, bhikkhu, pañhau aivaṃ pucchitabbau – ‘kattha nu khau, bhantai, imai cattārau mahābhūtā aparisaisā nirujjhanti, saiyyathidaṃ – pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti?

498. ‘‘aivañ‍ca khau aisau, bhikkhu, pañhau pucchitabbau –

‘kattha āpau ca pathavī, taijau vāyau na gādhati|

kattha dīghañ‍ca rassañ‍ca, aṇuṃ thūlaṃ subhāsubhaṃ|

kattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhatī’ti||

499. ‘‘tatra vaiyyākaraṇaṃ bhavati –

‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anidassanaṃ, anantaṃ sabbataupabhaṃ|

aittha āpau ca pathavī, taijau vāyau na gādhati||

aittha dīghañ‍ca rassañ‍ca, aṇuṃ thūlaṃ subhāsubhaṃ|

aittha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, aitthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’ti||

500. idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau kaivaṭṭau gahapatiputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

kaivaṭṭasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aikādasamaṃ|



12. lauhic‍casuttaṃ

lauhic‍cabrāhmaṇavatthu

501. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi yaina sālavatikā tadavasari| taina khau pana samayaina lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau sālavatikaṃ ajjhāvasati sattussadaṃ satiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ sadhañ‍ñaṃ rājabhauggaṃ rañ‍ñā pasainadinā kausalaina din‍naṃ rājadāyaṃ, brahmadaiyyaṃ|

502. taina khau pana samayaina lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘idha samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigacchaiyya, kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantvā na parassa āraucaiyya, kiñhi parau parassa karissati| saiyyathāpi nāma purāṇaṃ bandhanaṃ chinditvā añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ bandhanaṃ karaiyya, aivaṃsampadamidaṃ pāpakaṃ laubhadhammaṃ vadāmi, kiñhi parau parassa karissatī’’ti|

503. assausi khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sālavatikaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ  sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

504. atha  khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau rausikaṃ bhaisikaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) nhāpitaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, samma rausikai, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – lauhic‍cau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘‘adhivāsaitu kira bhavaṃ gautamau lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti|

505. ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti aivaṃ bhantaiti (sī॰ pī॰) khau rausikā nhāpitau lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rausikā nhāpitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘lauhic‍cau, bhantai, brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, bhagavā lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

506. atha khau rausikā nhāpitau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā lauhic‍caṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘avaucumhā khau mayaṃ bhautau mayaṃ bhantai tava (sī॰ pī॰) vacanaina taṃ bhagavantaṃ – ‘lauhic‍cau, bhantai, brāhmaṇau  bhagavantaṃ appābādhaṃ  appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, bhagavā lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’ti| adhivutthañ‍ca pana taina bhagavatā’’ti|

507. atha khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā rausikaṃ nhāpitaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, samma rausikai, yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā samaṇassa gautamassa kālaṃ āraucaihi – kālau bhau, gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau rausikā nhāpitau lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau rausikā nhāpitau bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

508. atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina  sālavatikā tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina rausikā nhāpitau bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhau hauti| atha khau rausikā nhāpitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘lauhic‍cassa, bhantai, brāhmaṇassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘idha samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigacchaiyya, kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantvā na parassa āraucaiyya – kiñhi parau parassa karissati| saiyyathāpi nāma purāṇaṃ bandhanaṃ chinditvā añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ bandhanaṃ karaiyya, aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ  pāpakaṃ laubhadhammaṃ vadāmi – kiñhi parau parassa karissatī’ti| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā lauhic‍caṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaitū’’ti| ‘‘appaiva nāma siyā rausikai, appaiva nāma siyā rausikai’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā yaina lauhic‍cassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi|

lauhic‍cabrāhmaṇānuyaugau

509. atha khau lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau lauhic‍caṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, lauhic‍ca, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘idha samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigacchaiyya, kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantvā na parassa āraucaiyya – kiñhi parau parassa karissati| saiyyathāpi nāma purāṇaṃ bandhanaṃ chinditvā añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ bandhanaṃ karaiyya, aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ pāpakaṃ laubhadhammaṃ vadāmi, kiñhi parau parassa karissatī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi lauhic‍ca nanu tvaṃ sālavatikaṃ ajjhāvasasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘yau nu khau, lauhic‍ca, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘lauhic‍cau brāhmaṇau sālavatikaṃ ajjhāvasati| yā sālavatikāya samudayasañ‍jāti lauhic‍cauva taṃ brāhmaṇau  aikakau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, na añ‍ñaisaṃ dadaiyyā’ti| aivaṃ vādī sau yai taṃ upajīvanti, taisaṃ antarāyakarau  vā hauti, nau vā’’ti?

‘‘antarāyakarau, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘antarāyakarau samānau hitānukampī vā taisaṃ hauti ahitānukampī vā’’ti? ‘‘ahitānukampī, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘ahitānukampissa maittaṃ vā taisu cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sapattakaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘sapattakaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘sapattakai cittai pac‍cupaṭṭhitai  micchādiṭṭhi vā hauti sammādiṭṭhi vā’’ti? ‘‘micchādiṭṭhi, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘micchādiṭṭhissa khau ahaṃ, lauhic‍ca, dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā’’|

510. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, lauhic‍ca, nanu rājā pasainadi kausalau kāsikausalaṃ ajjhāvasatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘yau nu khau, lauhic‍ca, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘rājā pasainadi kausalau kāsikausalaṃ ajjhāvasati; yā kāsikausalai samudayasañ‍jāti, rājāva taṃ pasainadi kausalau aikakau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, na añ‍ñaisaṃ dadaiyyā’ti| aivaṃ vādī sau yai rājānaṃ pasainadiṃ kausalaṃ upajīvanti tumhai caiva añ‍ñai ca, taisaṃ antarāyakarau vā hauti, nau vā’’ti?

‘‘antarāyakarau, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘antarāyakarau samānau hitānukampī vā taisaṃ hauti ahitānukampī vā’’ti? ‘‘ahitānukampī, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘ahitānukampissa maittaṃ vā taisu cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sapattakaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘sapattakaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘sapattakai cittai pac‍cupaṭṭhitai micchādiṭṭhi vā hauti sammādiṭṭhi vā’’ti? ‘‘micchādiṭṭhi, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘micchādiṭṭhissa  khau ahaṃ, lauhic‍ca, dvin‍naṃ gatīnaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi – nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā’’|
